# ===== 1 招聘與求職 =====
applicant  Each applicant must submit a resume and two references by Friday.  n. 應徵者  每位應徵者都必須在週五前繳交履歷表和兩份推薦函。
candidate  The hiring committee will interview three candidates for the sales role next week.  n. 候選人；應徵者  招聘委員會下週將面試三位業務職缺的候選人。
resume  Please attach your resume to the online application form.  n. 履歷表  請將您的履歷表附在線上申請表上。
resume  Normal service will resume on Monday morning.  v. 恢復；重新開始  正常服務將於週一上午恢復。
interview  The interview will take place at our Taipei office at ten o'clock.  n. 面試；v. 面談  面試將於十點在我們台北辦公室舉行。
position  We are pleased to announce that the accounting position has been filled.  n. 職位  我們很高興宣布會計職位已經補實。
position  The company is in a strong financial position.  n. 處境；地位  這家公司的財務狀況穩健。
vacancy  There is a vacancy in the customer service department.  n. 職缺  客服部門有一個職缺。
recruit  We plan to recruit ten engineers before the end of the year.  v. 招募  我們計畫在年底前招募十名工程師。
hire  The company will hire two project managers this quarter.  v. 僱用  公司本季將僱用兩名專案經理。
apply  Interested candidates should apply online before the deadline.  v. 申請；應徵  有興趣的人選應在截止日前上網應徵。
apply  The new travel policy will apply to all employees.  v. 適用於  新的差旅政策將適用於所有員工。
application  Your application has been received and is under review.  n. 申請書；申請  您的申請已收到，目前正在審核中。
qualification  A master's degree is the minimum qualification for this job.  n. 資格；資歷  碩士學位是這份工作的最低資格。
requirement  Fluency in Japanese is a requirement for this position.  n. 要求；必要條件  日語流利是這個職位的必要條件。
experience  Applicants need at least three years of experience in marketing.  n. 經驗；經歷  應徵者需要至少三年的行銷經驗。
employer  The employer offers a generous training program to new hires.  n. 雇主  雇主為新進員工提供優渥的訓練計畫。
opening  We have an opening for a graphic designer in our creative team.  n. 職缺  我們的創意團隊有一個平面設計師的職缺。
reference  Two professional references are required with every application.  n. 推薦人；推薦函  每份申請都須附上兩位專業推薦人。
reference  Please keep this manual for future reference.  n. 參考  請保留這本手冊以供日後參考。
skill  Strong communication skills are essential for this role.  n. 技能  優秀的溝通技巧對這個職務而言不可或缺。
qualified  Only qualified candidates will be contacted for an interview.  adj. 合格的  只有符合資格的人選會被通知面試。
background  She has a strong background in finance and data analysis.  n. 背景；經歷  她在財務和資料分析方面有深厚的背景。
referral  Employees who submit a successful referral receive a cash reward.  n. 引薦；推薦  成功引薦人選的員工可獲得現金獎勵。
shortlist  The committee will shortlist five applicants for the final round.  v. 列入決選名單；n. 決選名單  委員會將選出五位應徵者進入最後一輪。
screen  Our HR team will screen all resumes before the first interview.  v. 篩選  人資團隊會在第一次面試前篩選所有履歷。
screen  The presenter's screen was too small for the audience to read.  n. 螢幕  簡報者的螢幕太小，觀眾看不清楚。
assess  Interviewers will assess each candidate on communication and teamwork.  v. 評估  面試官將就溝通與團隊合作評估每位候選人。
assessment  Candidates must complete an online assessment before the interview.  n. 評估；測驗  候選人必須在面試前完成線上測驗。
recruiter  The recruiter will call you to schedule a phone interview.  n. 招募人員  招募人員會打電話給您安排電話面試。
recruitment  Our recruitment campaign will start in January.  n. 招募  我們的招募活動將於一月展開。
eligible  Only full-time staff are eligible to apply for this position.  adj. 有資格的  只有全職員工有資格應徵這個職位。
prospective  We invited several prospective employees to tour the factory.  adj. 潛在的；未來的  我們邀請了幾位未來的員工參觀工廠。
fluent  The ideal applicant is fluent in both English and Korean.  adj. 流利的  理想的應徵者英語和韓語都很流利。
proficient  Applicants should be proficient in spreadsheet software.  adj. 熟練的  應徵者應熟練使用試算表軟體。
credential  Please bring original copies of your credentials to the interview.  n. 資格證明；證書  請攜帶您的資格證明正本參加面試。
certification  A project management certification is preferred but not required.  n. 證照  持有專案管理證照者優先，但非必要條件。
degree  A bachelor's degree in business is required for this role.  n. 學位  這個職務要求具備商學學士學位。
degree  The project requires a high degree of accuracy.  n. 程度  這個專案要求高度的準確性。
graduate  The firm hires recent graduates for its management training program.  n. 畢業生；v. 畢業  這家公司為其管理培訓計畫僱用應屆畢業生。
internship  She completed a summer internship at a law firm.  n. 實習  她在一家律師事務所完成了暑期實習。
intern  Our marketing intern will help organize the product launch.  n. 實習生  我們的行銷實習生將協助籌備產品發表。
trainee  Each trainee will be paired with an experienced mentor.  n. 受訓人員；實習員  每位受訓人員都會配對一位經驗豐富的導師。
apprentice  The apprentice learned welding from a senior technician.  n. 學徒  這名學徒跟著資深技師學習焊接。
probation  New employees are on probation for the first three months.  n. 試用期  新進員工前三個月為試用期。
onboard  The HR department will onboard the new hires on Monday.  v. 帶領新進員工熟悉環境  人資部門將於週一帶領新進員工熟悉環境。
orientation  New staff must attend orientation on their first day.  n. 新進員工訓練；說明會  新進人員必須在到職第一天參加新人訓練。
offer  We are pleased to offer you the position of project coordinator.  v. 提供；n. 錄取通知  我們很高興提供您專案協調員的職位。
accept  He was delighted to accept the job offer from the bank.  v. 接受  他很高興接受了那家銀行的工作邀約。
decline  She had to decline the offer because of the long commute.  v. 婉拒  由於通勤時間太長，她只好婉拒這份工作邀約。
reject  Unfortunately, we must reject your application at this time.  v. 拒絕；不錄用  很遺憾，我們目前無法錄用您的申請。
select  The panel will select the best candidate by Friday.  v. 挑選  評審小組將於週五前選出最佳人選。
selection  The selection process includes two interviews and a written test.  n. 甄選；挑選  甄選流程包括兩次面試和一次筆試。
appoint  The board voted to appoint Linda Chen as the new director.  v. 任命  董事會投票決議任命陳琳達為新任主任。
appointment  Please confirm your interview appointment by email.  n. 約定時間；任命  請以電子郵件確認您的面試時間。
advertise  We will advertise the vacancy on several job websites.  v. 刊登廣告；宣傳  我們將在數個求職網站上刊登這個職缺。
advertisement  I saw your advertisement for a receptionist in the local newspaper.  n. 廣告  我在地方報紙上看到你們徵求櫃檯接待人員的廣告。
listing  The job listing includes the salary range and benefits.  n. 刊登的資訊；清單  這則職缺資訊包含薪資範圍和福利。
submit  Please submit your application through our website.  v. 提交  請透過我們的網站提交您的申請。
deadline  The deadline for applications is the end of this month.  n. 截止期限  申請的截止日期是本月底。
enclose  I have enclosed my resume and a letter of reference.  v. 隨函附上  我隨函附上我的履歷表和一封推薦信。
attach  Please attach a copy of your diploma to the form.  v. 附上  請在表格上附上您的畢業證書影本。
portfolio  Designers should bring a portfolio of their recent work.  n. 作品集  設計師應攜帶近期作品集。
expertise  The job requires technical expertise in network security.  n. 專業知識  這份工作需要網路安全方面的專業技術知識。
competent  We need a competent accountant who can work independently.  adj. 稱職的；有能力的  我們需要一位能獨立作業的稱職會計師。
motivated  We are looking for a motivated sales representative.  adj. 積極的；有幹勁的  我們正在尋找一位有幹勁的業務代表。
versatile  A versatile employee can handle several different tasks.  adj. 多才多藝的；多用途的  多才多藝的員工能處理多項不同的工作。
bilingual  Bilingual receptionists are in high demand at international hotels.  adj. 雙語的  國際飯店對雙語接待人員的需求很大。
full-time  The company is hiring a full-time assistant for its Osaka office.  adj. 全職的  公司正為大阪辦公室招聘一名全職助理。
part-time  Students may apply for part-time work during the summer.  adj. 兼職的  學生暑假期間可以申請兼職工作。
temporary  This is a temporary position that lasts for six months.  adj. 臨時的  這是一個為期六個月的臨時職位。
permanent  After a year, the temporary worker became a permanent employee.  adj. 永久的；正式的  一年後，這名臨時員工成為正式員工。
freelance  Many designers prefer to work as freelance consultants.  adj. 自由接案的  許多設計師偏好擔任自由接案的顧問。
contract  The contract for this role is renewable after one year.  n. 合約  這個職務的合約在一年後可以續約。
contract  The firm agreed to contract the cleaning work out to an outside company.  v. 發包；承包  公司同意將清潔工作外包給外部公司。
seasonal  Seasonal workers are needed during the holiday shopping period.  adj. 季節性的  假期購物旺季需要季節性員工。
headhunter  A headhunter contacted him about a senior position in Singapore.  n. 獵才者；獵人頭公司  一位獵才顧問與他聯絡，談到新加坡的一個高階職位。
overqualified  The manager said I was overqualified for the entry-level role.  adj. 資格過高的  經理說我對這個入門級職務而言資歷過高。
preferred  Previous experience in the retail industry is preferred.  adj. 優先考慮的  具備零售業相關經驗者優先。
desirable  Knowledge of French is desirable but not essential.  adj. 理想的；有則更佳的  會法語更好，但不是必要條件。
essential  Teamwork is essential in our fast-paced office.  adj. 必要的  在我們步調快速的辦公室裡，團隊合作不可或缺。
relevant  Please list only relevant work experience on your resume.  adj. 相關的  請在履歷表上只列出相關的工作經歷。
suitable  We could not find a suitable candidate for the position.  adj. 合適的  我們找不到適合這個職位的人選。
ideal  The ideal candidate has strong leadership skills.  adj. 理想的  理想的人選具備優秀的領導能力。
outstanding  She has an outstanding record in international sales.  adj. 傑出的  她在國際銷售方面有傑出的紀錄。
commitment  We value commitment and honesty in every new employee.  n. 承諾；投入  我們重視每位新進員工的投入與誠實。
enthusiasm  He showed great enthusiasm during the interview.  n. 熱忱  他在面試中展現出高度的熱忱。
aptitude  The test measures aptitude for technical problem solving.  n. 才能；性向  這項測驗衡量解決技術問題的能力傾向。
potential  The manager saw great potential in the young engineer.  n. 潛力；adj. 潛在的  經理在這位年輕工程師身上看到很大的潛力。
career  This role offers excellent opportunities for career growth.  n. 職涯  這個職務提供絕佳的職涯成長機會。
profession  Teaching is a rewarding profession for many people.  n. 專業；職業  對許多人來說，教書是一份有成就感的職業。
employment  The agency helps graduates find employment in the tech sector.  n. 就業；僱用  這家機構協助畢業生在科技業找到工作。
unemployed  The program provides training for unemployed workers.  adj. 失業的  這項計畫為失業勞工提供訓練。
workforce  Our workforce has grown by twenty percent this year.  n. 勞動力；全體員工  我們的員工人數今年成長了百分之二十。
talent  The company is eager to attract top talent from universities.  n. 人才；才能  公司渴望從各大學吸引頂尖人才。
criteria  All candidates are judged on the same criteria.  n. 標準（criterion 的複數）  所有候選人都依相同的標準評比。
vet  The agency will vet every applicant before sending resumes to us.  v. 審查  仲介公司會先審查每位應徵者，再把履歷寄給我們。
verify  We will verify your references before making an offer.  v. 查證；核實  在發出錄取通知前，我們會先查證您的推薦人。
inquire  Please inquire at the front desk about current job openings.  v. 詢問  請向櫃檯詢問目前的職缺。
candidacy  Your candidacy will be reviewed by the selection committee.  n. 候選資格  您的候選資格將由甄選委員會審查。
entry-level  This entry-level job requires no previous experience.  adj. 入門級的  這份入門級工作不需要任何經驗。
seasoned  We prefer a seasoned manager with global experience.  adj. 經驗豐富的  我們偏好具備國際經驗的資深經理。
dedicated  The firm is seeking a dedicated team player.  adj. 專注的；敬業的  公司正在尋找一位敬業的團隊成員。
adaptable  Successful candidates must be adaptable to changing priorities.  adj. 適應力強的  錄取者必須能適應不斷變動的優先順序。
reliable  We need a reliable assistant who arrives on time.  adj. 可靠的  我們需要一位準時到班的可靠助理。
ambitious  The ambitious intern asked for more responsibility.  adj. 有抱負的  這位有抱負的實習生要求承擔更多責任。
capable  She is a capable leader who motivates her team.  adj. 有能力的  她是一位能激勵團隊的能幹領導者。
# ===== 2 人事組織與職稱 =====
employee  Every employee must wear an ID badge inside the building.  n. 員工  每位員工在大樓內都必須佩戴識別證。
department  The marketing department will move to the third floor.  n. 部門  行銷部門將搬到三樓。
manager  The store manager will review the schedule with the staff.  n. 經理  店經理將與員工一起檢視班表。
supervisor  Please ask your supervisor to approve the request.  n. 主管；督導  請您的主管核准這項申請。
director  The director of finance will present the annual report.  n. 主任；董事  財務主任將提出年度報告。
executive  A senior executive will attend the opening ceremony.  n. 高階主管  一位高階主管將出席開幕典禮。
staff  All staff are invited to the year-end party.  n. 全體員工  全體員工都受邀參加尾牙。
staff  The hotel will staff the front desk around the clock.  v. 配置人員  飯店將全天候在櫃檯配置人員。
personnel  Only authorized personnel may enter the laboratory.  n. 人員；人事部門  只有獲授權的人員才能進入實驗室。
colleague  I asked a colleague to cover my shift on Friday.  n. 同事  我請一位同事代我週五的班。
subordinate  A good manager listens to the ideas of subordinates.  n. 部屬  好的主管會傾聽部屬的想法。
superior  Report any safety concerns to your immediate superior.  n. 上司；adj. 較優越的  任何安全疑慮都請向您的直屬上司回報。
division  The sales division exceeded its target for the third quarter.  n. 事業部；分部  業務事業部第三季超越了目標。
branch  She was promoted to manager of the Kaohsiung branch.  n. 分公司；分行  她被升為高雄分公司的經理。
headquarters  The company's headquarters are located in downtown Chicago.  n. 總部  這家公司的總部位於芝加哥市中心。
chairman  The chairman will deliver the opening speech at the shareholder meeting.  n. 董事長  董事長將在股東會上發表開場致詞。
president  The president announced a new hiring plan to all employees.  n. 總裁；總經理  總經理向全體員工宣布了新的招聘計畫。
vice  The vice chairman will lead the meeting in the chairman's absence.  adj. 副的  董事長缺席時，副董事長將主持會議。
assistant  The administrative assistant will book the conference room.  n. 助理  行政助理將預訂會議室。
associate  She joined the firm as a junior associate last year.  n. 專員；合夥人  她去年以初階專員的身分加入這家公司。
coordinator  The event coordinator will contact all the speakers.  n. 協調人；專員  活動協調人將聯絡所有講者。
administrator  The system administrator reset my password this morning.  n. 管理員；行政人員  系統管理員今天早上重設了我的密碼。
representative  A sales representative will visit your office on Tuesday.  n. 代表；業務代表  一位業務代表將於週二拜訪您的辦公室。
secretary  The secretary will take notes during the meeting.  n. 秘書  秘書將在會議期間做紀錄。
receptionist  Please leave your package with the receptionist.  n. 接待人員  請把您的包裹交給接待人員。
clerk  The filing clerk sorted the invoices by date.  n. 辦事員；店員  檔案管理員依日期整理了發票。
technician  A technician will repair the printer this afternoon.  n. 技術人員  技術人員今天下午會修理印表機。
engineer  The software engineer fixed the bug within an hour.  n. 工程師  軟體工程師在一小時內修好了這個錯誤。
consultant  We hired a consultant to improve our supply chain.  n. 顧問  我們聘請了一位顧問來改善供應鏈。
specialist  Our IT specialist can help you install the program.  n. 專員；專家  我們的資訊專員可以協助您安裝這個程式。
analyst  The financial analyst presented the quarterly forecast.  n. 分析師  財務分析師報告了季度預測。
officer  The compliance officer reviewed the new regulations.  n. 主管；官員  法遵主管審閱了新的法規。
leader  The team leader assigned tasks to each member.  n. 領導者；組長  組長將工作分派給每位成員。
leadership  Strong leadership is needed during the merger.  n. 領導力；領導階層  合併期間需要強而有力的領導。
head  The head of human resources will interview all finalists.  n. 主管；首長  人資主管將面試所有入圍者。
head  Rachel Kim will head the new research department.  v. 領導；主管  金瑞秋將領導新的研究部門。
chief  The chief financial officer approved the budget.  adj. 首席的；主要的  財務長核准了預算。
deputy  The deputy manager will cover while she is on leave.  n. 副手；adj. 副的  她休假期間，副理將代理其職務。
partner  He became a partner in the law firm after ten years.  n. 合夥人；夥伴  十年後，他成為這家律師事務所的合夥人。
owner  The owner of the restaurant plans to open a second location.  n. 所有人；老闆  這家餐廳的老闆計畫開設第二家分店。
founder  The founder started the company in a small garage.  n. 創辦人  創辦人在一間小車庫裡創立了這家公司。
board  The board of directors approved the merger on Thursday.  n. 董事會  董事會於週四核准了這項合併案。
board  Passengers should board the train at platform four.  v. 登上（交通工具）  乘客應在四號月台上車。
committee  A committee will review all proposals next month.  n. 委員會  委員會將於下個月審查所有提案。
hierarchy  The company has a flat hierarchy with few management levels.  n. 階層制度  這家公司採扁平化的階層制度，管理層級很少。
structure  The new organizational structure reduces the number of departments.  n. 結構；架構  新的組織架構減少了部門的數量。
structure  The firm will structure the deal as a merger.  v. 安排；建構  公司將把這筆交易安排成合併案。
organization  The organization employs more than two thousand people worldwide.  n. 組織；機構  這個機構在全球僱用了兩千多人。
restructure  The firm will restructure its sales team to cut costs.  v. 重組  公司將重組業務團隊以降低成本。
reorganize  We need to reorganize the department to improve efficiency.  v. 重新編組  我們需要重新編組部門以提升效率。
merge  The two banks will merge early next year.  v. 合併  這兩家銀行將於明年初合併。
transfer  She asked for a transfer to the Tokyo office.  n. 調職；v. 調動  她申請調職到東京辦公室。
transfer  Please transfer the funds to my account today.  v. 轉帳；匯款  請今天將款項轉入我的帳戶。
relocate  The company will relocate its staff to the new campus.  v. 遷移；調派  公司將把員工調派到新園區。
promote  The firm decided to promote him to regional manager.  v. 晉升；促銷  公司決定將他升為區域經理。
promotion  Her promotion to director came with a larger office.  n. 升遷  她升任主任後換到了較大的辦公室。
promotion  The summer promotion includes a twenty percent discount.  n. 促銷活動  夏季促銷活動包含八折優惠。
demote  The executive was demoted after the project failed.  v. 降職  專案失敗後，這位主管被降職了。
resign  The finance director will resign at the end of June.  v. 辭職  財務主任將於六月底辭職。
resignation  She submitted her resignation to the human resources office.  n. 辭呈；辭職  她向人資部門遞交了辭呈。
terminate  The company may terminate employment with thirty days of written notice.  v. 終止；解僱  公司可在三十天前以書面通知終止僱傭關係。
dismiss  The company had to dismiss two employees for misconduct.  v. 解僱  公司不得不因不當行為解僱兩名員工。
dismissal  Unfair dismissal claims are handled by the legal department.  n. 解僱  不當解僱的申訴由法務部門處理。
fire  The manager had to fire a worker who broke safety rules.  v. 解僱  經理不得不開除一名違反安全規定的工人。
fire  Employees must leave the building quickly in case of fire.  n. 火災  發生火災時，員工必須迅速離開大樓。
layoff  The factory announced a layoff of two hundred workers.  n. 裁員；資遣  工廠宣布裁員兩百人。
redundant  Many clerks became redundant after the system was automated.  adj. 冗員的；被裁的  系統自動化後，許多職員成為冗員。
downsize  The firm had to downsize its Asian operations last year.  v. 縮編；裁員  公司去年不得不縮編亞洲業務。
quit  Several engineers quit after the merger was announced.  v. 辭職；退出  合併消息公布後，有幾位工程師辭職了。
succeed  Daniel Wu will succeed Ann Lee as chief executive.  v. 接任  吳丹尼爾將接替李安成為執行長。
succeed  Our new campaign should succeed in attracting younger buyers.  v. 成功  我們的新活動應能成功吸引較年輕的買家。
successor  The board will name her successor next month.  n. 繼任者  董事會將於下個月公布她的繼任者。
replace  We need to replace the retiring receptionist soon.  v. 取代；接替  我們得盡快找人接替即將退休的接待人員。
replacement  A temporary replacement will cover her duties.  n. 接替者；替換品  一位臨時接替人員將代理她的職務。
delegate  A good manager knows how to delegate routine tasks.  v. 授權；委派  好的主管懂得委派例行工作。
delegate  Each country sent a delegate to the trade conference.  n. 代表  每個國家都派了一位代表參加貿易會議。
report  New employees report to the department head.  v. 向…報告；隸屬於  新進員工隸屬於部門主管。
report  The monthly sales report is due on Friday.  n. 報告  月銷售報告的繳交期限是週五。
oversee  She will oversee the construction of the new plant.  v. 監督  她將負責監督新廠房的興建。
supervise  Senior staff supervise the interns during the summer.  v. 監督；督導  暑假期間由資深員工督導實習生。
manage  He manages a team of fifteen sales representatives.  v. 管理；經營  他管理一個由十五名業務代表組成的團隊。
management  Senior management approved the new vacation policy.  n. 管理階層；管理  高階管理階層核准了新的休假政策。
authority  Only the director has authority to approve this expense.  n. 權限；權力  只有主任有權核准這筆費用。
responsibility  Safety is the responsibility of every employee.  n. 責任；職責  安全是每位員工的責任。
duty  Answering customer calls is her main duty.  n. 職責；義務  接聽客戶來電是她的主要職責。
duty  Imported goods are subject to a customs duty.  n. 關稅；稅  進口商品須課徵關稅。
role  Her role includes training new staff members.  n. 角色；職務  她的職務包括訓練新進員工。
assign  The manager will assign a mentor to each new hire.  v. 指派  經理會為每位新進員工指派一位導師。
assignment  Your first assignment is to update the client database.  n. 任務；工作  您的第一項任務是更新客戶資料庫。
mentor  An experienced mentor will guide you through your first year.  n. 導師；資深輔導者  一位經驗豐富的導師會在您的第一年中給予指導。
counterpart  Please send this proposal to your counterpart in the London office.  n. 對等職務的人  請將這份提案寄給您在倫敦辦公室的對口人員。
peer  The survey asks employees to evaluate their peers.  n. 同儕  這份問卷請員工評量自己的同儕。
liaison  She serves as a liaison between engineering and sales.  n. 聯絡人；聯繫  她擔任工程部與業務部之間的聯絡人。
spokesperson  The spokesperson declined to comment on the layoffs.  n. 發言人  發言人拒絕對裁員一事發表評論。
shareholder  Every shareholder will receive a copy of the annual report.  n. 股東  每位股東都會收到一份年報。
stakeholder  We will update every stakeholder on the progress of the project.  n. 利害關係人  我們會向每位利害關係人報告專案進度。
controller  The financial controller checks all major expenses.  n. 財務主管；會計主任  財務主管會審核所有重大支出。
treasurer  The treasurer will explain the financial status of the club.  n. 財務長；司庫  財務長將說明社團的財務狀況。
auditor  An external auditor will inspect the accounts in March.  n. 稽核員；審計員  外部稽核員將在三月查核帳目。
subsidiary  The company's subsidiary in Brazil is hiring new staff.  n. 子公司  該公司在巴西的子公司正在招募新員工。
performance  Her performance review is scheduled for next week.  n. 績效；表現  她的績效考核排定在下週。
appraisal  Annual appraisal meetings take place in December.  n. 考核；評鑑  年度考核會議在十二月舉行。
grievance  An employee may file a grievance with the personnel office.  n. 申訴；不滿  員工可向人事室提出申訴。
discipline  The company has a strict policy on workplace discipline.  n. 紀律；懲處  公司對職場紀律有嚴格的規定。
turnover  High staff turnover increases training costs.  n. 人員流動率；營業額  員工流動率高會增加訓練成本。
turnover  Annual turnover reached ten million dollars last year.  n. 營業額  去年的年營業額達到一千萬美元。
attrition  The firm reduced its staff through natural attrition.  n. 自然減員  該公司透過自然減員來縮減人力。
morale  The bonus helped improve morale across the department.  n. 士氣  這筆獎金有助於提升整個部門的士氣。
teamwork  Good teamwork helped us finish the project early.  n. 團隊合作  良好的團隊合作讓我們提早完成專案。
workplace  The company promotes a safe and respectful workplace.  n. 工作場所  公司致力於打造安全且相互尊重的工作場所。
accountable  Managers are accountable for the results of their teams.  adj. 負責的  主管要為團隊的成果負責。
designate  The director will designate a deputy to handle urgent matters.  v. 指定；任命  主任會指派一名代理人處理緊急事務。
# ===== 3 薪資福利與休假 =====
salary  The advertised salary depends on experience and qualifications.  n. 薪水  公告的薪資取決於經驗與資格條件。
wage  The minimum wage will rise by five percent next year.  n. 工資  基本工資明年將調漲百分之五。
pay  Employees receive their pay on the last working day of the month.  n. 薪資  員工在每月最後一個工作日領到薪水。
pay  Customers can pay by credit card or bank transfer.  v. 支付  顧客可以用信用卡或銀行轉帳付款。
payroll  The payroll department processes salaries every two weeks.  n. 薪資部門；薪資總額  薪資部門每兩週處理一次薪資。
bonus  Every employee will receive a year-end bonus this December.  n. 獎金  每位員工今年十二月都會領到年終獎金。
benefit  The benefits package includes health insurance and paid vacation.  n. 福利；好處  福利方案包含健康保險與有薪假。
benefit  Employees will benefit from the new flexible schedule.  v. 受益  員工將從新的彈性工時制度中受益。
allowance  Travel staff receive a daily meal allowance of fifty dollars.  n. 津貼；補助  出差人員每天可領五十美元的餐費津貼。
compensation  The compensation package includes stock options and a signing bonus.  n. 薪酬；補償  薪酬方案包含股票選擇權與簽約獎金。
raise  She asked her manager for a raise after the successful launch.  n. 加薪；v. 提高  產品成功上市後，她向主管要求加薪。
increment  Staff receive an annual increment based on their performance.  n. 定期加薪；增額  員工每年會依績效獲得定期加薪。
commission  Sales agents earn a five percent commission on every contract.  n. 佣金  業務員每簽成一份合約可賺取百分之五的佣金。
overtime  Overtime is paid at one and a half times the normal rate.  n. 加班；加班費  加班費按正常費率的一點五倍計算。
insurance  The company provides health insurance for all full-time employees.  n. 保險  公司為所有全職員工提供健康保險。
pension  She will receive a monthly pension after she retires.  n. 退休金  她退休後每月可領退休金。
retire  Our chief engineer will retire at the end of December.  v. 退休  我們的總工程師將在十二月底退休。
retirement  The retirement party will be held in the cafeteria.  n. 退休  退休慶祝會將在自助餐廳舉行。
leave  Employees may take unpaid leave for personal reasons.  n. 休假  員工可因私人因素請無薪假。
leave  Please leave your key at the front desk when you go.  v. 離開；留下  離開時請把鑰匙留在櫃檯。
vacation  All staff are entitled to ten days of paid vacation.  n. 休假；假期  所有員工都享有十天的有薪假期。
holiday  The office will be closed on the national holiday.  n. 國定假日；假日  辦公室在國定假日當天休息。
sick  Employees who feel sick should stay home and notify their supervisor.  adj. 生病的  覺得不舒服的員工應留在家中並通知主管。
maternity  Maternity leave lasts for eight weeks in our company.  adj. 產婦的；產假的  我們公司的產假為期八週。
paternity  New fathers can apply for paternity leave after the birth.  adj. 陪產的  新手爸爸可在孩子出生後申請陪產假。
absence  Please inform us of any planned absence in advance.  n. 缺席；請假  如有預定的請假，請提前告知我們。
absent  Three employees were absent because of the storm.  adj. 缺席的  因為暴風雨，有三名員工缺席。
deduct  The company will deduct tax from your monthly salary.  v. 扣除  公司會從你的月薪中扣除稅款。
deduction  The payslip lists every deduction from your gross pay.  n. 扣除額；扣款  薪資單列出了從你的稅前薪資中扣除的每一項款項。
withhold  Employers must withhold income tax from each paycheck.  v. 扣繳；保留  雇主必須從每張薪資支票中扣繳所得稅。
tax  Your tax is calculated on your annual income.  n. 稅  你的稅額是依年收入計算的。
reimburse  The firm will reimburse you for all travel costs.  v. 補償；核銷  公司會補償你所有的差旅費用。
reimbursement  Submit your receipts to request reimbursement for hotel expenses.  n. 報銷；補償  請提交收據以申請報銷住宿費用。
expense  Please attach receipts for every business expense.  n. 費用；開支  每筆業務支出都請附上收據。
stipend  Interns receive a monthly stipend during the program.  n. 津貼；生活補助  實習生在計畫期間每月可領生活津貼。
severance  Laid-off workers will receive severance pay equal to three months of salary.  n. 資遣費  被裁員的員工將領到相當於三個月薪水的資遣費。
entitle  Full-time status will entitle employees to dental coverage.  v. 使有資格；給予權利  全職身分讓員工有資格享有牙科保障。
perk  Free lunch is one of the perks of working here.  n. 額外福利  免費午餐是在這裡工作的額外福利之一。
incentive  The incentive program rewards teams that exceed their sales targets.  n. 獎勵；誘因  這項獎勵方案會獎賞超越銷售目標的團隊。
reward  The company will reward loyal employees with extra vacation days.  v. 獎勵；n. 獎賞  公司將以額外的休假天數獎勵忠誠的員工。
premium  The company pays half of the monthly insurance premium.  n. 保險費；溢價  公司負擔每月保險費的一半。
premium  We offer premium service to our corporate clients.  adj. 優質的；高級的  我們為企業客戶提供優質服務。
coverage  The plan offers full medical coverage for your family.  n. 保障範圍；承保  這個方案為你的家人提供完整的醫療保障。
dental  Our health plan includes dental and vision care.  adj. 牙齒的；牙科的  我們的健康方案包含牙科與視力照護。
medical  Employees receive a free medical checkup every year.  adj. 醫療的  員工每年可享有一次免費健康檢查。
healthcare  Healthcare costs have risen sharply in recent years.  n. 醫療保健  近年來醫療保健費用大幅上漲。
retiree  Retirees are invited to the annual company dinner.  n. 退休人員  退休人員受邀參加公司的年度聚餐。
annuity  He bought an annuity to guarantee income after retirement.  n. 年金  他購買了年金，以確保退休後有收入。
contribution  Your monthly contribution to the pension fund is automatic.  n. 繳款；貢獻  你每月提撥到退休基金的款項是自動扣繳的。
enroll  New employees can enroll in the health plan within thirty days.  v. 登記加入；註冊  新進員工可在三十天內登記加入健康方案。
enrollment  Open enrollment for benefits begins on November first.  n. 加入；登記  福利方案的開放登記從十一月一日開始。
vest  Your stock options will vest over a period of four years.  v. 取得既得權利  你的股票選擇權將在四年內分期生效。
gross  Gross income is your total pay before tax.  adj. 總額的；稅前的  總收入是你扣稅前的全部薪資。
net  Your net salary is the amount you receive after deductions.  adj. 淨額的；實領的  你的實領薪資是扣除各項款項後拿到的金額。
income  Her annual income increased after the promotion.  n. 收入  升遷後，她的年收入增加了。
earnings  The earnings of the company rose by ten percent last quarter.  n. 收益；獲利  該公司上一季的獲利成長了百分之十。
earn  Workers can earn extra pay by taking additional shifts.  v. 賺取  員工可以多排幾個班次來賺取額外薪資。
remuneration  Remuneration depends on the skills and experience of the candidate.  n. 酬勞  酬勞取決於應徵者的技能與經驗。
fringe  Health insurance is the most popular fringe benefit.  adj. 附加的；額外的  健康保險是最受歡迎的附加福利。
flextime  Flextime allows staff to choose their own starting hours.  n. 彈性上班制  彈性上班制讓員工可以自行選擇上班時間。
shift  The night shift begins at ten and ends at six.  n. 輪班；班次  夜班從十點開始，到六點結束。
shift  The company will shift its focus to online sales.  v. 轉移；轉變  公司將把重心轉向線上銷售。
paycheck  Your paycheck will be deposited into your account on Friday.  n. 薪資支票；薪水  你的薪水將於週五存入你的帳戶。
payslip  Your payslip is available on the employee portal.  n. 薪資單  你的薪資單可在員工入口網站上查看。
annual  Annual leave must be approved at least two weeks in advance.  adj. 年度的；每年的  年假必須至少提前兩週核准。
biweekly  Hourly staff are paid biweekly on Fridays through direct deposit.  adv. 每兩週一次  時薪員工每兩週的週五透過銀行直接匯款領薪。
hourly  Hourly workers record their time on the system each day.  adj. 按時計酬的；每小時的  時薪員工每天在系統上記錄工時。
paid  The position offers twelve days of paid leave.  adj. 有薪的  這個職位提供十二天的有薪假。
unpaid  Unpaid leave must be approved by the department head.  adj. 無薪的  無薪假必須經部門主管核准。
sabbatical  Professors may take a sabbatical after six years of service.  n. 休假進修；長假  教授在任職六年後可以休假進修。
furlough  The airline placed hundreds of workers on furlough.  n. 無薪休假  這家航空公司讓數百名員工放無薪假。
attendance  Regular attendance is required for all training sessions.  n. 出席；出勤  所有訓練課程都要求按時出席。
parental  Parental leave is available to both mothers and fathers.  adj. 父母的  育嬰假父母雙方都可以申請。
tuition  The company will cover tuition for approved evening courses.  n. 學費  公司會負擔經核准的夜間課程學費。
subsidy  The government offers a subsidy for employee training.  n. 補助金  政府提供員工訓練的補助金。
subsidize  The firm will subsidize half of your gym membership.  v. 補貼  公司會補貼你一半的健身房會費。
discount  Staff receive a thirty percent discount on all products.  n. 折扣  員工購買所有產品都享有七折優惠。
discount  The store will discount all winter coats by half.  v. 打折；減價  這家店將把所有冬季外套打五折。
voucher  Each employee received a meal voucher worth ten dollars.  n. 禮券；兌換券  每位員工都收到一張價值十美元的餐券。
housing  The package includes a monthly housing allowance for expatriates.  n. 住房；住宿  這個方案包含給外派人員的每月住房津貼。
negotiate  You can negotiate your salary after the first review.  v. 協商；談判  你可以在第一次考核後協商薪資。
negotiation  Salary negotiation usually takes place after the job offer.  n. 協商  薪資協商通常在錄取通知之後進行。
grade  Her pay grade increased after the promotion.  n. 等級；職等  升遷後，她的薪資職等提高了。
scale  The new pay scale rewards long-term service.  n. 薪級表；規模  新的薪級表獎勵長期服務的員工。
scale  The firm plans to scale up production next year.  v. 擴大規模  公司計畫明年擴大生產規模。
rate  The hourly rate for temporary staff is twenty dollars.  n. 費率；薪資標準  臨時人員的時薪是二十美元。
rate  Customers rate our service very highly.  v. 評價；評等  顧客對我們的服務評價很高。
minimum  The contract guarantees a minimum of forty hours per week.  n. 最低限度；adj. 最低的  合約保證每週至少四十小時。
fixed  Fixed salaries do not change with sales volume.  adj. 固定的  固定薪資不會隨銷售量而變動。
stock  Employees can buy company stock at a reduced price.  n. 股票  員工可以用優惠價格購買公司股票。
stock  The store keeps a large stock of office supplies.  n. 庫存  這家店備有大量的辦公用品庫存。
option  Stock options are part of the executive compensation plan.  n. 選擇權；選項  股票選擇權是高階主管薪酬方案的一部分。
equity  Senior staff also receive equity in the company.  n. 股權  資深員工也能獲得公司的股權。
profit-sharing  The profit-sharing plan pays out each March.  n. 利潤分享  利潤分享計畫每年三月發放。
adjustment  A salary adjustment will take effect in April.  n. 調整  薪資調整將於四月生效。
accrue  Vacation days accrue each month you work.  v. 累積  休假天數會隨著你每個月的工作而累積。
deposit  Your salary is deposited directly into your bank account.  v. 存入；n. 押金  你的薪水會直接存入你的銀行帳戶。
withdraw  You may withdraw money from the pension plan after retirement.  v. 提領  退休後你可以從退休金計畫中提領款項。
timesheet  Please submit your timesheet by noon on Friday.  n. 工時表  請在週五中午前繳交你的工時表。
bereavement  Bereavement leave is granted after the death of a close relative.  n. 喪假；喪親  近親過世後可請喪假。
wellness  The wellness program offers free yoga classes.  n. 健康；保健  這項保健計畫提供免費的瑜伽課程。
membership  The company pays for your gym membership.  n. 會員資格  公司會支付你的健身房會員費用。
commute  The shuttle makes the commute easier for staff.  n. 通勤；通勤路程  接駁車讓員工的通勤更輕鬆。
telecommute  Staff may telecommute two days a week.  v. 遠距工作  員工每週可以遠距工作兩天。
remote  Remote workers receive an allowance for home office equipment.  adj. 遠距的；偏遠的  遠距工作者可領取居家辦公設備津貼。
hybrid  Our hybrid schedule allows work from home on Wednesdays.  adj. 混合式的  我們的混合式工作制允許週三在家工作。
flexible  We offer flexible hours to parents of young children.  adj. 彈性的  我們為有幼兒的家長提供彈性工時。
maximum  The maximum bonus is equal to two months of salary.  adj. 最高的；n. 最大量  獎金上限相當於兩個月的薪水。
payout  The annual payout depends on company profits.  n. 給付；支付款項  年度給付金額取決於公司獲利。
# ===== 4 培訓與教育 =====
training  All new employees must complete the safety training before starting work on the factory floor.  n. 訓練；培訓  所有新進員工在進入廠區工作前，都必須完成安全訓練。
workshop  The human resources department will hold a workshop on effective communication next Friday.  n. 工作坊；研討會  人資部門下週五將舉辦一場有效溝通的工作坊。
seminar  Mr. Lee will lead a seminar on digital marketing at the downtown office.  n. 研討會；專題講座  李先生將在市區辦公室主講一場數位行銷講座。
course  Registration for the advanced accounting course closes at the end of this week.  n. 課程  進階會計課程的報名將於本週末截止。
certificate  Participants will receive a certificate after they finish all five training modules.  n. 證書；結業證明  參加者完成全部五個訓練單元後，將獲頒結業證書。
instructor  The instructor will explain how to use the new inventory software during the first session.  n. 講師；指導員  講師會在第一堂課說明如何使用新的庫存軟體。
curriculum  The school updated its curriculum to include more business English classes.  n. 課程；課程內容  學校更新了課程，增加更多商務英文課。
attend  All department managers are required to attend the leadership workshop on Thursday.  v. 參加；出席  所有部門主管都必須參加週四的領導力工作坊。
register  Please register for the training session before the end of the month.  v. 註冊；報名  請在月底前報名參加培訓課程。
register  The cashier opened the register and counted the cash.  n. 收銀機  收銀員打開收銀機清點現金。
evaluate  Supervisors will evaluate each trainee at the end of the program.  v. 評估；評核  主管將在課程結束時評核每位受訓學員。
tutorial  The online tutorial shows step by step how to create a monthly report.  n. 教學課程；使用教學  這份線上教學逐步示範如何製作月報表。
develop  The workshop helps managers develop stronger leadership skills.  v. 培養；發展  這個工作坊幫助主管培養更強的領導能力。
session  The morning session will cover customer service basics and complaint handling.  n. 場次；課程時段  上午場次將涵蓋客戶服務基礎與客訴處理。
participant  Each participant will receive a booklet containing the course materials.  n. 參加者；學員  每位參加者都會收到一本包含課程教材的手冊。
proficiency  Proof of English proficiency is required for all international applicants.  n. 熟練度；精通  所有國際申請者都必須提供英文能力證明。
competence  The test measures basic computer competence rather than advanced technical knowledge.  n. 能力；勝任  這項測驗評量的是基本的電腦能力，而非進階的技術知識。
accredit  Only accredited institutions can offer this professional license program.  v. 認可；認證  只有經過認證的機構才能開辦這個專業證照課程。
instruct  The manual will instruct users on how to reset their password safely.  v. 指導；教導  這份手冊會指導使用者如何安全地重設密碼。
lecture  The guest lecture on international trade begins at two o'clock in Room 301.  n. 演講；講課  關於國際貿易的專題演講將於兩點在301教室開始。
syllabus  The syllabus lists all reading assignments and exam dates for the semester.  n. 課程大綱  課程大綱列出了本學期所有的閱讀作業與考試日期。
module  The second module focuses on time management and workplace productivity.  n. 單元；模組  第二個單元的重點是時間管理與職場生產力。
refresher  A refresher course on fire safety will be offered to all staff in March.  n. 進修課程；複習課  三月將為全體員工開設消防安全的進修課程。
onboarding  The onboarding process includes a tour of the building and a meeting with the team.  n. 新人到職培訓  新人到職培訓包括參觀大樓以及與團隊見面。
coach  The company hired a coach to help sales representatives improve their presentation skills.  n. 教練；輔導員  公司聘請了一位教練，幫助業務代表提升簡報技巧。
scholarship  She received a scholarship to study business administration at a local university.  n. 獎學金  她獲得獎學金，到當地一所大學攻讀企業管理。
diploma  Candidates must submit a copy of their high school diploma with the application.  n. 文憑；畢業證書  應徵者必須在申請時附上高中畢業證書的影本。
semester  The new semester begins on September 5, and classes fill up quickly.  n. 學期  新學期從九月五日開始，課程很快就會額滿。
faculty  The faculty will meet next week to discuss changes to the curriculum.  n. 全體教職員；院系  全體教職員下週將開會討論課程的調整。
academic  The academic calendar is available on the university's official website.  adj. 學術的；學業的  學年行事曆可在大學官方網站上查閱。
vocational  The city offers vocational training for people who want to change careers.  adj. 職業的；職業技能的  市政府為想轉換職涯的人提供職業技能訓練。
feedback  Please give honest feedback about the workshop on the attached survey form.  n. 回饋；意見反應  請在附上的問卷中，針對這場工作坊提出真實的意見回饋。
knowledge  The course provides participants with the knowledge needed to manage a small budget.  n. 知識  這門課為參加者提供管理小額預算所需的知識。
familiarize  The first week helps new hires familiarize themselves with company policies.  v. 使熟悉  第一週能幫助新進員工熟悉公司的各項政策。
acquire  Employees can acquire new skills through the company's online learning platform.  v. 取得；習得  員工可以透過公司的線上學習平台習得新技能。
enhance  The program is designed to enhance employees' technical and managerial abilities.  v. 提升；增進  這項計畫旨在提升員工的技術與管理能力。
comprehensive  The comprehensive training guide covers every step of the ordering process.  adj. 全面的；綜合的  這份完整的訓練指南涵蓋了訂購流程的每個步驟。
mandatory  Attendance at the safety briefing is mandatory for all warehouse staff.  adj. 強制的；必須的  所有倉庫人員都必須出席安全說明會。
prerequisite  Basic accounting is a prerequisite for the advanced finance course.  n. 先修條件；必備條件  基礎會計是進階財務課程的先修條件。
hands-on  The workshop offers hands-on practice with the latest design software.  adj. 親自動手的；實務操作的  這個工作坊提供使用最新設計軟體的實務操作練習。
interactive  The interactive lesson allows learners to ask questions in real time.  adj. 互動式的  這堂互動式課程讓學習者可以即時提問。
practical  The training focuses on practical solutions that employees can use right away.  adj. 實用的；實務的  這項訓練著重於員工可以立即運用的實用解決方案。
beginner  This class is suitable for any beginner with no previous programming experience.  n. 初學者  這門課適合沒有任何程式設計經驗的初學者。
advanced  Advanced learners may join the evening class to prepare for the certification exam.  adj. 進階的；高級的  進階學習者可參加夜間班，準備認證考試。
intermediate  Intermediate students should have completed at least one semester of Spanish.  adj. 中級的  中級學生應已修完至少一學期的西班牙文。
registration  Online registration for the conference is available until the end of the month.  n. 註冊；登記  研討會的線上報名開放至月底。
exam  Candidates must pass the written exam before they can start the practical test.  n. 考試  考生必須先通過筆試，才能開始實作測驗。
quiz  A short quiz at the end of each unit checks what students have learned.  n. 小考；測驗  每個單元結束時的小考會檢驗學生學到了什麼。
pass  Trainees who pass the final test will be offered full-time positions.  v. 通過；及格  通過最終測驗的受訓學員將獲得全職職位。
pass  Please pass the report to the manager after lunch.  v. 傳遞；轉交  午餐後請把這份報告轉交給經理。
review  Please review the training materials before attending tomorrow's session.  v. 複習；審閱  請在參加明天的課程前先複習訓練教材。
review  The magazine published a favorable review of the new model.  n. 評論；評價  這本雜誌刊登了對新機型的正面評論。
practice  Regular practice helps new employees become confident in handling customer calls.  n. 練習；實務  定期練習能幫助新進員工在處理客戶來電時更有自信。
practice  It is standard practice to keep copies of all receipts.  n. 慣例；做法  保留所有收據的影本是標準做法。
drill  The company will conduct a fire drill on Wednesday morning.  n. 演習；反覆練習  公司將在週三早上進行消防演習。
simulation  The pilot training includes a flight simulation that reproduces real weather conditions.  n. 模擬  飛行員訓練包含重現真實天氣狀況的飛行模擬。
webinar  Register for our free webinar on cybersecurity and learn how to protect your data.  n. 線上研討會  報名我們的免費網路資安線上研討會，學習如何保護你的資料。
handout  The instructor distributed a handout summarizing the main points of the lecture.  n. 講義；散發的資料  講師發下一份摘要演講重點的講義。
textbook  The textbook for this course is available at the campus bookstore.  n. 教科書  這門課的教科書可在校園書店買到。
literacy  Financial literacy classes teach residents how to manage their savings.  n. 讀寫能力；素養  理財素養課程教導居民如何管理自己的存款。
fluency  Fluency in English and Japanese is a requirement for this position.  n. 流利；流暢  這個職位要求英文與日文都要流利。
lesson  Each lesson takes about thirty minutes and includes a short exercise.  n. 一堂課；課程  每堂課約三十分鐘，並包含一個簡短的練習。
educator  Experienced educators will present new teaching methods at the annual convention.  n. 教育工作者  經驗豐富的教育工作者將在年會上介紹新的教學方法。
trainer  The trainer demonstrated how to operate the machine safely.  n. 訓練師；培訓講師  培訓講師示範了如何安全地操作這台機器。
learner  Adult learners can choose between evening classes and weekend courses.  n. 學習者  成人學習者可以在夜間班與週末課程之間做選擇。
tutor  A private tutor helped him prepare for the language proficiency test.  n. 家教；輔導老師  一位家教協助他準備語言能力測驗。
guidance  Career guidance is provided free of charge to all current students.  n. 指導；輔導  在校學生均可免費獲得職涯輔導。
demonstrate  The instructor will demonstrate how to enter data into the new system.  v. 示範；展示  講師將示範如何把資料輸入新系統。
equip  The course will equip participants with tools for managing difficult conversations.  v. 使具備；配備  該課程將使參與者具備處理棘手對話的工具。
empower  The program aims to empower young workers to take on leadership roles.  v. 賦予能力；授權  該計畫旨在賦予年輕員工能力，讓他們承擔領導角色。
cultivate  Good managers cultivate a culture of continuous learning within their teams.  v. 培養；栽培  優秀的主管會在團隊中培養持續學習的文化。
sharpen  The workshop will sharpen your negotiation skills through realistic role-play exercises.  v. 磨練；加強  這場工作坊將透過擬真的角色扮演練習，加強你的談判技巧。
refine  Participants will refine their writing skills by editing real business letters.  v. 精進；改善  參加者將透過編修真實的商業書信來精進寫作技巧。
master  It takes several months to master the new accounting software.  v. 精通；掌握  要精通這套新的會計軟體需要好幾個月的時間。
attain  Employees who attain level three certification receive a salary increase.  v. 達到；取得  取得三級認證的員工可獲得加薪。
pursue  Many staff members pursue further education while working full time.  v. 追求；從事  許多員工在全職工作的同時持續進修。
license  A valid license is required to operate the heavy machinery.  n. 執照；許可證  操作這部重型機械需要持有有效的執照。
compliance  The annual compliance training covers data protection and workplace ethics.  n. 遵循；合規  年度合規訓練涵蓋資料保護與職場倫理。
outcome  The expected learning outcome is described at the beginning of each unit.  n. 成果；結果  預期的學習成果會在每個單元開頭說明。
objective  The main objective of the workshop is to improve team communication.  n. 目標；目的  這場工作坊的主要目標是改善團隊溝通。
milestone  Completing the first module is an important milestone for every new learner.  n. 里程碑；階段性成果  完成第一個模組對每位新學員而言是重要的里程碑。
retention  The study shows that short videos improve knowledge retention among adult learners.  n. 記憶；保留  研究顯示，短影片能提升成人學習者的知識記憶。
instructional  The company produced instructional videos to teach staff how to use the equipment.  adj. 教學的；教導的  公司製作了教學影片，教員工如何使用這項設備。
coursework  Coursework must be submitted online before the end of each month.  n. 課業；課堂作業  課堂作業必須在每月底前於線上繳交。
certify  The board will certify only trainers who pass the final evaluation.  v. 認證；證明  董事會只會認證通過最終評核的訓練師。
competency  Each employee must demonstrate basic competency in safety procedures before starting work.  n. 能力；勝任度  每位員工在開始工作前，都必須展現基本的安全程序能力。
cohort  The first cohort of trainees will begin the leadership program in January.  n. 同期學員；一批學員  第一批受訓學員將於一月開始領導力課程。
upskill  The company offers free courses to help employees upskill in data analysis.  v. 提升技能  公司提供免費課程，協助員工提升資料分析技能。
reskill  We plan to reskill factory workers for new roles in robotics maintenance.  v. 重新培訓技能  我們打算重新培訓工廠員工，以擔任機器人維修的新職務。
mentorship  She credits the mentorship of a senior manager for her rapid promotion.  n. 師徒指導；導師輔導  她將自己的快速升遷歸功於一位資深主管的指導。
coaching  Individual coaching sessions are available for all new team leaders.  n. 輔導；指導  所有新任組長都可以參加一對一輔導課程。
classroom  The training will be held in the main classroom on the third floor.  n. 教室  訓練將在三樓的主教室舉行。
workbook  Please complete the first chapter of the workbook before attending tomorrow's session.  n. 習作本；練習手冊  請在參加明天的課程前完成習作本的第一章。
lecturer  The guest lecturer will speak about international trade regulations.  n. 講師；講課者  這位客座講師將談論國際貿易法規。
induction  All new hires must attend an induction session on their first day.  n. 新進人員訓練；入職說明  所有新進員工都必須在第一天參加入職說明會。
debrief  We will debrief the participants immediately after the simulation ends.  v. 事後匯報；聽取報告  模擬結束後，我們會立即聽取參與者的報告。
shadow  New recruits will shadow an experienced agent for the first two weeks.  v. 跟隨見習  新進人員將在前兩週跟隨一位資深專員見習。
tutoring  Free tutoring is offered to employees who are preparing for the certification exam.  n. 個別輔導；家教  備考認證考試的員工可獲得免費輔導。
enrichment  The enrichment program includes weekend workshops on art and technology.  n. 充實課程；增進  這項充實課程包含關於藝術與科技的週末工作坊。
mastery  Mastery of the new software requires several weeks of regular practice.  n. 精通；熟練  要精通這套新軟體，需要數週的規律練習。
accreditation  The institute received accreditation from the national education board last year.  n. 認證；立案  該機構去年獲得國家教育委員會的認證。
pedagogy  Her research focuses on modern pedagogy in adult education.  n. 教學法；教育學  她的研究聚焦於成人教育中的現代教學法。
proctor  A proctor will supervise the online exam to prevent cheating.  n. 監考人；v. 監考  一名監考人員會監督這場線上考試，以防止作弊。
e-learning  The new e-learning platform lets staff complete courses at their own pace.  n. 線上學習；數位學習  這個新的線上學習平台讓員工能依自己的步調完成課程。
# ===== 5 會議與簡報 =====
meeting  The quarterly meeting will take place in the main conference room at ten o'clock.  n. 會議  季會將於十點在主會議室舉行。
agenda  Please review the agenda before the meeting and send me any additional topics.  n. 議程  請在會前先看過議程，並把其他想討論的議題寄給我。
presentation  Ms. Tan will give a presentation on the new marketing strategy.  n. 簡報；報告  譚女士將針對新的行銷策略進行簡報。
proposal  The board will vote on the proposal to open a second branch in Osaka.  n. 提案；企劃書  董事會將就在大阪開設第二間分公司的提案進行表決。
discuss  We will discuss the budget for next year at Thursday's meeting.  v. 討論  我們將在週四的會議上討論明年的預算。
minutes  The secretary will send out the minutes of the meeting by the end of the day.  n. 會議紀錄  祕書將在今天下班前寄出會議紀錄。
conference  The annual sales conference will be held at the Grand Hotel in Chicago.  n. 大型會議；研討會  年度業務大會將在芝加哥的格蘭飯店舉行。
chair  Ms. Kim will chair the meeting while the director is traveling.  v. 主持；擔任主席  主任出差期間，金女士將主持這場會議。
agree  All team members agree that the project deadline should be extended.  v. 同意；贊同  所有團隊成員都同意專案期限應該延長。
decision  The final decision will be announced after the committee reviews all the reports.  n. 決定；決策  委員會審閱完所有報告後，將公布最終決定。
resolution  The board passed a resolution to reduce energy costs across all offices.  n. 決議；決議案  董事會通過一項決議，要降低所有辦公室的能源成本。
vote  Members will vote on the new bylaws at the end of the meeting.  v. 投票；表決  成員將在會議結束時就新的章程進行投票。
motion  Mr. Brown made a motion to postpone the discussion until next month.  n. 動議  布朗先生提出動議，將討論延後到下個月。
propose  I would like to propose a change to the current work schedule.  v. 提議；提出  我想針對目前的工作時程提議做個調整。
suggest  Several participants suggest holding the meeting online to save travel costs.  v. 建議  幾位與會者建議以線上方式開會，以節省差旅費用。
recommend  The consultant will recommend ways to reduce production costs.  v. 建議；推薦  顧問將建議降低生產成本的方法。
concern  Some employees expressed concern about the proposed changes to the schedule.  n. 疑慮；擔憂  一些員工對於時程的擬議變動表達了疑慮。
clarify  Could you clarify what you mean by the second item on the agenda?  v. 釐清；說明  您可以說明一下議程第二項是什麼意思嗎？
summarize  Let me summarize the main points before we move to the next topic.  v. 總結；摘要  在進入下一個議題之前，讓我先總結重點。
outline  The speaker will outline the company's goals for the coming year.  v. 概述；簡述  講者將概述公司來年的目標。
overview  The first slide gives an overview of the project timeline.  n. 概觀；概要  第一張投影片概述了專案的時程。
slide  Please keep each slide simple and limit the text to five lines.  n. 投影片  請保持每張投影片簡潔，文字限制在五行以內。
projector  The projector in Room 4 is not working, so we will use the large monitor.  n. 投影機  四號會議室的投影機壞了，所以我們會改用大螢幕。
brainstorm  The team will brainstorm ideas for the product launch on Monday afternoon.  v. 腦力激盪  團隊將在週一下午為產品上市進行腦力激盪。
consensus  After a long discussion, the committee reached a consensus on the budget.  n. 共識  經過長時間的討論，委員會對預算達成共識。
objection  If there is no objection, we will approve the schedule as proposed.  n. 反對；異議  如果沒有異議，我們就依提案通過這份時程。
update  Mr. Garcia will provide an update on the construction project during the meeting.  n. 最新進度；v. 更新  賈西亞先生將在會議中報告建設專案的最新進度。
postpone  The meeting has been postponed until next Wednesday because of the holiday.  v. 延後；延期  由於假期，會議已延後到下週三。
reschedule  I need to reschedule our appointment because I have a conflict on Tuesday.  v. 重新安排時間  我必須重新安排我們的約會，因為我週二有衝突。
schedule  The schedule for the workshop is attached to this email.  n. 時程表；v. 排定  工作坊的時程表附在這封電子郵件中。
highlight  Use the last slide to highlight the key results of the survey.  v. 強調；n. 重點  請用最後一張投影片來強調這項調查的關鍵結果。
emphasize  The CEO emphasized the importance of customer satisfaction in her speech.  v. 強調  執行長在演說中強調了顧客滿意度的重要性。
topic  The main topic of today's meeting is the upcoming office relocation.  n. 主題；議題  今天會議的主要議題是即將到來的辦公室搬遷。
item  The next item on the agenda is the revised sales forecast.  n. 項目；議程事項  議程上的下一項是修訂後的銷售預測。
follow-up  A follow-up meeting is scheduled for the following Monday at nine.  n. 後續追蹤；adj. 後續的  後續追蹤會議訂於下週一九點舉行。
adjourn  The chairperson decided to adjourn the meeting at five o'clock.  v. 休會；散會  主席決定在五點休會。
convene  The committee will convene at noon to discuss the revised budget.  v. 召集；開會  委員會將在中午召集開會，討論修訂後的預算。
quorum  We cannot hold a vote today because we do not have a quorum.  n. 法定出席人數  由於未達法定出席人數，我們今天無法進行表決。
briefing  The manager will hold a short briefing on the new safety rules.  n. 簡報會；情況說明  經理將就新的安全規定舉行一場簡短的說明會。
forum  The online forum allows customers to share their ideas with the product team.  n. 論壇；討論會  這個線上論壇讓顧客能與產品團隊分享想法。
panel  A panel of industry experts will answer questions after the keynote speech.  n. 專家小組；座談小組  一組產業專家將在主題演講後回答提問。
speaker  The keynote speaker will talk about trends in renewable energy.  n. 講者；演講者  主題演講者將談論再生能源的趨勢。
audience  The audience asked many questions about the new pricing plan.  n. 聽眾；觀眾  聽眾對新的定價方案提出了許多問題。
keynote  The keynote address will open the conference at nine on Friday.  n. 主題演講  主題演講將於週五九點為大會揭開序幕。
moderator  The moderator will make sure each panelist has equal time to speak.  n. 主持人；司儀  主持人將確保每位與談人都有相同的發言時間。
present  Ms. Reyes will present the quarterly results to the board.  v. 報告；呈現  雷耶斯女士將向董事會報告季度成果。
present  Only three of the managers were present at the meeting.  adj. 出席的；在場的  經理中只有三人出席了這場會議。
illustrate  This chart helps illustrate the sharp increase in online sales.  v. 說明；圖解  這張圖表有助於說明線上銷售的大幅成長。
argue  Some members argue that the project is too expensive for this year.  v. 主張；爭論  一些成員主張，這個專案對今年來說太昂貴了。
debate  The directors continued to debate the proposal for more than two hours.  v. 辯論；討論  董事們持續就這項提案辯論了兩個多小時。
challenge  The main challenge is to finish the project without exceeding the budget.  n. 挑戰  最主要的挑戰是在不超出預算的情況下完成專案。
feasible  The engineers believe the plan is feasible if the budget is increased.  adj. 可行的  工程師認為，只要增加預算，這項計畫就是可行的。
priority  Reducing costs is our top priority for the next quarter.  n. 優先事項  降低成本是我們下一季的首要任務。
strategy  The marketing team presented a new strategy for entering the Asian market.  n. 策略  行銷團隊提出了進軍亞洲市場的新策略。
goal  Our goal is to increase customer satisfaction by ten percent this year.  n. 目標  我們的目標是今年將顧客滿意度提高百分之十。
target  The sales target for the third quarter was discussed at length.  n. 目標；指標  第三季的銷售目標獲得了詳盡的討論。
target  The new ads target young professionals.  v. 以…為目標對象  這些新廣告以年輕上班族為目標對象。
progress  The project manager reported steady progress on the website redesign.  n. 進展；進度  專案經理回報網站改版進度穩定。
input  We would appreciate your input on the draft before Friday's meeting.  n. 意見；投入  我們希望能在週五的會議前，收到你對草案的意見。
suggestion  Please submit any suggestions for improvement through the online form.  n. 建議  請透過線上表單提交任何改進建議。
alternative  The team presented an alternative to the original plan.  n. 替代方案；adj. 替代的  團隊提出了一個取代原計畫的替代方案。
approval  The proposal is still waiting for approval from the finance department.  n. 核准；批准  這項提案仍在等待財務部門的核准。
endorse  The union leaders endorse the new work schedule.  v. 贊同；背書  工會領袖贊同新的工作時程。
support  Most board members support the plan to expand into new markets.  v. 支持  大多數董事會成員支持拓展新市場的計畫。
support  Contact technical support if the problem continues.  n. 技術支援；支援  如果問題持續發生，請聯絡技術支援。
oppose  A few directors oppose the merger because of the high cost.  v. 反對  幾位董事因成本過高而反對這項合併案。
disagree  I disagree with the estimate because it does not include shipping costs.  v. 不同意  我不同意這個估算，因為它沒有包含運費。
compromise  Both sides made a compromise to settle the dispute over the budget.  n. 妥協；v. 讓步  雙方做出妥協，以解決預算上的爭議。
modify  The team will modify the proposal based on the feedback from the client.  v. 修改  團隊將根據客戶的意見修改提案。
finalize  We hope to finalize the agreement before the end of the week.  v. 定案；完成  我們希望在本週結束前敲定協議。
conclude  The meeting will conclude with a short question and answer session.  v. 結束；下結論  會議將以簡短的問答時間作結。
recap  Let me recap the three decisions we made during today's meeting.  v. 扼要重述；n. 摘要  讓我扼要重述今天會議中我們做出的三項決定。
circulate  Please circulate the draft agenda to all department heads by Monday.  v. 傳閱；分發  請在週一前把議程草案傳閱給所有部門主管。
distribute  The assistant will distribute copies of the report at the start of the meeting.  v. 分發；分送  助理將在會議開始時分發報告的影本。
confirm  Please confirm your attendance at the meeting by replying to this message.  v. 確認  請回覆這則訊息，確認你會出席會議。
invite  We invite all department heads to join the planning meeting on Friday.  v. 邀請  我們邀請所有部門主管參加週五的規劃會議。
cancel  The afternoon session was canceled because the speaker was ill.  v. 取消  由於講者生病，下午的場次已被取消。
conflict  I have a scheduling conflict on Thursday and cannot attend the meeting.  n. 衝突；牴觸  我週四的行程有衝突，無法出席會議。
availability  Please let me know your availability for a call next week.  n. 空檔；可行時間  請告訴我你下週哪些時間可以通話。
venue  The venue for the conference has been changed to the City Convention Center.  n. 場地；會場  會議場地已改到市政會議中心。
teleconference  The teleconference will connect our offices in Seoul, Berlin, and Toronto.  n. 電話會議  這場電話會議將連線我們位於首爾、柏林與多倫多的辦公室。
participate  All team members are encouraged to participate in the discussion.  v. 參與  我們鼓勵所有團隊成員參與討論。
record  The assistant will record the minutes and share them after the meeting.  v. 記錄；n. 紀錄  助理會記錄會議紀錄，並在會後分享。
record  Sales reached a record high in the third quarter.  adj. 創紀錄的  第三季的銷售額創下歷史新高。
notes  Please bring your notes from the last meeting to tomorrow's session.  n. 筆記；備忘  請帶著上次會議的筆記來參加明天的會議。
timeline  The proposed timeline shows the project finishing in late November.  n. 時間表；進度表  擬議的時間表顯示專案將在十一月底完成。
bullet  Use a bullet list on each slide so the audience can follow the main points.  n. 項目符號  請在每張投影片上使用項目符號清單，讓聽眾能跟上主要重點。
chart  The chart on page four compares sales figures from the last two years.  n. 圖表  第四頁的圖表比較了過去兩年的銷售數字。
statistics  The presentation included statistics from the latest customer survey.  n. 統計數據  簡報中包含了最新顧客調查的統計數據。
forecast  The sales forecast for next quarter was presented at the meeting.  n. 預測  下一季的銷售預測已在會議中提出。
rehearse  Please rehearse your presentation at least twice before the client arrives.  v. 排練；預演  請在客戶抵達前，至少排練兩次你的簡報。
podium  The speaker stepped up to the podium and thanked the audience.  n. 講台  講者走上講台，向聽眾致謝。
preside  The vice president will preside over the meeting in the director's absence.  v. 主持；主導  董事長缺席期間，副總裁將主持這場會議。
summary  A short summary of the results is included on the first page.  n. 摘要；總結  結果的簡短摘要附在第一頁。
remark  The director made a brief remark about the delay before starting the discussion.  n. 發言；評論  主任在開始討論前，簡短評論了延誤一事。
announce  The company will announce its new product line at the press conference.  v. 宣布；公布  公司將在記者會上宣布新的產品系列。
elaborate  Could you elaborate on how the new system will reduce our costs?  v. 詳細說明  您可以詳細說明新系統將如何降低我們的成本嗎？
pending  The decision on the budget is still pending until the next meeting.  adj. 待決的；未定的  預算的決定在下次會議前仍待定。
presenter  The presenter used several charts to explain the quarterly results.  n. 簡報者；報告人  簡報者用了幾張圖表來解釋季度成果。
panelist  Each panelist will have five minutes to share an opinion on the proposal.  n. 座談會與談人  每位與談人將有五分鐘分享對這項提案的看法。
slideshow  The slideshow includes photos from last year's company retreat.  n. 投影片放映；幻燈片秀  這個投影片放映包含去年公司旅遊的照片。
chairperson  The chairperson called the meeting to order at nine o'clock sharp.  n. 主席；會議主持人  主席在九點整宣布會議開始。
rebuttal  The finance team prepared a rebuttal to the criticism raised in the meeting.  n. 反駁；反論  財務團隊針對會議中提出的批評準備了反駁。
takeaway  The main takeaway from the discussion is that we need a clearer schedule.  n. 重點結論；心得  這場討論的主要重點結論是，我們需要更明確的時程。
# ===== 6 辦公室與設備 =====
equipment  All office equipment must be turned off at the end of the working day.  n. 設備；器材  所有辦公設備都必須在下班時關閉。
copier  The copier on the third floor is out of toner again.  n. 影印機  三樓的影印機又沒碳粉了。
printer  Please check that the printer has enough paper before you send the document.  n. 印表機  請在傳送文件前，確認印表機有足夠的紙張。
photocopy  Could you photocopy these contracts and bring them to my office?  v. 影印；n. 影本  你可以把這些合約影印一份，帶到我的辦公室嗎？
supply  Office supply orders must be approved by the department manager.  n. 用品；供應品  辦公用品的訂購必須經部門經理核准。
supply  The factory will supply parts to three automakers.  v. 供應；供給  這家工廠將為三家汽車製造商供應零件。
stationery  The company ordered new stationery with the updated logo and address.  n. 文具  公司訂購了印有新標誌和地址的新文具。
cartridge  The ink cartridge needs to be replaced before we can print the brochures.  n. 墨水匣  我們得先更換墨水匣，才能列印這些手冊。
toner  Please order extra toner for the laser printer in the accounting office.  n. 碳粉  請為會計室的雷射印表機多訂一些碳粉。
jam  A paper jam in the copier delayed the printing of the handouts.  n. 卡紙；堵塞  影印機卡紙，延誤了講義的列印。
maintenance  Routine maintenance of the air conditioning system will take place on Saturday.  n. 維護；保養  空調系統的例行維護將於週六進行。
repair  A technician will repair the broken scanner this afternoon.  v. 修理；n. 維修  技術人員今天下午會修理壞掉的掃描機。
malfunction  A malfunction in the elevator caused a delay in the morning.  n. 故障；v. 發生故障  電梯故障造成早上的延誤。
defective  The defective keyboard was returned to the manufacturer for a full refund.  adj. 有瑕疵的；故障的  這個有瑕疵的鍵盤已退還給製造商並全額退款。
install  The IT staff will install the new software on all computers this weekend.  v. 安裝  資訊人員本週末將在所有電腦上安裝新軟體。
upgrade  The company plans to upgrade its network equipment by the end of the year.  v. 升級；n. 升級版  公司計畫在年底前升級網路設備。
troubleshoot  The technician will troubleshoot the connection problem in the conference room.  v. 排除故障  技術人員將排除會議室的連線問題。
manual  The user manual explains how to load paper into the printer.  n. 使用手冊；adj. 手動的  使用手冊說明了如何將紙張放入印表機。
instruction  Follow the instructions on the screen to reset your password.  n. 說明；指示  請依照螢幕上的指示重設你的密碼。
warranty  The warranty covers all repairs for the first two years after purchase.  n. 保固  保固涵蓋購買後前兩年的所有維修。
device  Please keep your mobile device away from the testing equipment.  n. 裝置；設備  請讓你的行動裝置遠離測試設備。
machine  The coffee machine in the break room has been out of order since Monday.  n. 機器  休息室的咖啡機從週一起就故障了。
scanner  Place the document face down on the scanner and press the green button.  n. 掃描機  請把文件正面朝下放在掃描機上，然後按下綠色按鈕。
fax  Please fax the signed form to our office before noon.  v. 傳真；n. 傳真機  請在中午前把簽好的表格傳真到我們辦公室。
shredder  Confidential documents must be destroyed in the shredder located next to the copier.  n. 碎紙機  機密文件必須在影印機旁的碎紙機銷毀。
stapler  Could I borrow your stapler for a moment?  n. 釘書機  我可以借用一下你的釘書機嗎？
staple  Please staple the receipts to the expense form before submitting it.  v. 裝訂；n. 釘書針  請在提交前，把收據釘在費用申請單上。
envelope  Please place the invoice in a large envelope and mail it today.  n. 信封  請把發票放進大信封裡，今天寄出。
folder  Store all signed contracts in the blue folder on my desk.  n. 資料夾；文件夾  請把所有簽好的合約存放在我桌上的藍色資料夾裡。
binder  The training binder contains copies of all company policies.  n. 活頁夾  訓練手冊活頁夾裡有所有公司政策的影本。
label  Please label each box clearly before it is moved to the storage room.  v. 貼標籤；n. 標籤  請在箱子搬到儲藏室之前，清楚貼上標籤。
cabinet  All personnel files are kept in a locked cabinet in the records room.  n. 檔案櫃；櫥櫃  所有人事檔案都存放在檔案室的上鎖櫃子裡。
desk  Each employee will receive a new desk and chair after the renovation.  n. 辦公桌  裝修後，每位員工都會拿到新的辦公桌和椅子。
cubicle  Her cubicle is located next to the window on the second floor.  n. 隔間辦公位  她的隔間位於二樓靠窗的位置。
workstation  Every workstation is equipped with a computer and a phone.  n. 工作站；工作區  每個工作站都配有電腦和電話。
furniture  The new office furniture will be delivered on Tuesday morning.  n. 家具  新的辦公家具將於週二早上送達。
ergonomic  The company bought ergonomic chairs to reduce back pain among employees.  adj. 人體工學的  公司購買了人體工學椅，以減輕員工的背痛。
layout  The new layout makes it easier for teams to work together.  n. 配置；格局  新的配置讓團隊更容易合作。
renovate  The company will renovate the lobby and install a new reception desk.  v. 整修；翻新  公司將整修大廳並安裝新的接待櫃檯。
floor  The accounting department is on the fourth floor of the main building.  n. 樓層  會計部門位於主棟大樓的四樓。
lobby  Visitors should wait in the lobby until their host arrives.  n. 大廳  訪客請在大廳等候，直到接待人員到來。
reception  Please leave your package at reception and someone will collect it.  n. 接待處；櫃檯  請把你的包裹留在櫃檯，會有人來領取。
storage  Extra paper and ink are kept in the storage room next to the kitchen.  n. 儲藏；儲存空間  額外的紙張和墨水存放在廚房旁的儲藏室。
shelf  Place the reference books on the top shelf of the bookcase.  n. 架子；層板  請把參考書放在書櫃最上層的架子上。
inventory  The office manager checks the inventory of supplies at the end of each month.  n. 庫存；存貨清單  辦公室經理在每月底盤點用品庫存。
reorder  Please reorder printer paper when the stock falls below ten boxes.  v. 再訂購；補貨  當庫存低於十箱時，請補訂影印紙。

charge  Please charge your laptop fully before the presentation.  v. 充電  請在簡報前將你的筆電充滿電。
charge  The hotel will charge a small fee for late checkout.  v. 收費；向…收取  飯店將對延遲退房收取少許費用。
charge  There is no charge for delivery within the city limits.  n. 費用；收費  市區範圍內的配送不收取費用。
plug  Plug the power cord into the outlet before turning on the machine.  v. 插上插頭；n. 插頭  開機前，請先把電源線插上插座。
power  The power went out for ten minutes, so all computers shut down.  n. 電力；電源  停電了十分鐘，所以所有電腦都關機了。

hardware  The company will replace outdated hardware during the next fiscal year.  n. 硬體  公司將在下一個會計年度汰換過時的硬體設備。
monitor  Each employee has a large monitor and a wireless keyboard.  n. 螢幕；v. 監控  每位員工都配有一台大螢幕和一支無線鍵盤。
keyboard  The keyboard on this laptop is not responding to my keystrokes.  n. 鍵盤  這台筆電的鍵盤對我的按鍵沒有反應。
laptop  Staff members may borrow a laptop from the IT desk for business trips.  n. 筆記型電腦  員工出差時可向資訊部門櫃檯借用筆記型電腦。
headset  Customer service agents wear a headset while answering calls.  n. 耳機麥克風  客服人員接聽電話時會戴著耳機麥克風。
extension  Please dial extension 305 to reach the facilities department.  n. 分機；延長  請撥分機 305 聯絡設施部門。

intercom  Announcements will be made through the intercom in case of an emergency.  n. 對講機；內部廣播  發生緊急狀況時，將透過內部廣播發布通知。

notice  Customers will notice a difference in quality right away.  v. 注意到；察覺  顧客馬上就會察覺品質上的差異。
bulletin  The latest company bulletin is available on the intranet.  n. 公告；簡報  最新的公司公告已刊登在內部網路上。
operate  Employees must complete training before they operate the new machine.  v. 操作；運作  員工必須先完成訓練，才能操作新機器。
operate  The surgeon will operate on the patient on Monday.  v. 動手術  外科醫師將於週一為這名病患動手術。
handle  Please handle the fragile equipment with care when moving it.  v. 處理；搬運  搬運易碎設備時請小心處理。
user-friendly  The new software is user-friendly, so staff learn it quickly.  adj. 容易使用的  新軟體操作簡單，所以員工很快就學會了。
durable  This durable printer is designed for heavy use in a busy office.  adj. 耐用的  這台耐用的印表機是為繁忙辦公室的大量使用而設計。
compatible  The new printer is compatible with both Windows and Mac computers.  adj. 相容的  這台新印表機與 Windows 和 Mac 電腦都相容。
portable  A portable projector is available for use in small meeting rooms.  adj. 可攜式的；便攜的  小型會議室可使用可攜式投影機。
adapter  You will need an adapter to connect your laptop to the projector.  n. 轉接器  你需要一個轉接器才能把筆電連接到投影機。
whiteboard  Please erase the whiteboard after you finish using the meeting room.  n. 白板  使用完會議室後，請擦乾淨白板。
ink  The printer needs black ink before we can print the reports.  n. 墨水  印表機需要先換上黑色墨水，我們才能列印報告。
tape  Use clear tape to attach the notice to the door of the meeting room.  n. 膠帶  用透明膠帶把公告貼在會議室的門上。
outage  A power outage affected all offices on the second floor this morning.  n. 停電；停機  今天早上停電，影響了二樓所有的辦公室。
reboot  Please reboot your computer if the screen freezes again.  v. 重新開機  如果螢幕再次當機，請將電腦重新開機。
ceiling  Water leaked through the ceiling of the storage room during the storm.  n. 天花板  暴風雨期間，儲藏室的天花板漏水。
partition  A glass partition separates the reception area from the open office.  n. 隔板；隔間  一道玻璃隔間將接待區與開放式辦公區隔開。
restroom  The restroom on the first floor is closed for cleaning until noon.  n. 洗手間  一樓的洗手間因清潔而關閉至中午。
kitchenette  The kitchenette is equipped with a refrigerator and a microwave oven.  n. 小廚房；茶水間  茶水間配有冰箱和微波爐。
calibrate  The technician will calibrate the scanner to improve the image quality.  v. 校準  技術人員將校準掃描器以提升影像品質。
dispenser  The water dispenser in the break room needs a new filter.  n. 飲水機；分配器  休息室的飲水機需要換新濾心。
# ===== 7 行政與文書 =====
document  Please save every signed document in the shared folder before Friday.  n. 文件；v. 以文件記錄  請在週五前將每份簽署好的文件存放到共用資料夾。
file  The assistant will file all invoices by date at the end of each month.  v. 歸檔；n. 檔案  助理會在每個月底依日期將所有發票歸檔。
file  You can open the attached file to see the revised budget.  n. 檔案；文件夾  你可以開啟附加檔案查看修訂後的預算。
form  Please complete the form and return it to the personnel office.  n. 表格；表單  請填寫這份表格並交回人事室。
form  Employees can form a committee to review the new vacation policy.  v. 組成；形成  員工可以組成委員會來審查新的休假政策。
sign  Please sign the contract at the bottom of the last page.  v. 簽署；簽名  請在最後一頁的底部簽署合約。
sign  A large sign at the entrance lists the opening hours.  n. 標誌；告示牌  入口處的大告示牌列出了營業時間。
copy  Please make a copy of the agenda before the meeting begins.  n. 影本；複本；v. 影印  請在會議開始前影印一份議程。

signature  A manager's signature is required before the payment can be processed.  n. 簽名；簽章  付款需先取得經理的簽名才能處理。
original  Please keep the original and send only a copy to the client.  n. 原件；adj. 原始的  請保留原件，只將影本寄給客戶。
draft  The legal team will send the first draft of the contract tomorrow.  n. 草稿；v. 起草  法務團隊明天會寄出合約的初稿。
revise  Please revise the schedule and send the updated version to everyone.  v. 修訂；修改  請修改時程表，並將更新版寄給所有人。

template  Use the company template when you write a new proposal.  n. 範本；樣板  撰寫新提案時請使用公司的範本。
paperwork  The new employee spent the morning completing paperwork in the human resources office.  n. 文書作業；文件  新進員工整個早上都在人資辦公室處理文書作業。
supplies  Office supplies are kept in the cabinet next to the printer.  n. 用品；補給品  辦公用品存放在印表機旁的櫃子裡。
mail  The receptionist will mail the package to the customer this afternoon.  v. 郵寄；n. 郵件  櫃檯人員今天下午會將包裹寄給客戶。
deliver  The courier will deliver the signed contract to your office by noon.  v. 遞送；交付  快遞員會在中午前將簽署好的合約送到你的辦公室。
courier  We sent the documents by courier to make sure they arrived on time.  n. 快遞員；快遞  我們用快遞寄送文件，以確保準時送達。
parcel  A large parcel arrived for you at the front desk this morning.  n. 包裹  今天早上有一個大包裹送到櫃檯給你。
package  The package must be signed for when it arrives at the office.  n. 包裹；套裝  包裹送達辦公室時必須簽收。
filing  The filing cabinet in the corner holds all of last year's tax records.  n. 歸檔；adj. 歸檔用的  角落的檔案櫃存放著去年所有的稅務紀錄。
memo  The director sent a memo about the new parking rules yesterday.  n. 備忘錄  主任昨天發出了一份有關新停車規定的備忘錄。
request  A request for new equipment must be signed by your supervisor.  n. 申請；請求；v. 請求  新設備的申請必須由你的主管簽名。

print  Please print two copies of the report for tomorrow's meeting.  v. 列印  請為明天的會議列印兩份報告。
duplicate  Please do not send a duplicate of the form that was already submitted.  n. 副本；adj. 重複的  請不要再寄送已提交表格的副本。
stamp  The clerk will stamp the form once all the information is checked.  v. 蓋章；n. 印章；郵票  所有資料核對完畢後，職員會在表格上蓋章。
seal  Please seal the envelope and place it in the outgoing mail tray.  v. 封緘；n. 封條；印章  請將信封封好，放進待寄郵件的文件盤。
initial  Please initial each page of the contract to show you have read it.  v. 簽姓名縮寫；adj. 最初的  請在合約每一頁簽上姓名縮寫，表示你已閱讀過。
witness  A witness must sign the document to confirm the date.  n. 見證人；v. 見證  見證人必須在文件上簽名以確認日期。
clause  The contract contains a clause about early termination.  n. 條款  這份合約包含一項有關提前終止的條款。
agreement  Both parties read the agreement carefully before they signed it.  n. 協議；合約  雙方在簽署前都仔細閱讀了這份協議。
amend  The board voted to amend the contract after reviewing the legal advice.  v. 修訂；修正  董事會在審閱法律意見後投票決定修訂合約。
amendment  An amendment to the lease was signed by both parties last week.  n. 修訂；修正案  租約的修正案已於上週由雙方簽署。
revision  The latest revision of the manual includes new safety instructions.  n. 修訂版；修改  手冊的最新修訂版包含新的安全說明。
version  Please make sure you are using the latest version of the form.  n. 版本  請確認你使用的是表格的最新版本。
organize  Please organize the documents by date and place them in the cabinet.  v. 整理；組織  請將文件依日期整理好，放進櫃子裡。
index  The index at the back of the manual lists every procedure alphabetically.  n. 索引；v. 編索引  手冊後面的索引依字母順序列出每項程序。
retain  Companies must retain tax records for at least seven years.  v. 保留；保存  公司必須將稅務紀錄至少保存七年。
retrieve  The system can retrieve any file within a few seconds.  v. 檢索；取回  這個系統能在幾秒內檢索出任何檔案。
store  All personnel files are stored in a locked room on the first floor.  v. 儲存；保管  所有人事檔案都存放在一樓一間上鎖的房間。
shred  Please shred all confidential documents before you throw them away.  v. 銷毀；用碎紙機絞碎  丟棄所有機密文件前，請先用碎紙機銷毀。
dispose  Old records should be disposed of in accordance with company policy.  v. 處理；丟棄  舊紀錄應依公司規定處理。
confidential  This report is confidential and must not be shared outside the department.  adj. 機密的  這份報告屬於機密，不得在部門外分享。
policy  The company policy requires two signatures on every large purchase.  n. 政策；規定  公司規定每一筆大額採購都需要兩個簽名。
procedure  Follow the procedure in the manual to request new office equipment.  n. 程序；手續  請依手冊中的程序申請新的辦公設備。
regulation  Safety regulations require all visitors to wear a badge in the building.  n. 規定；法規  安全規定要求所有訪客在大樓內配戴識別證。

documentation  Please provide the necessary documentation to support your travel claim.  n. 文件資料；證明文件  請提供必要的證明文件來佐證你的差旅費申報。

clerical  She was hired to handle clerical work in the accounting department.  adj. 文書的；事務性的  她受聘負責會計部門的文書工作。

bureaucracy  Too much bureaucracy slowed down the approval process for the new project.  n. 官僚作風；繁瑣手續  過多的繁瑣手續拖慢了新專案的核准流程。
permit  You need a permit to park in the company lot.  n. 許可證；v. 允許  你需要許可證才能把車停在公司停車場。
legible  Please write in a legible hand so the staff can read the form.  adj. 清晰可讀的  請把字寫清楚，讓員工能看懂這份表格。
accurate  Accurate records are essential for passing the annual inspection.  adj. 準確的；正確的  準確的紀錄是通過年度檢查的關鍵。
proofread  Please proofread the letter carefully before you mail it to the client.  v. 校對  寄給客戶前，請仔細校對這封信。
notarize  The lawyer will notarize the agreement before it is filed.  v. 公證  律師會在協議歸檔前予以公證。
validate  Please validate the form with a manager's signature before filing.  v. 驗證；使生效  歸檔前，請用經理的簽名讓這份表格生效。
countersign  A second manager must countersign every contract over ten thousand dollars.  v. 副署；會簽  每份超過一萬元的合約都必須由第二位經理會簽。
clipboard  Please attach the sign-in sheet to the clipboard at the front desk.  n. 寫字板；剪貼簿  請把簽到表夾在櫃檯的寫字板上。
notepad  She keeps a notepad beside the phone to record each message.  n. 便條本；記事本  她在電話旁放了一本便條本，用來記錄每則留言。
highlighter  Use a yellow highlighter to mark the key figures in the report.  n. 螢光筆  用黃色螢光筆標出報告中的關鍵數字。
photocopier  The photocopier on the second floor is out of toner again.  n. 影印機  二樓的影印機又沒有碳粉了。
paperclip  Fasten the receipt to the invoice with a paperclip.  n. 迴紋針  用迴紋針把收據固定在發票上。
logbook  Visitors must record their arrival time in the logbook at reception.  n. 登記簿；日誌  訪客必須在接待處的登記簿上記錄到達時間。
notary  The contract must be signed in front of a notary to be valid.  n. 公證人  這份合約必須在公證人面前簽署才有效。
affix  Please affix the company seal to the bottom of each page.  v. 貼上；蓋上  請在每一頁的底部蓋上公司印章。
memorandum  A memorandum of understanding was signed by both companies last week.  n. 備忘錄  兩家公司已於上週簽署了合作備忘錄。
dossier  The assistant prepared a dossier on each candidate before the interviews.  n. 檔案卷宗；資料袋  助理在面試前為每位應徵者準備了一份檔案卷宗。
docket  The court clerk updated the docket with the new hearing dates.  n. 案件表；待辦事項表  法院書記官在案件表中更新了新的開庭日期。
paperless  Our office is going paperless to reduce printing costs and waste.  adj. 無紙化的  我們辦公室正朝無紙化邁進，以降低列印成本並減少浪費。
digitize  We plan to digitize all customer records by the end of the year.  v. 數位化  我們計畫在年底前將所有客戶紀錄數位化。
administer  Ms. Lee will administer the new employee benefits program.  v. 管理；執行  李女士將負責執行新的員工福利方案。
tray  Please place the completed forms in the tray on my desk.  n. 文件盤；托盤  請把填好的表格放在我桌上的文件盤裡。
drawer  The spare keys are kept in the top drawer of the filing cabinet.  n. 抽屜  備用鑰匙放在檔案櫃最上層的抽屜裡。
organizer  A desk organizer can help you keep your workspace neat.  n. 整理盒；籌辦人  桌上整理盒能幫你保持工作空間整齊。
planner  I wrote the deadline in my planner so I would not forget it.  n. 行事曆本；規劃者  我把截止日期寫在行事曆本上，這樣就不會忘記。
calendar  Please check the shared calendar before scheduling a team meeting.  n. 日曆；行事曆  安排團隊會議前，請先查看共用行事曆。
diary  The manager keeps a diary of all client visits for the year.  n. 日誌；記事簿  經理把全年所有的客戶拜訪都記錄在日誌裡。
roster  The duty roster for next month will be posted on Friday.  n. 值班表；名冊  下個月的值班表將於週五公布。
directory  The staff directory lists every employee's extension number.  n. 通訊錄；目錄  員工通訊錄列出了每位員工的分機號碼。
dictate  The director will dictate several letters to her assistant this afternoon.  v. 口述；聽寫  主任今天下午會向她的助理口述幾封信。
transcribe  Could you transcribe the recording of yesterday's meeting by noon?  v. 謄寫；轉錄  你能在中午前把昨天會議的錄音謄寫出來嗎？
typo  There is a typo in the second paragraph of the contract.  n. 打字錯誤  合約的第二段有一處打字錯誤。
formatting  Consistent formatting makes the long report easier to read.  n. 格式設定；排版  一致的排版讓這份冗長的報告更容易閱讀。
authorization  You need written authorization from your supervisor to access these files.  n. 授權；核准  你需要主管的書面授權才能存取這些檔案。
retrieval  Fast retrieval of old records saves the department a lot of time.  n. 檢索；取回  快速調閱舊紀錄為部門節省了許多時間。
clutter  Too much clutter on the desk can reduce productivity.  n. 雜亂；v. 使雜亂  桌面過於雜亂會降低工作效率。
tidy  Please tidy the supply room before the inspection on Friday.  v. 整理；adj. 整齊的  請在週五檢查前整理好用品室。
attest  The signatures attest that all copies are true and complete.  v. 證明；作證  這些簽名證明所有副本都真實且完整。
enclosure  See the enclosure for a complete list of attendees.  n. 附件；隨函附寄物  完整的出席者名單請見附件。
letterhead  All official letters must be printed on company letterhead.  n. 公司信紙；信頭  所有正式信函都必須印在公司信紙上。
collate  Please collate the handouts into packets before the meeting.  v. 整理；依序排列  請在會議前將講義依序整理成一份份資料袋。
errand  The new intern runs errands such as mailing packages and picking up supplies.  n. 跑腿；差事  新來的實習生負責跑腿，例如寄包裹和領取用品。
bookmark  Bookmark the intranet page so you can find the forms quickly.  v. 加入書籤；n. 書籤  請將內部網路頁面加入書籤，這樣就能快速找到表格。
# ===== 8 郵件與通訊 =====
message  I left a message on your desk about tomorrow's meeting.  n. 訊息；留言  我在你桌上留了一則有關明天會議的留言。
reply  Please reply to this email by the end of the day.  v. 回覆；n. 回覆  請在今天下班前回覆這封電子郵件。
respond  The customer service team will respond to your question within two days.  v. 回應；答覆  客服團隊會在兩天內回覆你的問題。
response  We have not received a response from the supplier yet.  n. 回應；答覆  我們還沒有收到供應商的回覆。
forward  Please forward the itinerary to everyone on the sales team.  v. 轉寄；轉交  請將行程表轉寄給業務團隊的所有人。
forward  The project is moving forward on schedule.  adv. 向前；往前  這項專案正依照進度向前推進。
attachment  The attachment contains the updated schedule for next week.  n. 附件  附件包含下週更新後的時程表。
notify  We will notify you by email when your order has shipped.  v. 通知  你的訂單出貨時，我們會以電子郵件通知你。
notification  You will receive a notification when your payment is processed.  n. 通知；通報  你的付款處理完成時會收到通知。
inquiry  We received an inquiry about the new product line this morning.  n. 詢問；查詢  我們今天早上收到一則有關新產品系列的詢問。
inform  Please inform the front desk if you will be late.  v. 通知；告知  如果你會遲到，請通知櫃檯。
contact  You can contact our customer service team by phone or email.  v. 聯絡；n. 聯絡人；聯繫  你可以透過電話或電子郵件聯絡我們的客服團隊。
call  Please call the client back before the end of the day.  v. 打電話；n. 來電  請在今天下班前回電給客戶。
call  The conference call will begin at nine o'clock sharp.  n. 電話會議；通話  電話會議將在九點整開始。
voicemail  I left a voicemail asking him to call me back this afternoon.  n. 語音信箱；語音留言  我留了語音留言，請他今天下午回電給我。
dial  Dial nine first if you want to make an outside call.  v. 撥號  如果要打外線，請先撥九。
hold  Please hold while I connect you to the manager.  v. 保持通話等候；持有  請稍候，我幫你轉接給經理。
hold  The company will hold its annual meeting in June.  v. 舉行；召開  公司將於六月舉行年度大會。
operator  The operator connected me to the human resources department.  n. 總機人員；操作員  總機人員幫我轉接到人力資源部門。
recipient  The email was sent to the wrong recipient by mistake.  n. 收件人；接收者  這封電子郵件被誤寄給錯誤的收件人。
sender  Please reply directly to the sender of this message.  n. 寄件者；發送者  請直接回覆這則訊息的寄件者。
subject  Please write a clear subject line so the email gets read quickly.  n. 主旨；主題  請寫上清楚的主旨，讓郵件能被快速閱讀。
subject  All prices are subject to change without notice.  adj. 受…影響的；取決於  所有價格若有變動，恕不另行通知。
inbox  My inbox was full of unread messages after the holiday.  n. 收件匣  假期過後，我的收件匣塞滿了未讀訊息。
delete  Please delete the old messages to free up space in your mailbox.  v. 刪除  請刪除舊訊息，以釋出信箱空間。
subscribe  Customers can subscribe to our newsletter on the company website.  v. 訂閱  顧客可以在公司網站上訂閱我們的電子報。
newsletter  The monthly newsletter includes news about new products and staff changes.  n. 電子報；通訊  每月電子報包含新產品和人事異動的消息。
announcement  The announcement about the merger was sent to all employees today.  n. 公告；宣布  有關合併的公告今天已發送給所有員工。
broadcast  The speech will be broadcast live on the company website.  v. 播送；n. 廣播  這場演講將在公司網站上直播。
correspondence  All correspondence with clients should be saved in the shared folder.  n. 往來信件；通信  所有與客戶的往來信件都應存放在共用資料夾。
communicate  Managers should communicate changes to the team as early as possible.  v. 溝通；傳達  主管應盡早向團隊傳達變動事項。
communication  Clear communication between departments helps us avoid mistakes.  n. 溝通；通訊  部門之間清楚的溝通有助於我們避免錯誤。
mention  Please do not mention the price in your email to the client.  v. 提及；n. 提到  請不要在寄給客戶的電子郵件中提到價格。
remind  Please remind the staff about the safety training on Monday.  v. 提醒  請提醒員工週一有安全訓練。
reminder  This is a reminder that your subscription expires next week.  n. 提醒；提醒通知  提醒您，您的訂閱將於下週到期。
urgent  I sent an urgent message to the supplier this morning.  adj. 緊急的  我今天早上傳了一則緊急訊息給供應商。
acknowledge  Please acknowledge receipt of this email by replying today.  v. 確認收到；承認  請於今天回信，確認已收到這封電子郵件。
confirmation  A confirmation email will be sent once your booking is complete.  n. 確認；確認函  預訂完成後，我們會寄出確認郵件。
convey  Please convey our thanks to your team for their quick response.  v. 傳達；表達  請向你的團隊轉達我們對他們迅速回應的謝意。
relay  Please relay this message to the night shift supervisor.  v. 轉達；傳遞  請將這則訊息轉達給夜班主管。
return  I will return your call as soon as the meeting ends.  v. 回電；歸還  會議一結束我就會回電給你。
return  The return on this investment is about five percent.  n. 報酬；收益  這項投資的報酬率約為百分之五。
reach  You can reach me on my mobile phone after six.  v. 聯絡到；到達  六點以後你可以打我的手機找到我。
available  Ms. Lee is not available right now, but she will call you back.  adj. 有空的；可取得的  李女士現在沒空，但她會回電給你。
voice  The caller left a voice message about the delayed shipment.  n. 聲音；語音  來電者留下了一則有關出貨延遲的語音留言。
signal  I lost the signal in the elevator, so the call dropped.  n. 訊號；v. 示意  我在電梯裡收不到訊號，所以通話中斷了。
connection  I could not join the meeting because of a poor connection.  n. 連線；連接  由於連線品質不佳，我無法加入會議。
mobile  Please send me a text message on my mobile phone.  adj. 行動的；n. 手機  請傳簡訊到我的手機。
hotline  Customers can call the hotline for help at any time.  n. 專線；服務熱線  顧客隨時都可以撥打服務專線尋求協助。
comment  Please leave a comment below the post if you have any questions.  n. 意見；評論；v. 評論  如果有任何問題，請在貼文下方留言。
survey  Please complete the short survey and send it back by Friday.  n. 問卷調查；v. 調查  請填寫這份簡短的問卷，並於週五前寄回。
query  Please send any query about your account to the support team.  n. 疑問；查詢  有關帳戶的任何疑問，請寄給客服團隊。
invitation  The invitation was sent by email two weeks before the event.  n. 邀請函；邀請  邀請函在活動前兩週以電子郵件寄出。
greeting  The email began with a friendly greeting from the director.  n. 問候；招呼  這封電子郵件以主任親切的問候開頭。
regards  Please give my regards to your colleagues in the Paris office.  n. 問候；致意  請代我向巴黎辦公室的同事問好。
sincerely  We sincerely hope that this solution meets your needs.  adv. 誠摯地  我們誠摯地希望這個解決方案能符合您的需求。
apologize  We apologize for the delay in answering your email.  v. 道歉  我們為延遲回覆您的電子郵件致歉。
apology  Please accept our apology for the error on your invoice.  n. 道歉  您的發票有誤，還請接受我們的歉意。
appreciate  We appreciate your quick reply to our inquiry.  v. 感謝；欣賞  感謝您迅速回覆我們的詢問。
appreciate  We appreciate your prompt reply to our inquiry.  v. 感激；感謝  感謝您對我們的詢問做出及時回覆。
appreciate  The value of the property is expected to appreciate over time.  v. 升值；增值  這項不動產的價值預期會隨時間增加。
inconvenience  We apologize for any inconvenience caused by the phone outage.  n. 不便；麻煩  電話中斷造成的任何不便，我們深感抱歉。
alert  An alert was sent to all staff about the system maintenance.  n. 警報；警示通知；v. 向…示警  系統維護的警示通知已發給全體員工。
brief  The manager sent a brief message to confirm the meeting time.  adj. 簡短的；v. 向某人簡報  經理傳了一則簡短的訊息來確認會議時間。
concise  Please keep your message concise so the readers can respond quickly.  adj. 簡明的；簡潔的  請讓您的訊息保持簡潔，讀者才能快速回應。
prompt  Thank you for your prompt reply to my message.  adj. 迅速的；v. 促使  謝謝您迅速回覆我的訊息。
timely  A timely reply will help us keep the project on schedule.  adj. 及時的；適時的  及時的回覆有助於我們讓專案按進度進行。
spam  The filter blocks most spam before it reaches your inbox.  n. 垃圾郵件  過濾器會在大部分垃圾郵件進入收件匣之前將其攔截。
account  Your email account will be closed if you do not use it.  n. 帳號；帳戶  您的電子郵件帳號若長期不使用將會被關閉。
account  Please account for the missing funds in your report.  v. 解釋；說明原因  請在報告中說明短缺款項的原因。
account  The new product accounts for forty percent of our sales.  v. 占（比例）  這項新產品占了我們銷售額的百分之四十。
server  The email server was down for maintenance last night.  n. 伺服器  電子郵件伺服器昨晚因維護而停機。
website  Details are posted on our website and in the newsletter.  n. 網站  詳細資訊刊登在我們的網站與電子報上。
online  You can chat with a representative online during business hours.  adj. 線上的；adv. 在線上  營業時間內，您可以在線上與客服人員聊天。
download  You can download the attachment from the link in this email.  v. 下載；n. 下載檔案  您可以從這封郵件中的連結下載附件。
upload  Please upload the signed form to the secure website.  v. 上傳  請將簽署好的表單上傳到安全網站。
link  Click the link below to confirm your email address.  n. 連結；v. 連結  請點選下方連結以確認您的電子郵件地址。
browse  Customers can browse the catalog on our website at any time.  v. 瀏覽  顧客隨時都可以在我們的網站上瀏覽型錄。
post  The company will post the announcement on its website tomorrow.  v. 張貼；發布  公司明天會在網站上發布這則公告。
