media  The press release was sent to the local media this morning.  n. 媒體  新聞稿今天早上已寄給當地媒體。
press  Members of the press were invited to the product launch on Monday.  n. 新聞界；媒體  新聞媒體受邀參加週一的產品發表會。
release  The firm will release a statement about the delay tomorrow.  v. 發布；n. 發布；釋出  公司明天將發布一份關於延誤的聲明。
publicity  The new store received a lot of publicity in the local newspaper.  n. 宣傳；曝光  這家新店在當地報紙上獲得大量曝光。
interpreter  An interpreter will join the call to help with the translation.  n. 口譯員  通話中會有一位口譯員加入協助翻譯。
translate  Please translate the email into Spanish before you send it.  v. 翻譯  寄出之前，請先把這封電子郵件翻譯成西班牙文。
translation  The translation of the contract was completed on Tuesday.  n. 翻譯；譯文  合約的翻譯工作已於週二完成。
correspond  Please correspond with our office by email rather than by phone.  v. 通信；相符  請以電子郵件而非電話與我們辦公室聯繫。
disclose  Staff may not disclose client information by phone or email.  v. 透露；公開  員工不得透過電話或電子郵件透露客戶資料。
dispatch  We will dispatch a technician as soon as we receive your message.  v. 派遣；發送  一收到您的訊息，我們就會派技術人員前往。
intranet  The new policy is posted on the company intranet.  n. 內部網路  新政策已公布在公司內部網路上。
unsubscribe  Click the link below if you wish to unsubscribe from our mailing list.  v. 取消訂閱  若您想取消訂閱我們的郵寄名單，請點選下方連結。
mailbox  Your mailbox is almost full, so please delete old messages.  n. 信箱；郵筒  您的信箱幾乎滿了，請刪除舊的郵件。
ping  I will ping you on the chat app when the file is ready.  v. 傳訊提醒；n. 提醒訊息  檔案準備好時，我會在聊天軟體上傳訊息通知你。
salutation  Begin the email with a polite salutation such as Dear Ms. Chen.  n. 稱呼語；問候語  電子郵件請以禮貌的稱呼開頭，例如「陳女士您好」。
telephone  The telephone rang several times before the receptionist answered.  n. 電話  電話響了好幾聲，接待人員才接聽。
texting  Texting during client presentations is considered rude.  n. 傳簡訊  在客戶簡報時傳簡訊被視為不禮貌。
postage  Postage for international parcels has increased this year.  n. 郵資  今年國際包裹的郵資調漲了。
postal  Please include your postal code when you fill out the form.  adj. 郵政的；郵遞的  填寫表單時，請附上您的郵遞區號。
smartphone  You can scan the code with your smartphone to get your ticket.  n. 智慧型手機  您可以用智慧型手機掃描條碼來取得票券。
videoconference  We will hold a videoconference with the Tokyo office on Thursday.  n. 視訊會議  我們週四將與東京辦公室召開視訊會議。
caller  The caller asked to speak with someone in the billing department.  n. 來電者  來電者要求與帳務部門的人員通話。
callback  A representative will give you a callback within one business day.  n. 回電  客服人員會在一個工作天內回電給您。
hyperlink  Click the hyperlink in the message to confirm your registration.  n. 超連結  請點選訊息中的超連結以確認您的註冊。
acknowledgment  Please send an acknowledgment as soon as you receive the package.  n. 確認回覆；收據  一收到包裹，請立即寄出確認回覆。
wording  The wording of the announcement was clear and polite.  n. 措辭；用語  這則公告的措辭清楚又有禮貌。
digest  Subscribers receive a weekly digest of the most important industry news.  n. 摘要；精選彙編  訂閱者每週會收到最重要產業新聞的精選摘要。
mailer  The store sent a mailer announcing its summer sale to all members.  n. 廣告郵件；郵寄傳單  商店寄給所有會員一份宣傳夏季特賣的廣告郵件。
cellphone  Cellphone use is not allowed inside the laboratory.  n. 手機  實驗室內禁止使用手機。
messaging  The messaging system allows teams to share files instantly.  n. 訊息傳遞；通訊  這套訊息系統讓團隊能即時分享檔案。
# ===== 9 財務與會計 =====
budget  The marketing budget was approved by the board on Monday.  n. 預算；v. 編列預算  行銷預算已於週一獲董事會核准。
revenue  Annual revenue grew by eight percent compared with last year.  n. 營收；收入  年度營收比去年成長了百分之八。
profit  The company made a profit of two million dollars last quarter.  n. 利潤；v. 獲利  公司上一季獲利兩百萬美元。
cost  The cost of raw materials rose sharply this year.  n. 成本；v. 花費  今年原物料成本大幅上漲。
cost  The new equipment will cost about ten thousand dollars.  v. 花費；使付出代價  這批新設備大約要花一萬美元。
invoice  Please pay the invoice within thirty days of receiving it.  n. 發票；請款單；v. 開發票  請在收到請款單後三十天內付款。
accounting  The accounting department will close the books on the last day of the month.  n. 會計；會計學  會計部門將在月底最後一天結帳。
accountant  The accountant prepared the tax report for the whole company.  n. 會計師  會計師為整間公司編製了稅務報告。
audit  An outside firm will audit the company's accounts in March.  v. 查帳；n. 審計  一家外部事務所將在三月查核公司的帳目。
statement  The monthly statement shows all payments made to your account.  n. 對帳單；報表；聲明  月結對帳單列出了存入您帳戶的所有款項。
balance  Please check the balance in your account before making the payment.  n. 餘額；v. 使平衡  付款前請先確認帳戶餘額。
ledger  All transactions are recorded in the general ledger.  n. 帳簿；分類帳  所有交易都記錄在總分類帳中。
asset  The building is the largest asset on the company's balance sheet.  n. 資產  這棟大樓是公司資產負債表上最大的資產。
liability  The firm has a large liability because of its unpaid loans.  n. 負債；責任  由於貸款尚未還清，這家公司背負了龐大負債。
debt  The company paid off its debt ahead of schedule.  n. 債務  公司提前還清了債務。
loan  The bank approved a loan of fifty thousand dollars for the new store.  n. 貸款；v. 借出  銀行核准了新店五萬美元的貸款。
interest  The bank charges interest on any unpaid balance.  n. 利息；興趣  銀行會對任何未付餘額收取利息。
refund  Customers may request a refund within fourteen days of purchase.  n. 退款；v. 退還款項  顧客可在購買後十四天內申請退款。
margin  A lower price will reduce our profit margin on this product.  n. 利潤率；邊際  降價會壓縮我們這項產品的利潤率。
fiscal  The fiscal year ends on the thirty-first of March.  adj. 會計年度的；財政的  會計年度於三月三十一日結束。
quarter  Sales rose sharply in the first quarter of this year.  n. 季度；四分之一  今年第一季的銷售額大幅成長。
deficit  The company faced a deficit after its overseas expansion.  n. 赤字；虧損  公司在海外擴張後面臨赤字。
surplus  The firm had a surplus of cash at the end of the year.  n. 盈餘；剩餘  這家公司年底時有現金盈餘。
capital  The startup needs more capital to open a second factory.  n. 資本；資金  這家新創公司需要更多資金來開設第二座工廠。
investment  The investment in new software will pay for itself within two years.  n. 投資  對新軟體的投資將在兩年內回本。
invest  The company plans to invest in new equipment next year.  v. 投資  公司計畫明年投資購置新設備。
investor  Investors were pleased with the strong quarterly results.  n. 投資人  投資人對強勁的季度業績感到滿意。
dividend  Shareholders will receive a dividend of two dollars per share.  n. 股息；紅利  股東每股將領到兩美元的股息。
share  Investors bought shares in the firm before its market debut.  n. 股份；v. 分享  投資人在公司上市前買進了它的股份。
share  Staff on the third floor share a large office.  v. 共用；分享  三樓的員工共用一間大辦公室。
bond  The city sold bonds to pay for the new bridge.  n. 債券；v. 建立關係  市政府發行債券來支付新橋的費用。
fund  The fund was set up to pay for employee training.  n. 基金；v. 提供資金  這項基金是為支付員工訓練費用而設立的。
finance  The finance team will present the budget at the board meeting.  n. 財務；金融；v. 融資  財務團隊將在董事會上報告預算。
financial  The financial report shows a steady rise in profit.  adj. 財務的；金融的  財務報告顯示利潤穩定成長。
cash  The store accepts cash and all major credit cards.  n. 現金  這家店接受現金與各大信用卡。
payment  Payment is due within thirty days of the invoice date.  n. 付款；款項  款項須於發票日期起三十天內支付。
transaction  Each transaction is recorded in the ledger on the same day.  n. 交易  每筆交易都會在當天記入帳簿。
overdraft  The bank charged a fee for the overdraft on her account.  n. 透支  銀行就她帳戶的透支收取了一筆費用。
credit  You can buy the goods on credit and pay next month.  n. 賒帳；信用；信貸  您可以賒帳購買這些商品，下個月再付款。
credit  The company will credit your account with the full amount.  v. 將款項記入帳戶  公司會將全額款項記入您的帳戶。
debit  The amount will be taken from your account by direct debit.  n. 借方；扣款  這筆金額將以直接扣款方式從您的帳戶扣除。
receipt  Please keep the receipt as proof of payment.  n. 收據  請保留收據作為付款證明。
bill  The electricity bill is higher than usual this month.  n. 帳單；v. 開帳單  這個月的電費帳單比平常高。
bill  The new bill was passed by the legislature last week.  n. 法案  這項新法案上週在立法機關通過。
billing  The billing department will correct the error on your invoice.  n. 計費；開立帳單  帳務部門會更正您發票上的錯誤。
fee  A small fee is charged for each international transfer.  n. 費用；手續費  每筆國際轉帳都會收取少許手續費。
price  The price of fuel rose again this month.  n. 價格  燃料價格這個月又上漲了。
rebate  You can receive a rebate when you buy this appliance.  n. 退款；回扣  購買這款家電可獲得退款。
grant  The university received a research grant from the government.  n. 補助金；v. 授予  這所大學獲得了政府的研究補助金。
grant  The manager agreed to grant the employee two extra days off.  v. 同意；准予  經理同意多給這名員工兩天假。
allocate  The manager will allocate more funds to the marketing team.  v. 分配；撥款  經理將撥更多經費給行銷團隊。
estimate  The contractor gave an estimate of ten thousand dollars for the repairs.  n. 估價；v. 估計  承包商開出維修費用一萬美元的估價。
quote  The supplier sent a quote for two hundred chairs.  n. 報價；v. 報價；引用  供應商寄來了兩百張椅子的報價。
overhead  Reducing overhead is the key to improving our profit.  n. 管理費用；經常開支  降低管理費用是提高我們利潤的關鍵。
depreciate  New cars depreciate quickly in the first two years.  v. 貶值；折舊  新車在前兩年會快速貶值。
reconcile  The accountant must reconcile the bank statement with the ledger.  v. 核對；調節  會計師必須將銀行對帳單與帳簿核對。
expenditure  Total expenditure on research rose by ten percent last year.  n. 支出；經費  去年的研究總支出增加了百分之十。
savings  The new system led to substantial savings in energy costs.  n. 節省；存款  新系統大幅節省了能源成本。
afford  We cannot afford to hire more staff this year.  v. 負擔得起  我們今年負擔不起再多僱用人手。
affordable  The company offers affordable housing to new employees.  adj. 負擔得起的；平價的  公司為新進員工提供負擔得起的住房。
frugal  The firm was frugal with its spending during the recession.  adj. 節儉的  這家公司在經濟衰退期間支出十分節儉。
profitable  The new branch became profitable within six months.  adj. 有利潤的；獲利的  這間新分公司在六個月內就開始獲利。
solvent  The company remained solvent despite the economic downturn.  adj. 有償債能力的  儘管經濟低迷，這家公司仍保有償債能力。
bankrupt  The retailer went bankrupt after losing its largest customer.  adj. 破產的；v. 使破產  這家零售商在失去最大客戶後破產了。
bankruptcy  The firm filed for bankruptcy after years of heavy losses.  n. 破產  這家公司在多年嚴重虧損後申請破產。
liquidity  Low liquidity made it hard for the firm to pay its bills.  n. 流動性；變現能力  流動性不足使這家公司難以支付帳款。
inflation  Inflation has pushed up the cost of raw materials this year.  n. 通貨膨脹  通貨膨脹使今年的原物料成本上升。
currency  The hotel accepts payment in more than one currency.  n. 貨幣；通貨  這間飯店接受多種貨幣付款。
exchange  The exchange rate changes every day, so check it before you buy.  n. 兌換；匯兌；v. 交換  匯率每天都在變動，購買前請先確認。
broker  The broker will buy the shares on your behalf.  n. 經紀人；仲介  經紀人會代您買進這些股票。
royalty  The author receives a royalty on every copy sold.  n. 版稅；權利金  作者每售出一本都能獲得版稅。
mortgage  They took out a mortgage to buy the office building.  n. 抵押貸款  他們貸了一筆抵押貸款來購買這棟辦公大樓。
collateral  The bank asked for collateral before it approved the loan.  n. 抵押品；擔保品  銀行在核准貸款前要求提供擔保品。
lender  The lender asked for proof of income before approving the loan.  n. 貸方；放款人  放款人在核准貸款前要求提供收入證明。
creditor  The company paid its creditors before the end of the year.  n. 債權人  公司在年底前還清了債權人的款項。
debtor  The debtor agreed to repay the balance in monthly installments.  n. 債務人  債務人同意以每月分期方式償還餘額。
overdue  Your invoice is overdue, so please pay it as soon as possible.  adj. 逾期的；過期未付的  您的發票已逾期，請儘快付款。
installment  You can pay for the equipment in six monthly installments.  n. 分期付款；一期款項  您可以分六個月分期支付這批設備的費用。
penalty  A penalty will be added if you pay after the due date.  n. 罰款；處罰  若在到期日之後付款，將加收罰款。
fraud  The audit uncovered fraud in the sales department.  n. 詐欺；舞弊  這次查帳發現銷售部門有舞弊情事。
embezzle  A former clerk was caught trying to embezzle company funds.  v. 盜用；挪用公款  一名前職員因企圖挪用公司資金而被抓到。
bookkeeper  The bookkeeper records every payment in the company's accounts.  n. 記帳員  記帳員會把每筆付款記錄在公司帳目中。
accrual  Accrual accounting records income when it is earned, not when it is received.  n. 應計；權責發生制  權責發生制會計在收入賺得時入帳，而非收到款項時。
amortize  The firm will amortize the loan over ten years.  v. 分期攤還；攤銷  公司將在十年內分期攤還這筆貸款。
depreciation  Depreciation reduces the recorded value of equipment each year.  n. 折舊  折舊使設備的帳面價值每年減少。
liabilities  The company's total liabilities exceeded its assets last quarter.  n. 負債  公司上一季的總負債超過了資產。
solvency  Investors worry about the firm's long-term solvency.  n. 償債能力  投資人擔心這家公司的長期償債能力。
insolvent  The retailer was declared insolvent after losing its largest customer.  adj. 無力償債的  這家零售商在失去最大客戶後被宣告無力償債。
capitalize  Some costs can be capitalized instead of being treated as immediate expenses.  v. 資本化；以資本投入  有些成本可以資本化，而不必當作當期費用處理。
stockholder  Each stockholder received a copy of the annual report.  n. 股東  每位股東都收到一份年報。
reconciliation  Monthly bank reconciliation helps us detect errors in the ledger.  n. 對帳；調節  每月的銀行對帳有助於我們發現帳簿中的錯誤。
appreciation  The appreciation of the property increased the firm's net worth.  n. 增值；升值  不動產的增值提高了公司的淨值。
taxation  Changes in taxation will affect small business owners.  n. 課稅；稅制  稅制的變動將影響小型企業主。
write-off  The unpaid invoice was treated as a write-off at year end.  n. 沖銷；註銷  這張未付的發票在年底被當作沖銷處理。
deflation  Prolonged deflation can slow down economic growth.  n. 通貨緊縮  長期的通貨緊縮可能拖慢經濟成長。
valuation  An independent valuation of the building is required before the sale.  n. 估價；評價  出售前必須由獨立機構對這棟大樓進行估價。
liquidation  The company entered liquidation after failing to pay its creditors.  n. 清算；變現  公司因無法償還債權人而進入清算程序。
disbursement  Disbursement of the funds will begin once the contract is signed.  n. 支付；撥款  合約簽署後就會開始撥款。
budgetary  Budgetary constraints forced the team to delay the project.  adj. 預算的  預算限制迫使團隊延後專案。
payoff  The investment had a large payoff after only three years.  n. 回報；收益  這項投資僅僅三年就帶來豐厚的回報。
windfall  The sale of the old warehouse was an unexpected windfall for the firm.  n. 意外之財；橫財  出售舊倉庫對公司來說是一筆意外之財。
outlay  The initial outlay for the new equipment was quite high.  n. 支出；開銷  新設備的初期支出相當高。
financier  A well-known financier agreed to back the new venture.  n. 金融家；出資者  一位知名的金融家同意資助這項新事業。
overspend  Departments that overspend their budgets must submit a written explanation.  v. 超支  預算超支的部門必須提交書面說明。
creditworthy  Only creditworthy applicants will be approved for the loan.  adj. 信用良好的  只有信用良好的申請人才會獲准貸款。
# ===== 10 銀行與投資 =====
teller  The teller counted the cash twice before handing it to the customer.  n. 銀行櫃員  櫃員在把現金交給顧客前數了兩次。
principal  Your monthly payment covers both the principal and the interest.  n. 本金；校長；adj. 主要的  您每月的還款包含本金與利息。
yield  The bond yields four percent each year.  v. 產生收益；n. 殖利率；收益  這檔債券每年產生百分之四的收益。
borrower  Each borrower must sign the agreement in front of a bank officer.  n. 借款人  每位借款人都必須在銀行行員面前簽署協議。
borrow  We had to borrow money to cover the cost of the new equipment.  v. 借入  我們必須借錢來支付新設備的費用。
lend  Banks lend money to businesses that can show a stable income.  v. 借出；貸款給  銀行會把錢貸給能證明收入穩定的企業。
repay  You must repay the full amount within five years.  v. 償還；還款  您必須在五年內還清全額。
maturity  The bond reaches maturity in the year 2030.  n. 到期；成熟  這檔債券將於二○三○年到期。
securities  The agency regulates the sale of securities to the public.  n. 有價證券  該機構負責監管向大眾銷售有價證券的行為。
commodity  Gold is a commodity that investors often buy when markets are unstable.  n. 商品；大宗物資  黃金是市場不穩時投資人常買進的大宗商品。
recession  Many small firms closed during the long recession.  n. 經濟衰退  許多小公司在長期的經濟衰退期間倒閉。
volatile  Oil prices have been volatile since the start of the year.  adj. 波動劇烈的；不穩定的  油價自今年年初以來一直波動劇烈。
speculate  Analysts speculate that the central bank will raise rates again.  v. 推測；投機  分析師推測中央銀行會再度升息。
diversify  It is wise to diversify your investments across several industries.  v. 使多元化；分散投資  明智的做法是把投資分散到幾個不同產業。
hedge  The company used contracts to hedge against changes in exchange rates.  v. 避險；n. 對沖  公司利用合約來對沖匯率變動的風險。
fluctuate  Exchange rates fluctuate throughout the day.  v. 波動；起伏  匯率整天都在波動。
liquidate  The owners decided to liquidate the company's assets after the bankruptcy.  v. 清算；變現  業主在破產後決定清算公司的資產。
liquid  Cash is the most liquid asset on a balance sheet.  adj. 流動性高的；液體的  現金是資產負債表上流動性最高的資產。
default  Borrowers who default on payments may lose their property.  v. 違約；n. 違約；預設值  拖欠還款的借款人可能會失去他們的財產。
cashier  Please take your receipt to the cashier at the front of the bank.  n. 出納員；收銀員  請把您的收據拿到銀行前方的出納櫃檯。
cheque  The accounting office issued a cheque to the supplier on Monday.  n. 支票  會計室週一開了一張支票給供應商。
beneficiary  Please list a beneficiary for your investment account.  n. 受益人  請為您的投資帳戶指定一位受益人。
refinance  Many homeowners refinance when interest rates drop.  v. 重新融資；轉貸  利率下降時，許多屋主會辦理轉貸。
delinquent  The bank sent a notice about the delinquent account.  adj. 拖欠的；逾期的  銀行寄出了關於逾期帳戶的通知。
remittance  The remittance arrived in our account on Wednesday morning.  n. 匯款  這筆匯款於週三早上進入我們的帳戶。
wire  Please wire the payment to our overseas branch today.  v. 電匯；n. 電匯  請今天將款項電匯到我們的海外分行。
withdrawal  A withdrawal of more than ten thousand dollars requires approval.  n. 提款；撤回  超過一萬美元的提款需經核准。
vault  The documents are kept in the bank's secure vault.  n. 金庫；保險庫  這些文件存放在銀行安全的金庫中。
bullish  Investors are bullish about the outlook for the technology sector.  adj. 看漲的；樂觀的  投資人對科技類股的前景看漲。
bearish  Analysts remain bearish on retail stocks this quarter.  adj. 看跌的；悲觀的  分析師對本季的零售類股仍持看跌態度。
rally  Stock prices began to rally after the positive jobs report.  v. 反彈；n. 反彈行情  在就業報告表現亮眼後，股價開始反彈。
benchmark  The fund uses the national index as its benchmark.  n. 基準；v. 以…為基準比較  這檔基金以全國指數作為其基準。
underwrite  The bank agreed to underwrite the new share offering.  v. 承銷；為...保險  銀行同意承銷這次新股發行。
venture  The investment group backed a risky venture in the energy market.  n. 新創事業；冒險投資  這個投資集團資助了能源市場中一項高風險的事業。
leverage  The firm used leverage to finance the acquisition.  n. 槓桿；v. 運用槓桿  該公司利用槓桿來為這項併購籌措資金。
monetary  The central bank announced a change in monetary policy.  adj. 貨幣的；金錢的  中央銀行宣布調整貨幣政策。
treasury  The treasury department manages the company's cash reserves.  n. 財務部；國庫  財務部負責管理公司的現金準備。
reserve  The bank keeps a large cash reserve to cover unexpected losses.  n. 準備金；儲備；v. 預訂  銀行保有大量現金準備，以因應突發的損失。
wealth  The report shows a growing gap in wealth between regions.  n. 財富  報告顯示各地區之間的財富差距日益擴大。
compound  Interest will compound monthly on this account.  v. 複利計算；n. 複合物  這個帳戶的利息將按月以複利計算。
variable  The loan has a variable rate that changes every six months.  adj. 可變的；n. 變數  這筆貸款採機動利率，每六個月調整一次。
prime  The bank offers its best loan rates to customers with prime credit.  adj. 最優的；主要的  銀行將最優惠的貸款利率提供給信用良好的客戶。
bailout  The government approved a bailout to save the struggling bank.  n. 紓困；財務救援  政府核准紓困方案，以拯救陷入困境的銀行。
bank  The bank will open a new branch near the train station next month.  n. 銀行  這家銀行下個月將在火車站附近開設新分行。
banking  Online banking lets customers check their balances at any time of day.  n. 銀行業務  網路銀行讓客戶能在一天中的任何時間查詢餘額。
brokerage  The brokerage charges a small fee for every trade placed online.  n. 證券經紀商  這家證券經紀商對每筆線上交易收取少許手續費。
foreclose  The lender may foreclose on the property if payments stop for six months.  v. 取消抵押品贖回權  若停止還款六個月，貸款機構可能會取消該房產的贖回權。
foreclosure  Many families faced foreclosure after the housing market collapsed last year.  n. 法拍；止贖  去年房市崩盤後，許多家庭面臨法拍。
startup  The bank offers special loans to every startup in the region.  n. 新創公司  銀行向該地區的每家新創公司提供特別貸款。
accumulate  Interest will accumulate on the unpaid balance each month.  v. 累積  未付餘額的利息每個月都會累積。
guarantor  The bank requires a guarantor before it approves the student loan.  n. 保證人  銀行要求有保證人才會核准這筆助學貸款。
cosign  My manager agreed to cosign the loan so I could buy a house.  v. 連署擔保  我的經理同意為這筆貸款連署擔保，讓我能買房子。
lien  The bank placed a lien on the building until the debt is paid.  n. 留置權  在債務還清之前，銀行對這棟建築物設定了留置權。
pledge  Investors pledge to contribute five million dollars to the new fund.  v. 承諾捐助；n. 承諾  投資人承諾捐出五百萬美元給這個新基金。
bullion  The vault stores gold bullion worth millions of dollars for the bank.  n. 金銀塊  這座金庫為銀行存放價值數百萬美元的金條。
speculation  There is growing speculation that the central bank will cut rates.  n. 臆測；投機  外界越來越多人揣測中央銀行將會降息。
speculator  A currency speculator can lose a fortune within a single day.  n. 投機者  外匯投機客可能在一天之內賠掉一大筆財富。
proxy  Shareholders who cannot attend may vote by proxy.  n. 代理人；委託書  無法出席的股東可以委託代理人投票。
derivative  Regulators warned banks about the risks of trading complex derivatives.  n. 衍生性金融商品  監管機關警告銀行，交易複雜的衍生性金融商品具有風險。
plunge  Share prices plunge when investors lose confidence in the market.  v. 暴跌  當投資人對市場失去信心時，股價就會暴跌。
slump  The housing market began to slump after interest rates rose sharply.  v. 衰退；n. 不景氣  利率大幅上升後，房市開始走下坡。
rebound  Stocks are expected to rebound once the trade talks end.  v. 反彈  貿易談判一結束，股市預期將會反彈。
soar  Profits soar when demand for the new product exceeds forecasts.  v. 飆升  當新產品的需求超出預期時，利潤便會飆升。
tumble  Oil prices tumble whenever the supply forecast is revised downward.  v. 暴跌  每當供給預測被下修，油價就會大跌。
endowment  The university used its endowment to fund new scholarships.  n. 捐贈基金  這所大學動用捐贈基金來提供新的獎學金。
trustee  The trustee manages the fund on behalf of the retired employees.  n. 受託人  受託人代表退休員工管理這筆基金。
custody  The bank holds the securities in custody for its clients.  n. 保管  銀行為客戶保管這些有價證券。
fiduciary  Advisers have a fiduciary duty to act in the best interests of clients.  adj. 受託的  顧問負有受託責任，必須以客戶的最大利益行事。
passbook  Please bring your passbook to the counter when you make a deposit.  n. 存摺  存款時請攜帶存摺到櫃檯辦理。
checkbook  She wrote a check from her checkbook to pay the monthly rent.  n. 支票簿  她從支票簿上開了一張支票來支付每月房租。
disburse  The fund will disburse the first payment to the borrowers on Friday.  v. 支付；撥款  基金將於週五向借款人撥付第一筆款項。
stake  The investor bought a ten percent stake in the logistics company.  n. 股份；利害關係  這位投資人買下了這家物流公司百分之十的股份。
launder  Banks must report any attempt to launder money through client accounts.  v. 洗錢  銀行必須通報任何透過客戶帳戶洗錢的企圖。
fraudulent  The bank froze the account after detecting fraudulent transactions.  adj. 詐欺的  銀行在偵測到詐欺交易後凍結了該帳戶。
kickback  The manager was fired for accepting a kickback from the vendor.  n. 回扣  這位經理因收受廠商回扣而遭到解雇。
arbitrage  Traders use arbitrage to profit from price gaps between two markets.  n. 套利  交易員利用套利，從兩個市場之間的價差中獲利。
takeover  The board rejected the hostile takeover bid from its largest rival.  n. 收購  董事會拒絕了最大競爭對手提出的敵意收購。
buyout  A private firm completed the buyout of the retail chain in May.  n. 買斷；收購  一家民營公司在五月完成了對這家零售連鎖企業的收購。
float  The company plans to float its shares on the stock exchange next year.  v. 上市；使浮動  該公司計畫明年在證券交易所掛牌上市。
issuer  The issuer must publish a prospectus before selling the bonds.  n. 發行人  發行人在銷售債券之前必須公布公開說明書。
prospectus  Read the prospectus carefully before you invest in any mutual fund.  n. 公開說明書  投資任何共同基金之前，請仔細閱讀公開說明書。
redemption  Early redemption of the bonds may involve a small penalty.  n. 贖回  提前贖回債券可能需要支付少許違約金。
mature  The bonds will mature in ten years and pay full value.  v. 到期  這些債券將在十年後到期，並支付全額。
overdraw  Customers who overdraw their accounts are charged a daily fee.  v. 透支  帳戶透支的客戶會被按日收取費用。
checking  Funds were moved from her savings to her checking account yesterday.  n. 支票存款帳戶  昨天資金從她的儲蓄帳戶轉到了支票存款帳戶。
inflate  Rising wages may inflate the cost of goods across the economy.  v. 使物價上漲  薪資上漲可能會推高整體經濟的商品成本。
recoup  The firm hopes to recoup its losses by the end of the year.  v. 彌補；收回  該公司希望在年底前彌補損失。
stimulus  The government announced a stimulus package to support small businesses.  n. 刺激措施  政府宣布了一項刺激方案來扶持小型企業。
austerity  The government introduced austerity measures to reduce its national debt.  n. 緊縮  政府推出緊縮措施以降低國債。
interest-bearing  This interest-bearing account pays two percent annually.  adj. 附息的  這個附息帳戶的年利率為百分之二。
overvalue  Analysts say investors overvalue technology stocks at the moment.  v. 高估  分析師表示，投資人目前高估了科技股。
undervalue  The report claims the market tends to undervalue small companies.  v. 低估  報告指出，市場往往會低估小型公司。
capitalization  The company's market capitalization surpassed one billion dollars this week.  n. 市值  該公司的市值本週突破了十億美元。
stockbroker  My stockbroker advised me to sell the shares before the announcement.  n. 股票經紀人  我的股票經紀人建議我在公告前賣掉這些股票。
savvy  She is a savvy investor who rarely loses money in volatile markets.  adj. 精明的  她是個精明的投資人，在波動的市場中很少虧錢。
transferable  The funds are transferable between accounts without any extra charge.  adj. 可轉讓的  這些資金可在帳戶之間轉移，無須額外收費。
hike  The central bank announced a rate hike to control inflation.  n. 調升  中央銀行宣布升息以控制通貨膨脹。
unsecured  Unsecured loans usually carry higher interest than secured ones.  adj. 無擔保的  無擔保貸款的利息通常比有擔保的高。
escrow  The deposit will be held in escrow until the sale is completed.  n. 第三方託管  訂金將由第三方託管，直到交易完成。
bankroll  A wealthy donor agreed to bankroll the new research center.  v. 提供資金  一位富有的捐款人同意出資成立這座新的研究中心。
# ===== 11 稅務保險與法律 =====
claim  You must submit your insurance claim within thirty days.  n. 理賠申請；索賠  您必須在三十天內提出保險理賠申請。
claim  The company claims that the new device is safe for children.  v. 聲稱；主張  該公司聲稱這款新裝置對兒童是安全的。
deductible  You pay the first two hundred dollars, which is the deductible.  n. 自付額；adj. 可扣除的  前兩百美元由您負擔，這就是自付額。
legal  The legal team reviewed the contract before the meeting.  adj. 法律的；合法的  法務團隊在會議前審閱了這份合約。
law  A new law will require firms to report their emissions.  n. 法律；法規  一項新法律將要求企業申報其排放量。
lawsuit  The employee filed a lawsuit against the company for unpaid wages.  n. 訴訟  這名員工因積欠薪資而對公司提起訴訟。
sue  The customer threatened to sue the store over the faulty product.  v. 控告；提起訴訟  顧客揚言要因瑕疵商品控告這家商店。
court  The case will be heard in court next Tuesday.  n. 法院；法庭  此案將於下週二在法院開庭審理。
attorney  Our attorney will review the agreement and send comments tomorrow.  n. 律師  我們的律師會審閱這份協議，並於明天提供意見。
lawyer  She hired a lawyer to help with the property dispute.  n. 律師  她聘請了一位律師來處理財產糾紛。
statute  The statute requires employers to provide written contracts.  n. 成文法；法令  這項法令規定雇主必須提供書面合約。
legislation  New legislation will change how small businesses pay taxes.  n. 立法；法規  新的立法將改變小型企業的繳稅方式。
ordinance  A city ordinance limits noise after ten at night.  n. 市府法令；條例  一項市府條例限制晚上十點以後的噪音。
violate  Companies that violate the rules may face heavy fines.  v. 違反；侵犯  違反規定的公司可能面臨高額罰款。
violation  The inspector found a safety violation in the warehouse.  n. 違規；違反  檢查員在倉庫中發現了一項安全違規事項。
fine  The firm paid a large fine for dumping waste.  n. 罰款；v. 罰款  該公司因傾倒廢棄物而被處以鉅額罰款。
fine  The new schedule seems fine to me.  adj. 良好的；沒問題的  我覺得新的時程表沒問題。
withholding  Withholding is deducted from each paycheck before you receive it.  n. 預扣稅款；扣繳  預扣稅款會在您領薪水之前從每張薪資單中扣除。
exemption  Nonprofit groups may qualify for a tax exemption.  n. 豁免；免稅額  非營利團體可能符合免稅資格。
taxable  Bonuses are considered taxable income by the revenue office.  adj. 應課稅的  稅務機關將獎金視為應課稅所得。
levy  The government plans to levy a new tax on imported goods.  v. 徵收；n. 稅款  政府計畫對進口商品徵收新的稅。
tariff  The tariff on steel raised costs for local manufacturers.  n. 關稅；收費表  鋼鐵關稅提高了本地製造商的成本。
customs  The package was held at customs for two days.  n. 海關；關稅  這個包裹在海關被扣留了兩天。
evade  The owner was fined for trying to evade taxes.  v. 逃避；規避  這位業主因試圖逃稅而被罰款。
negligence  The court found the contractor guilty of negligence.  n. 疏忽；過失  法院認定該承包商犯有疏忽之過失。
damages  The company was ordered to pay damages to the injured worker.  n. 損害賠償金  該公司被判須賠償受傷的員工。
settlement  Both sides reached a settlement before the trial began.  n. 和解；理賠金；結算  雙方在審判開始前達成了和解。
plaintiff  The plaintiff claims the firm failed to deliver the goods.  n. 原告  原告主張該公司未能交付貨物。
defendant  The defendant denied all charges in court.  n. 被告  被告在法庭上否認了所有指控。
testify  The manager was asked to testify at the hearing.  v. 作證  經理被要求在聽證會上作證。
verdict  The jury reached a verdict after two days.  n. 裁決；判決  陪審團在兩天後做出了裁決。
judge  The judge dismissed the case for lack of evidence.  n. 法官；v. 判斷  法官因證據不足而駁回此案。
jury  The jury listened to the evidence for three weeks.  n. 陪審團  陪審團聽取證據長達三週。
appeal  The company will appeal the ruling next month.  v. 上訴；n. 上訴；吸引力  該公司下個月將對這項裁定提出上訴。
arbitration  The dispute will go to arbitration instead of court.  n. 仲裁  這起爭議將交付仲裁，而不是訴諸法院。
mediation  Mediation helped both parties resolve their disagreement quickly.  n. 調解  調解協助雙方迅速解決了歧見。
dispute  The two firms are in a dispute over the terms of the contract.  n. 爭議；v. 爭論  這兩家公司在合約條款上發生爭議。
litigation  The litigation lasted nearly three years.  n. 訴訟  這場訴訟持續了將近三年。
injunction  The court issued an injunction to stop the sale.  n. 禁制令  法院發出禁制令以阻止這項銷售。
jurisdiction  This matter falls outside the jurisdiction of the local court.  n. 管轄權  這件事不在本地法院的管轄範圍內。
patent  The inventor applied for a patent on the new battery design.  n. 專利；v. 取得專利  發明人為這項新電池設計申請了專利。
trademark  The company registered its logo as a trademark.  n. 商標  該公司將其標誌註冊為商標。
copyright  The copyright on the book belongs to the publisher.  n. 版權；著作權  這本書的著作權屬於出版社。
infringe  The competitor was accused of trying to infringe our patent.  v. 侵害；侵犯  這家競爭對手被指控企圖侵害我們的專利。
infringement  The firm sued for copyright infringement.  n. 侵權  該公司以著作權侵權為由提起訴訟。
disclosure  The report requires full disclosure of all financial interests.  n. 揭露；公開  這份報告要求完整揭露所有財務利益。
waiver  Please sign the waiver before joining the tour.  n. 棄權書；免責聲明  參加行程前請先簽署免責聲明。
indemnity  The contract includes an indemnity clause for the supplier.  n. 賠償；補償  合約中包含一項針對供應商的賠償條款。
insure  The firm decided to insure its fleet against theft.  v. 投保；為...保險  該公司決定為其車隊投保竊盜險。
insurer  The insurer paid the claim within ten days.  n. 保險公司；承保人  保險公司在十天內支付了理賠金。
policyholder  Each policyholder will receive a renewal notice by mail.  n. 保單持有人  每位保單持有人都會收到郵寄的續約通知。
claimant  The claimant must provide proof of the loss.  n. 申請人；索賠人  索賠人必須提供損失證明。
adjuster  An insurance adjuster will visit the site on Friday.  n. 理賠員  保險理賠員將於週五前往現場。
hazard  Loose cables are a safety hazard in the office.  n. 危險；危害  鬆脫的電線是辦公室裡的安全隱患。
exclusion  The exclusion in the policy means flood damage is not covered.  n. 除外責任；排除  保單中的除外條款表示水災損失不在理賠範圍內。
renewal  The renewal of your policy is due next month.  n. 續約；更新  您的保單將於下個月到期續約。
lapse  Your coverage will lapse if the premium is not paid.  v. 失效；n. 失效  若未繳納保費，您的保障將會失效。
void  The agreement becomes void if either party breaks the terms.  adj. 無效的；v. 使無效  只要任一方違反條款，這份協議就會失效。
enforce  The agency will enforce the new rules starting in June.  v. 執行；強制實施  該機關將自六月起執行新規定。
enforcement  Strict enforcement of the law has reduced illegal dumping.  n. 執法；實施  嚴格執法已減少了非法傾倒。
prohibit  The policy prohibits employees from accepting gifts from vendors.  v. 禁止  這項政策禁止員工接受廠商的禮物。
legitimate  The firm provided legitimate reasons for the delay.  adj. 合法的；正當的  該公司為延誤提出了正當的理由。
liable  The company is liable for any damage during delivery.  adj. 負有法律責任的  公司須對運送過程中的任何損壞負責。
binding  The signed agreement is legally binding on both parties.  adj. 有約束力的  這份簽署完成的協議對雙方都具有法律約束力。
affidavit  She signed an affidavit confirming the facts of the case.  n. 宣誓書  她簽署了一份宣誓書，確認此案的事實。
regulate  The commission will regulate the sale of financial products.  v. 管制；監管  委員會將監管金融商品的銷售。
regulator  The regulator approved the merger on Monday.  n. 監管機關  監管機關於週一核准了這項合併案。
ruling  The ruling in favor of the firm surprised many analysts.  n. 裁定；判決  有利於該公司的裁定讓許多分析師感到意外。
precedent  The decision set a precedent for similar cases.  n. 先例  這項決定為類似案件開了先例。
accident  Report any workplace accident to your supervisor immediately.  n. 意外；事故  任何職場意外都請立即向主管報告。
counsel  Legal counsel advised the board to delay the announcement.  n. 法律顧問；v. 建議  法律顧問建議董事會延後發布公告。
stipulate  The agreement stipulates that payment is due within thirty days.  v. 規定；約定  協議規定款項須於三十天內付清。
contractual  The supplier failed to meet its contractual obligations.  adj. 合約的  該供應商未能履行其合約義務。
subpoena  The court sent a subpoena to the company's accounting office.  n. 傳票  法院向該公司的會計部門寄發了傳票。
tribunal  The tribunal will hear the complaint next month.  n. 法庭；仲裁庭  仲裁庭將於下個月審理這項申訴。
lawful  The search was lawful and carried out with a warrant.  adj. 合法的  這次搜查是合法的，並且持有搜索令。
illegal  It is illegal to sell products without a valid license.  adj. 非法的  沒有有效執照就銷售產品是違法的。
restrict  The new rule will restrict access to the files.  v. 限制  新規定將限制對這些檔案的存取。
taxpayer  Every taxpayer must report income from foreign sources.  n. 納稅人  每位納稅人都必須申報國外來源的所得。
bylaw  The club's bylaws state that meetings are held monthly.  n. 內部章程；細則  俱樂部章程規定每月舉行一次會議。
ethical  The firm has a strict code of ethical conduct.  adj. 道德的；倫理的  該公司有嚴格的道德行為準則。
loophole  The accountant found a legal loophole that reduced the tax bill.  n. 漏洞  會計師發現了一個能減少稅額的法律漏洞。
indemnify  The contract states that the supplier will indemnify the buyer against losses.  v. 賠償；保障  合約規定供應商須賠償買方的損失。
plead  The defendant will plead not guilty at tomorrow's hearing.  v. 答辯  被告將在明天的聽證會上答辯，主張無罪。
defend  A senior lawyer will defend the company in court next week.  v. 為...辯護  一位資深律師將於下週在法庭上為公司辯護。
felony  Tax fraud above a certain amount is considered a felony.  n. 重罪  超過一定金額的稅務詐欺被視為重罪。
misdemeanor  Parking without a permit is only a misdemeanor in this city.  n. 輕罪  在這座城市，無許可停車只算是輕罪。
acquit  The jury voted to acquit the manager of all charges.  v. 宣告無罪  陪審團投票通過，判定這位經理所有罪名均不成立。
convict  Prosecutors need strong evidence to convict an executive of fraud.  v. 定罪  檢察官需要有力的證據，才能判定一名高階主管詐欺罪成立。
sentence  The judge will sentence the offender to two years in prison.  v. 判刑；n. 刑期  法官將判處這名罪犯兩年有期徒刑。
bail  The court set bail at ten thousand dollars.  n. 保釋金  法院將保釋金定為一萬美元。
perjury  Lying under oath is a crime known as perjury.  n. 偽證罪  宣誓後說謊是一種稱為偽證的罪行。
trespass  Signs warn visitors not to trespass on the private construction site.  v. 擅闖  告示牌警告訪客不得擅闖這處私人工地。
testimony  The witness gave testimony that supported the claim of the plaintiff.  n. 證詞  證人提供了支持原告主張的證詞。
deposition  Each executive must give a deposition before the trial begins.  n. 宣誓證詞  每位主管在審判開始前都必須提供宣誓證詞。
plea  The company entered a plea of not guilty on all counts.  n. 答辯  該公司對所有罪名都做出無罪答辯。
heir  The heir will receive the family business after the will is read.  n. 繼承人  遺囑宣讀之後，繼承人將接手這間家族企業。
inherit  She will inherit the property when her father dies.  v. 繼承  她父親過世後，她將繼承這筆房產。
probate  The court must approve the will through the probate process.  n. 遺囑認證  法院必須透過遺囑認證程序來核准這份遺囑。
actuary  An actuary calculates the likelihood of claims to set insurance premiums.  n. 精算師  精算師計算理賠發生的可能性，以訂定保險費。
casualty  The policy covers casualty losses caused by fire or storms.  n. 意外損失；傷亡  這份保單涵蓋因火災或暴風雨造成的意外損失。
theft  The insurer paid the claim for the theft of the equipment.  n. 竊盜  保險公司支付了設備遭竊的理賠金。
# ===== 12 行銷與廣告 =====
market  The firm plans to market its new product in Europe.  v. 行銷；推廣  該公司計畫在歐洲行銷其新產品。
market  The mobile phone market is growing quickly in Asia.  n. 市場  亞洲的手機市場正快速成長。
brand  The brand is known for its reliable and affordable appliances.  n. 品牌  這個品牌以可靠又平價的家電聞名。
campaign  The advertising campaign begins on Monday.  n. 活動；宣傳活動  這場廣告宣傳活動將於週一展開。
consumer  Consumer spending increased during the holiday season.  n. 消費者  消費者支出在假期期間有所增加。
demographic  The survey shows the demographic of our typical customer.  n. 人口統計族群  調查顯示了我們典型客戶的人口統計特徵。
segment  The company divided the market into three customer segments.  n. 區塊；細分市場  該公司將市場劃分為三個客群區塊。
slogan  The new slogan is short and easy to remember.  n. 口號；標語  新的標語簡短又好記。
logo  Please place the company logo at the top of the page.  n. 標誌  請將公司標誌放在頁面頂端。
launch  The firm will launch its new smartphone in March.  v. 推出；n. 發表  該公司將於三月推出新款智慧型手機。
sponsor  A local bank agreed to sponsor the charity event.  v. 贊助；n. 贊助者  一家當地銀行同意贊助這場慈善活動。
sponsorship  Sponsorship from the beverage company covered all event costs.  n. 贊助  飲料公司的贊助涵蓋了活動的所有費用。
commercial  The television commercial aired during the evening news.  n. 廣告；adj. 商業的  這支電視廣告在晚間新聞時段播出。
billboard  A large billboard along the highway promotes the new resort.  n. 廣告看板  公路旁的一面大型看板正在宣傳這間新度假村。
flyer  Volunteers handed a flyer about the sale to every shopper.  n. 傳單  志工向每位購物者發送了一張促銷活動的傳單。
brochure  The travel agency sent us a brochure about the tour packages.  n. 小冊子；簡介  旅行社寄給我們一本介紹旅遊套裝行程的小冊子。
coupon  Bring this coupon to the store to receive ten percent off.  n. 優惠券  憑這張優惠券到店內即可享九折優惠。
giveaway  The giveaway attracted hundreds of new followers online.  n. 贈品；抽獎活動  這場抽獎活動吸引了數百名網路新追蹤者。
sample  Customers can pick up a free sample at the entrance.  n. 樣品；試用品  顧客可以在入口處領取免費試用品。
loyalty  The rewards program builds customer loyalty over time.  n. 忠誠度  這項回饋方案會隨著時間培養顧客忠誠度。
engagement  The post received high engagement from our online audience.  n. 參與度；互動  這則貼文獲得了網路受眾的高度互動。
outreach  The outreach program helped us connect with new communities.  n. 外展活動；推廣  這項外展計畫幫助我們與新的社群建立聯繫。
awareness  The campaign raised awareness of the new product.  n. 知名度；認知  這場活動提升了新產品的知名度。
exposure  The sponsorship gave the brand great exposure at the festival.  n. 曝光  這項贊助讓該品牌在藝術節上獲得大量曝光。
impression  The first impression of the website was very positive.  n. 印象；廣告曝光次數  網站給人的第一印象非常好。
viewer  Each viewer received a link to download the coupon.  n. 觀眾；瀏覽者  每位觀眾都收到一個下載優惠券的連結。
subscriber  The magazine gained ten thousand new subscribers this year.  n. 訂閱者  這本雜誌今年新增了一萬名訂閱者。
influencer  A popular influencer posted a review of our new product.  n. 網紅；意見領袖  一位人氣網紅發布了我們新產品的評價。
testimonial  The website features a testimonial from a satisfied customer.  n. 推薦書；證言  該網站刊登了一位滿意顧客的推薦證言。
trend  Online shopping is a growing trend among older customers.  n. 趨勢  網路購物在年長顧客之間日益盛行。
niche  The company found a niche in organic pet food.  n. 利基市場；小眾市場  這家公司在有機寵物食品領域找到了利基市場。
positioning  The positioning of the brand targets luxury travelers.  n. 市場定位  這個品牌的市場定位鎖定奢華旅遊客群。
differentiate  Quality service helps us differentiate our brand from competitors.  v. 區別；使差異化  優質服務有助於我們讓品牌與競爭對手有所區隔。
competitor  Our main competitor lowered its prices last week.  n. 競爭對手  我們的主要競爭對手上週降價了。
competitive  The firm offers competitive prices and fast delivery.  adj. 有競爭力的  這家公司提供有競爭力的價格與快速的送貨服務。
rival  The rival firm released a similar product in June.  n. 對手；adj. 競爭的  對手公司在六月推出了類似的產品。
penetration  Market penetration in rural areas remains low.  n. 市場滲透率；滲透  鄉村地區的市場滲透率仍然偏低。
saturate  Too many brands saturate the market for bottled water.  v. 使飽和  太多品牌使瓶裝水市場趨於飽和。
expand  The retailer plans to expand into three new regions.  v. 擴張；擴大  這家零售商計畫拓展到三個新的區域。
demand  Demand for electric cars has grown steadily.  n. 需求；v. 要求  電動車的需求持續穩定成長。
pricing  The pricing of the product attracted many first-time buyers.  n. 定價  這項產品的定價吸引了許多首次購買的顧客。
bundle  The store sells the phone and case in a bundle.  n. 組合包；v. 搭售  這家店把手機和保護殼組合成套裝販售。
upsell  Staff are trained to upsell premium services to new clients.  v. 向上銷售  員工受過訓練，會向新客戶推銷高階服務。
merchandise  The shop sells merchandise featuring the team logo.  n. 商品；v. 銷售  這家店販售印有球隊標誌的商品。
retail  Retail sales rose sharply during the holiday season.  n. 零售；adj. 零售的  假期期間零售銷售額大幅上升。
wholesale  The shop buys goods at wholesale prices.  n. 批發；adj. 批發的  這家店以批發價進貨。
distribution  The distribution of free samples began on Friday.  n. 配銷；分發  免費試用品的發放從週五開始。
storefront  The storefront was redesigned to attract more visitors.  n. 店面  店面經過重新設計，以吸引更多訪客。
packaging  The new packaging is made entirely from recycled paper.  n. 包裝  新包裝完全以再生紙製成。
display  The display at the entrance features our spring collection.  n. 陳列；展示；v. 展示  入口處的陳列展示了我們的春季系列。
showcase  The trade fair will showcase our latest designs.  v. 展示；n. 展示會  這場貿易展將展出我們最新的設計。
exhibit  The firm will exhibit its new products at the convention.  v. 展出；n. 展覽品  這家公司將在會議上展出新產品。
expo  The company will have a booth at the travel expo.  n. 博覽會  這家公司將在旅遊博覽會上設攤位。
rollout  The rollout of the new campaign starts next week.  n. 推出；上市  新活動將於下週開始推出。
teaser  A short teaser was posted online before the launch.  n. 預告；吸睛廣告  上市前，網路上先發布了一支簡短的預告。
tagline  The tagline appears under the logo on every package.  n. 廣告標語  每個包裝上的標誌下方都印有廣告標語。
jingle  The catchy jingle made the commercial memorable.  n. 廣告歌曲  這首朗朗上口的廣告歌讓廣告令人印象深刻。
copywriter  The copywriter drafted three versions of the headline.  n. 文案撰寫人  文案撰寫人擬了三個版本的標題。
headline  The headline should grab the reader's attention.  n. 標題  標題應該要能抓住讀者的注意力。
banner  A banner on the home page announces the sale.  n. 橫幅廣告；旗幟  首頁上的橫幅廣告宣布了這次特賣。
mailing  The mailing went out to five thousand households.  n. 郵寄；郵寄廣告  這批郵寄廣告寄給了五千戶家庭。
traffic  The campaign doubled the traffic to our website.  n. 流量；車流  這項活動使我們網站的流量增加了一倍。
conversion  The conversion rate improved after the site redesign.  n. 轉換；轉換率  網站改版後，轉換率有所提升。
analytics  Web analytics show which ads attract the most clicks.  n. 數據分析  網站數據分析顯示哪些廣告吸引最多點擊。
metric  Sales growth is the key metric for this campaign.  n. 指標  銷售成長是這項活動的關鍵指標。
celebrity  A celebrity appeared in the new television commercial.  n. 名人  一位名人出現在新的電視廣告中。
buzz  The trailer created a lot of buzz online.  n. 話題；議論  這支預告片在網路上引起了許多話題。
viral  The video went viral within a few hours.  adj. 病毒式傳播的  這支影片在幾小時內就爆紅了。
persuade  The ad aims to persuade shoppers to try the new flavor.  v. 說服  這則廣告旨在說服消費者嘗試新口味。
persuasive  The presentation was clear and persuasive.  adj. 有說服力的  這場簡報條理清楚且很有說服力。
tailor  We tailor each message to a specific group of customers.  v. 量身訂做；n. 裁縫  我們為特定客群量身訂做每則訊息。
customize  Customers can customize the product with their own name.  v. 客製化  顧客可以在產品上客製化自己的名字。
personalize  The email was personalized with the customer's first name.  v. 個人化  這封電子郵件加上了顧客的名字，做了個人化處理。
cater  The new menu will cater to health-conscious diners.  v. 迎合；提供餐飲  新菜單將迎合注重健康的用餐者。
clientele  The boutique has a wealthy international clientele.  n. 客群；顧客群  這家精品店擁有富裕的國際客群。
patron  Loyal patrons received a free gift at the door.  n. 老主顧；贊助人  忠實的老主顧在門口領到了免費贈品。
shopper  Each shopper received a coupon at the checkout.  n. 購物者  每位購物者在結帳時都收到一張優惠券。
prospect  The sales team contacted each prospect by phone.  n. 潛在客戶；前景  銷售團隊以電話聯絡每一位潛在客戶。
pitch  She will pitch the new campaign to the client on Monday.  v. 提案；推銷；n. 提案  她將在週一向客戶提案這項新活動。
rebrand  The company will rebrand itself to attract younger customers.  v. 重塑品牌  這家公司將重塑品牌，以吸引更年輕的顧客。
exclusive  The store offers an exclusive deal to members.  adj. 獨家的；專屬的  這家店為會員提供專屬優惠。
complimentary  Guests receive a complimentary gift with every order.  adj. 免費贈送的  每筆訂單賓客都會獲贈一份免費禮品。
redeem  You can redeem the voucher at any of our stores.  v. 兌換  您可以在我們任何一家門市兌換這張禮券。
clearance  The clearance sale at our outlet ends on Sunday.  n. 清倉大拍賣；許可  我們暢貨中心的清倉大拍賣將於週日結束。
bargain  Shoppers found great bargains at the weekend sale.  n. 特價品；v. 討價還價  購物者在週末特賣會上找到了很划算的特價品。
popularity  The popularity of the product grew after the ad appeared.  n. 受歡迎程度  廣告推出後，這項產品的受歡迎程度提高了。
endorsement  The athlete signed an endorsement deal with a sportswear brand.  n. 代言；背書  這位運動員與一個運動服飾品牌簽下了代言合約。
impulse  Placing candy near the register encourages impulse purchases.  n. 衝動  把糖果放在收銀台附近會刺激衝動購物。
advertiser  Every advertiser must submit the final artwork by Friday noon.  n. 廣告商  每位廣告商都必須在週五中午前交出完稿。
publicize  We will publicize the event on social media next week.  v. 宣傳  我們下週將在社群媒體上宣傳這場活動。
mascot  The new mascot appears on all packaging and promotional materials.  n. 吉祥物  新吉祥物出現在所有包裝與宣傳資料上。
spotlight  The campaign puts the spotlight on our eco-friendly product line.  n. 焦點  這項活動將焦點放在我們的環保產品系列上。
hype  Despite the hype, early reviews of the device were mixed.  n. 炒作  儘管炒作聲勢浩大，這款裝置的早期評價仍好壞參半。
branding  Consistent branding helps customers recognize our products quickly.  n. 品牌塑造  一致的品牌塑造有助於顧客快速認出我們的產品。
persona  Each buyer persona describes the needs of a typical customer group.  n. 人物誌；客群角色  每個買家人物誌都描述了典型客群的需求。
unveil  The company will unveil its new logo at the trade fair.  v. 揭幕  這家公司將在貿易展上揭幕新標誌。
catchy  The catchy melody made the commercial popular overnight.  adj. 朗朗上口的  這段朗朗上口的旋律讓廣告一夜爆紅。
eye-catching  An eye-catching display near the entrance attracted many shoppers.  adj. 吸睛的  入口附近一個吸睛的陳列吸引了許多購物者。
tout  The advertisement touts the new phone as the thinnest ever made.  v. 吹捧  這則廣告吹捧這款新手機是有史以來最薄的。
promotional  Promotional materials will be mailed to all members this week.  adj. 促銷的  促銷資料本週將寄給所有會員。
leaflet  Volunteers handed out leaflets describing the new service.  n. 傳單  志工發送了介紹新服務的傳單。
# ===== 13 銷售與客戶服務 =====
customer  Every customer who signs up this month will receive a free gift card.  n. 顧客  本月註冊的每位顧客都會收到一張免費禮物卡。
client  Our sales team will meet the client on Thursday to discuss the contract.  n. 客戶  我們的銷售團隊將在週四與客戶會面討論合約。
sales  Sales of the new model exceeded our forecast by twenty percent.  n. 銷售額；銷售量  新機型的銷售額比我們的預測高出百分之二十。
complaint  We received a complaint about the late delivery of the order.  n. 抱怨；投訴  我們收到一則關於訂單延遲送達的投訴。
satisfaction  Customer satisfaction rose sharply after we extended our service hours.  n. 滿意；滿意度  我們延長服務時間後，顧客滿意度大幅上升。
purchase  Please keep your receipt as proof of purchase.  n. 購買；購買品；v. 購買  請保留收據作為購買證明。
quota  Each representative must meet a monthly quota of fifty new accounts.  n. 配額；業績指標  每位業務代表每月必須達成五十個新客戶的業績指標。
deal  The manager closed a major deal with a hotel chain yesterday.  n. 交易；協議  經理昨天與一家連鎖飯店談成了一筆大交易。
deal  Our staff are trained to deal with difficult customers politely.  v. 應付；處理  我們的員工受過訓練，能有禮貌地應付難搞的顧客。
subscription  Your subscription will renew automatically unless you cancel it before May 1.  n. 訂閱  除非您在五月一日前取消，否則您的訂閱將自動續約。
resolve  The support team managed to resolve the billing problem within an hour.  v. 解決  支援團隊成功在一小時內解決了帳單問題。
assist  A sales associate will assist you with choosing the right model.  v. 協助  銷售人員會協助您挑選合適的機型。
satisfy  Our goal is to satisfy every customer on the first visit.  v. 使滿意  我們的目標是讓每位顧客在第一次光臨時就感到滿意。
courteous  The courteous staff helped me find a gift for my colleague.  adj. 有禮貌的  這位有禮貌的店員協助我為同事找到了禮物。
checkout  Please proceed to checkout after reviewing the items in your cart.  n. 結帳  確認購物車內的商品後，請前往結帳。
delivery  Free delivery is available for orders placed before noon.  n. 送貨  中午前下的訂單可享免費送貨。
tracking  Use the tracking number in your email to check your parcel.  n. 追蹤  請使用電子郵件中的追蹤編號查詢您的包裹。
escalate  Unresolved cases will be escalated to a senior manager.  v. 升級處理  未解決的案件將升級交由資深經理處理。
compensate  We will compensate you for the delay with a free upgrade.  v. 補償  我們將以免費升級來補償您所遭遇的延誤。
guarantee  We guarantee delivery within three business days of your order.  v. 保證；n. 保證  我們保證在您下單後三個工作天內送達。
lead  Marketing passed twenty new leads to the sales team this week.  n. 銷售線索  行銷部本週將二十條新的銷售線索轉交給銷售團隊。
lead  Ms. Kim will lead the new marketing team.  v. 領導；帶領  金女士將領導新的行銷團隊。
rating  The store earned a five-star rating from online reviewers.  n. 評分；評價  這家店獲得網路評論者的五星評價。
demonstration  The demonstration of the new blender begins at two o'clock.  n. 示範  新款果汁機的示範將於兩點開始。
trial  Sign up for a free thirty-day trial of our software.  n. 試用  歡迎註冊我們軟體為期三十天的免費試用。
markdown  The markdown on last season's shoes ends on Sunday.  n. 降價  上一季鞋款的降價活動將於週日結束。
cancellation  Cancellation within twenty-four hours of booking is free of charge.  n. 取消  預訂後二十四小時內取消免收費用。
valid  This coupon is valid only at participating locations.  adj. 有效的  這張優惠券僅限參與活動的門市使用。
expire  Your discount code will expire at midnight on Friday.  v. 到期；失效  您的折扣碼將於週五午夜失效。
dissatisfied  Any dissatisfied customer may request a callback from a manager.  adj. 不滿意的  任何不滿意的顧客都可要求經理回電。
courtesy  As a courtesy, we will waive the late fee this time.  n. 禮遇；好意  基於禮遇，這次我們將免收滯納金。
rapport  A good salesperson builds rapport with clients quickly.  n. 融洽關係  優秀的銷售員能迅速與客戶建立融洽的關係。
accommodate  We will try to accommodate your request for a later delivery date.  v. 配合；容納  我們會盡量配合您延後送貨日期的要求。
expectation  The service exceeded our expectations in every way.  n. 期望  這項服務在各方面都超出了我們的期望。
reputation  Our reputation for quick service has attracted many new clients.  n. 聲譽  我們服務迅速的聲譽吸引了許多新客戶。
after-sales  Our after-sales service includes free repairs for one year.  adj. 售後的  我們的售後服務包含一年免費維修。
backorder  The item is on backorder and will ship in two weeks.  n. 缺貨待補訂單  這項商品目前缺貨待補，將於兩週後出貨。
catalog  Our spring catalog features over two hundred new items.  n. 型錄  我們的春季型錄收錄了超過兩百項新品。
itemize  Each invoice will itemize every charge for your review.  v. 逐項列出  每張發票都會逐項列出所有費用供您核對。
surcharge  A small surcharge applies to orders shipped overnight.  n. 附加費  隔夜寄送的訂單需加收一筆小額附加費。
settle  We settled the billing dispute with the customer by issuing a credit.  v. 解決；了結  我們以開立退款額度的方式與顧客解決了帳單爭議。
settle  Please settle your account by the end of the month.  v. 結清；償還  請在月底前結清您的帳款。
overcharge  Please tell us if you believe we overcharged you for the service.  v. 多收費  若您認為我們對這項服務多收了費用，請告訴我們。
salesperson  A salesperson will contact you within one business day.  n. 銷售員  銷售員將在一個工作天內與您聯絡。
buyer  The buyer requested a sample before placing a large order.  n. 買家  買家在下大筆訂單前要求先提供樣品。
seller  The seller offered free shipping on all orders above fifty dollars.  n. 賣家  賣家對五十美元以上的所有訂單提供免運費。
purchaser  The purchaser must pay the full amount within thirty days.  n. 買方  買方必須在三十天內付清全額款項。
dealer  The dealer will deliver the vehicle to your door on Monday.  n. 經銷商  經銷商將於週一把車輛送到您家門口。
dealership  Visit our dealership to test drive the latest hybrid models.  n. 汽車經銷店  歡迎蒞臨我們的經銷店試駕最新的油電混合車款。
showroom  The new showroom displays our entire line of kitchen appliances.  n. 展示間  新的展示間陳列了我們全系列的廚房電器。
reseller  Authorized resellers can offer the software at a lower price.  n. 轉售商  授權的轉售商能以較低的價格提供這套軟體。
wholesaler  The wholesaler offers lower prices for bulk purchases.  n. 批發商  這家批發商對大量採購提供較低的價格。
shopkeeper  The shopkeeper greeted each customer with a warm smile.  n. 店主  店主以溫暖的笑容迎接每位顧客。
pipeline  Our sales pipeline looks strong for the coming quarter.  n. 銷售管道  我們下一季的銷售管道看起來很穩健。
cross-sell  Agents are trained to cross-sell insurance when customers open an account.  v. 交叉銷售  專員受過訓練，會在顧客開戶時交叉銷售保險。
helpdesk  Contact the helpdesk if you cannot log in to your account.  n. 服務台  若無法登入帳號，請聯絡服務台。
ticket  Your support ticket has been assigned to a senior technician.  n. 服務單  您的支援服務單已指派給一位資深技術人員。
ticket  A parking ticket was left on the windshield.  n. 罰單  擋風玻璃上被留了一張停車罰單。
agent  A customer service agent will answer your call shortly.  n. 客服人員  客服人員很快就會接聽您的來電。
convince  Our demonstration convinced the client to sign a two-year contract.  v. 說服  我們的示範說服了客戶簽下一份兩年期合約。
reassure  The manager tried to reassure the worried customer about the delay.  v. 使安心  經理試圖就延誤一事安撫憂心的顧客。
assure  I can assure you that your order will arrive on time.  v. 向…保證  我可以向您保證，您的訂單會準時送達。
apologetic  The clerk was very apologetic about the billing mistake.  adj. 道歉的  店員對這次帳單錯誤深表歉意。
attentive  The attentive staff refilled our drinks without being asked.  adj. 殷勤的  殷勤的服務人員不待我們開口就替我們續了飲料。
responsive  Customers praised the responsive support team for fast solutions.  adj. 反應迅速的  顧客稱讚反應迅速的支援團隊能快速解決問題。
helpful  Several customers described the staff as friendly and helpful.  adj. 樂於協助的  好幾位顧客形容這些員工親切又樂於協助。
empathy  Good service representatives show empathy toward upset customers.  n. 同理心  優秀的客服人員會對心情不悅的顧客展現同理心。
patience  Thank you for your patience while we resolve this issue.  n. 耐心  感謝您在我們處理此問題期間的耐心等候。
frustrate  Long waiting times frustrate customers and hurt our reputation.  v. 使沮喪  漫長的等候時間讓顧客感到挫折，也損害了我們的聲譽。
irate  An irate customer demanded to speak with the manager.  adj. 憤怒的  一位憤怒的顧客要求與經理談話。
complain  Several customers complain about the long lines at checkout.  v. 抱怨  有幾位顧客抱怨結帳時排隊的人龍太長。
convenience  For your convenience, our store stays open until midnight.  n. 便利  為了方便您，本店營業到午夜。
reasonable  We offer reasonable prices and a thirty-day return policy.  adj. 合理的  我們提供合理的價格與三十天的退貨政策。
pricey  The model is pricey, but it comes with a five-year warranty.  adj. 昂貴的  這款機型價格偏高，但附有五年保固。
costly  A single repair could prove costly after the warranty expires.  adj. 昂貴的  保固到期後，光是一次維修就可能花費不貲。
overpriced  Some shoppers felt the souvenirs were overpriced.  adj. 定價過高的  有些購物者覺得這些紀念品定價過高。
worthwhile  Spending extra on a durable printer is worthwhile in the long run.  adj. 值得的  長遠來看，多花錢買一台耐用的印表機是值得的。
entice  Free samples entice shoppers to try new products.  v. 吸引  免費試用品吸引購物者嘗試新產品。
tempt  A limited-time offer may tempt hesitant customers to buy today.  v. 誘使  限時優惠可能誘使猶豫不決的顧客今天就下單。
hesitate  Do not hesitate to call us if you need any help.  v. 猶豫  如果需要任何協助，請不要猶豫，儘管致電給我們。
compelling  The sales team made a compelling case for upgrading the system.  adj. 有說服力的  銷售團隊提出了令人信服的理由，主張升級系統。
swap  You may swap the item for a different size within two weeks.  v. 調換  您可以在兩週內將商品換成不同的尺寸。
stockout  A stockout of our best seller disappointed many loyal customers.  n. 缺貨  我們熱銷商品的缺貨讓許多忠實顧客感到失望。
overstock  The store sold its overstock at half price this weekend.  n. 庫存過剩  這家店本週末以半價出售過剩的庫存。
closeout  The closeout sale ends on Sunday, so visit soon.  n. 清倉  清倉特賣將於週日結束，請儘早光臨。
loyal  We reward loyal customers with exclusive discounts every month.  adj. 忠實的  我們每個月都以專屬折扣回饋忠實顧客。
discontinue  We will discontinue this model once the current stock sells out.  v. 停產  現有庫存售完後，我們將停產這款機型。
upfront  The vendor asks for a fifty percent deposit upfront.  adv. 預先  廠商要求預先支付百分之五十的訂金。
knowledgeable  The knowledgeable staff helped me choose the right laptop.  adj. 知識豐富的  知識豐富的店員協助我挑選了合適的筆記型電腦。
goodwill  A small gift can build goodwill with long-term clients.  n. 商譽；善意  一份小禮物就能與長期客戶建立善意。
trade-in  Bring your old phone for a trade-in and save fifty dollars.  n. 以舊換新  帶舊手機來以舊換新，即可省下五十美元。
layaway  Many stores offer layaway plans for expensive items.  n. 分期保留付款  許多商店為高價商品提供分期保留付款方案。
flash  The flash sale starts at noon and lasts only two hours.  adj. 限時的  限時特賣中午開始，只持續兩小時。
upscale  The company opened an upscale boutique in the city center.  adj. 高檔的  這家公司在市中心開了一間高檔精品店。
hassle  Our online return process is fast and free of hassle.  n. 麻煩  我們的線上退貨流程快速又不麻煩。
seamless  We aim to provide a seamless experience from order to delivery.  adj. 無縫的  我們致力於提供從下單到送貨的無縫體驗。
pushy  A pushy salesperson can drive potential buyers away.  adj. 強迫推銷的  強迫推銷的銷售員可能會把潛在買家嚇跑。
greet  Staff should greet every visitor within thirty seconds.  v. 問候  員工應在三十秒內問候每一位來客。
cashback  Cardholders enjoy five percent cashback on grocery purchases.  n. 現金回饋  持卡人購買生鮮雜貨可享百分之五的現金回饋。
gift-wrap  We will gift-wrap any purchase free of charge.  v. 禮品包裝  我們將免費為任何購買的商品做禮品包裝。
unbeatable  Our unbeatable prices apply to every item in the store.  adj. 無可匹敵的  我們無可匹敵的價格適用於店內所有商品。
limited-time  This limited-time offer ends on Friday at midnight.  adj. 限時的  這項限時優惠將於週五午夜結束。
one-stop  Our website is a one-stop shop for office supplies.  adj. 一站式的  我們的網站是辦公用品的一站式購物平台。
in-person  You can make the purchase online or in-person at any branch.  adv. 親自  您可以在線上購買，或親自到任何一家分店購買。
# ===== 14 採購與供應鏈 =====
supplier  We have used the same supplier for office equipment for ten years.  n. 供應商  我們的辦公設備已向同一家供應商採購十年。
vendor  All vendors must submit their price lists by the end of the month.  n. 廠商；供應商  所有廠商都必須在月底前提交價目表。
procurement  The procurement department reviews every request before placing an order.  n. 採購  採購部門在下訂單前會審核每一項申請。
order  We placed an order for five hundred units of the new model.  n. 訂單；v. 訂購  我們訂購了五百台新機型。
order  Please keep the files in alphabetical order.  n. 順序；次序  請將檔案按字母順序存放。
quotation  Please send us a written quotation for the items listed below.  n. 報價單  請寄給我們下列項目的書面報價單。
bulk  Ordering in bulk lowers the cost per unit significantly.  n. 大量  大量訂購能大幅降低每件的成本。
specification  The product must meet every specification listed in the contract.  n. 規格  產品必須符合合約中列出的每一項規格。
shipment  The shipment is expected to reach our warehouse on Wednesday.  n. 出貨；貨物  這批貨預計週三抵達我們的倉庫。
tender  The city invited tenders for the construction of the new library.  n. 投標  市政府為新圖書館的興建工程公開招標。
bid  Three firms submitted a bid for the packaging project.  n. 投標；v. 出價  有三家公司為這項包裝專案提出了投標。
shortage  A shortage of raw materials delayed the production schedule.  n. 短缺  原物料短缺延誤了生產進度。
backlog  There is a backlog of unprocessed requests on my desk.  n. 積壓的工作  我的桌上積壓了一堆未處理的申請。
warehouse  The goods will be stored in our warehouse until the stores need them.  n. 倉庫  這些貨物會存放在我們的倉庫，直到門市需要為止。
logistics  Our logistics team coordinates deliveries to more than forty countries.  n. 物流  我們的物流團隊負責協調運往四十多個國家的貨物。
freight  Air freight is faster but costs far more than sea transport.  n. 貨運；運費  空運比較快，但費用遠高於海運。
shipping  Shipping costs are calculated based on the weight of the package.  n. 運送；運費  運費依包裹重量計算。
carrier  The carrier will pick up the cargo from our dock on Monday morning.  n. 運輸業者  運輸業者將於週一早上到我們的碼頭提領貨物。
import  The company will import electronic parts from three different countries.  v. 進口；n. 進口品  這家公司將從三個不同的國家進口電子零件。
export  Taiwan will export more semiconductors to Europe this year.  v. 出口；n. 出口品  台灣今年將對歐洲出口更多半導體。
consignment  The goods arrived on consignment and will be paid for after they sell.  n. 寄售；托運貨物  這些貨物以寄售方式送達，售出後才付款。
pallet  Each pallet holds forty boxes of canned goods.  n. 貨板；棧板  每個貨板可裝四十箱罐頭食品。
container  The container left the port on Friday and will arrive in three weeks.  n. 貨櫃；容器  貨櫃週五離開港口，預計三週後抵達。
fulfill  We fulfill most orders within two business days.  v. 履行；完成訂單  我們大多數訂單都會在兩個工作天內完成出貨。
replenish  Staff replenish the shelves every morning before the store opens.  v. 補貨；補充  員工每天早上在商店開門前為貨架補貨。
restock  We expect to restock the popular items by the end of next week.  v. 補貨  我們預計在下週末前為熱門商品補貨。
sourcing  The company is changing its sourcing strategy to reduce risk.  n. 採購；貨源籌措  公司正在調整採購策略以降低風險。
outsource  Many firms outsource their payroll work to specialized providers.  v. 外包  許多企業把薪資作業外包給專業業者。
terms  Payment terms are net thirty days from the date of invoice.  n. 條款；條件  付款條件為發票日起算三十天內付清。
requisition  Submit a requisition form to your manager before ordering supplies.  n. 請購單  訂購耗材前，請先向主管提交請購單。
unit  The price per unit drops when you order more than one hundred.  n. 單位；單品  訂購超過一百件時，每單位價格會下降。
quantity  Please confirm the quantity and delivery address before we ship.  n. 數量  出貨前請確認數量與送貨地址。
expedite  We can expedite your order for an additional fee.  v. 加速處理  我們可以加收費用，加速處理您的訂單。
rush  A rush order must be placed before noon to ship the same day.  adj. 緊急的；n. 匆忙  急單必須在中午前下單，才能當天出貨。
delay  A delay at the port pushed back the arrival date by a week.  n. 延誤；v. 延遲  港口的延誤使到貨日期往後延了一週。
inspect  Inspectors will inspect the goods before they leave the factory.  v. 檢查；查驗  檢驗人員會在貨物出廠前進行檢查。
component  A single faulty component caused the whole shipment to be rejected.  n. 零件；組件  單一瑕疵零件就導致整批貨被退回。
barcode  Staff scan the barcode on each box when goods arrive.  n. 條碼  貨物抵達時，員工會掃描每個箱子上的條碼。
distributor  The distributor delivers our products to hundreds of grocery stores.  n. 經銷商  經銷商把我們的產品配送到數百家雜貨店。
manufacturer  The manufacturer promised to ship the parts by the end of the week.  n. 製造商  製造商承諾在本週末前出貨零件。
retailer  The retailer placed a large order ahead of the holiday season.  n. 零售商  零售商在節慶旺季前下了大筆訂單。
transit  The parcel was damaged while in transit to the customer.  n. 運輸途中  包裹在運送給客戶的途中受損。
route  The truck took a different route to avoid the highway closure.  n. 路線；路徑  卡車改走另一條路線，以避開高速公路封閉路段。
disruption  A strike at the port caused serious disruption to our supply.  n. 中斷；擾亂  港口罷工嚴重擾亂了我們的供貨。
excess  Excess inventory ties up money that could be used elsewhere.  adj. 過量的；n. 過量  過量的庫存會占用本可另作他用的資金。
obsolete  Obsolete parts are removed from the catalog each year.  adj. 過時的  過時的零件每年都會從型錄中移除。
perishable  Perishable goods must be kept in refrigerated trucks.  adj. 易腐壞的  易腐壞的貨物必須用冷藏車運送。
fragile  Please handle the fragile items with extra care during loading.  adj. 易碎的  裝載時請格外小心處理易碎物品。
damage  Report any damage to the goods within forty-eight hours of delivery.  n. 損壞；v. 損害  請在交貨後四十八小時內回報任何貨物損壞。
discrepancy  We found a discrepancy between the invoice and the goods received.  n. 差異；不符  我們發現發票與實收貨物之間有出入。
substitute  The supplier offered a substitute because the original item was unavailable.  n. 替代品；v. 代替  由於原本的品項缺貨，供應商提供了替代品。
qualify  Only vendors that qualify under our criteria may submit a bid.  v. 符合資格  只有符合我們標準的廠商才能參與投標。
renew  The company decided to renew its contract with the packaging firm.  v. 續約；更新  公司決定與包裝廠商續約。
payable  The balance is payable within thirty days of delivery.  adj. 應付的  餘額須在交貨後三十天內支付。
tolerance  The parts must be made within a tolerance of one millimeter.  n. 公差；容許度  零件製造的公差必須在一公釐以內。
stockpile  The company began to stockpile fuel before the price increase.  v. 囤積  公司在漲價前開始囤積燃料。
consolidate  We plan to consolidate our orders to reduce handling fees.  v. 合併；整合  我們打算合併訂單，以降低處理費用。
sustainable  The firm prefers sustainable materials from certified forests.  adj. 永續的  這家公司偏好使用來自認證森林的永續材料。
negotiable  The price is negotiable if you commit to a long contract.  adj. 可議價的  如果您簽訂長期合約，價格可以商議。
markup  The retailer applies a markup of forty percent on imported goods.  n. 加價；成本加成  零售商對進口商品加價百分之四十。
dock  Trucks must wait in line before they can enter the loading dock.  n. 裝卸碼頭  卡車必須排隊等候，才能進入裝卸碼頭。
stockroom  Extra supplies are kept in the stockroom behind the front desk.  n. 庫房  櫃檯後方的庫房存放著額外的備品。
turnaround  The vendor promises a turnaround of three days for urgent requests.  n. 處理週期  供應商承諾緊急需求的處理時間為三天。
merchant  The merchant ships goods to buyers in over thirty countries.  n. 商人；商家  這位商人把貨物出口給三十多個國家的買家。
supplement  We will supplement our regular stock with imports during the summer.  v. 補充；n. 補充品  我們會在夏季以進口貨補充一般庫存。
procure  The department will procure all office furniture from a local supplier this year.  v. 採購；取得  今年部門將向當地供應商採購所有辦公家具。
allocation  The budget allocation for new equipment was increased in the revised plan.  n. 分配；配額  修訂後的計畫增加了新設備的預算分配。
bidder  The lowest bidder was awarded the contract for the new warehouse.  n. 投標人；出價者  報價最低的投標人取得了新倉庫的合約。
framework  Both parties signed a framework agreement covering annual purchases of packaging materials.  n. 架構；框架協議  雙方簽署了涵蓋包裝材料年度採購的框架協議。
intermediary  We bought the components through an intermediary based in Singapore.  n. 中間人；仲介  我們透過一家位於新加坡的仲介商購買這些零件。
stockist  Please contact your nearest stockist to place an urgent order for spare parts.  n. 現貨供應商；經銷店  如需緊急訂購備用零件，請聯絡離您最近的現貨供應商。
aggregate  Purchasing managers aggregate small orders to qualify for larger volume discounts.  v. 彙總；合併  採購經理會彙總小額訂單，以符合較大量折扣的資格。
criterion  Price is only one criterion we use when we evaluate each vendor.  n. 標準；評選準則  價格只是我們評估每家廠商時的其中一項標準。
award  The board will award the supply contract to the most reliable vendor.  v. 授予；決標  董事會將把供貨合約授予最可靠的廠商。
solicit  We plan to solicit proposals from at least five qualified vendors.  v. 徵求；招攬  我們打算向至少五家合格廠商徵求提案。
solicitation  The bid solicitation was posted on the procurement portal on Monday.  n. 招標公告；徵求  招標公告已於週一刊登在採購入口網站上。
prequalify  Suppliers must prequalify before they are invited to submit a formal bid.  v. 資格預審  供應商必須先通過資格預審，才會受邀提出正式投標。
annex  Technical specifications are listed in the annex attached to the contract.  n. 附件；附錄  技術規格列於合約所附的附件中。
appendix  Please refer to the appendix for a complete list of approved suppliers.  n. 附錄  完整的核准供應商名單，請參閱附錄。
acknowledgement  Please send a written acknowledgement as soon as you receive the purchase order.  n. 確認回覆；收悉通知  收到採購單後，請盡快回覆書面確認。
fulfil  The vendor failed to fulfil its obligations under the original agreement.  v. 履行；達成  廠商未能履行原協議中的義務。
expiry  The expiry of the supply contract is set for the end of December.  n. 到期；期滿  供貨合約將於十二月底到期。
threshold  Orders above this threshold require approval from a senior manager.  n. 門檻；臨界值  超過此門檻的訂單須經資深經理核准。
cap  The agreement puts a cap on annual price increases at three percent.  n. 上限；v. 設定上限  協議將每年的漲價幅度上限訂為百分之三。
sole  We chose a sole supplier to simplify our ordering process.  adj. 唯一的；獨家的  我們選擇獨家供應商，以簡化訂購流程。
pooling  Pooling orders across branches helped us negotiate a lower unit price.  n. 合併；集中  跨分公司合併訂單，幫助我們談到較低的單價。
futures  The firm uses futures contracts to protect itself from rising metal prices.  n. 期貨  公司利用期貨合約來避免金屬價格上漲的風險。
incoterm  The incoterm in this contract states that the seller pays for shipping.  n. 國貿條規  本合約中的國貿條規規定由賣方支付運費。
tolerate  The buyer will not tolerate repeated delays in delivery.  v. 容忍；忍受  買方不會容忍交貨一再延誤。
submission  The deadline for bid submission is next Friday at noon.  n. 提交；投標文件  投標文件的提交期限是下週五中午。
middleman  Buying directly from the factory cuts out the middleman and lowers costs.  n. 中間商  直接向工廠購買能省去中間商，降低成本。
rollover  Automatic rollover of the contract occurs unless either party gives notice.  n. 自動展期；續約  除非任一方發出通知，否則合約將自動展期。
deviation  Any deviation from the approved specifications must be reported immediately.  n. 偏差；背離  任何偏離核准規格的情形都必須立即回報。
tendering  The tendering process is open to all registered companies in the region.  n. 招標；投標  本地區所有登記有案的公司都能參與招標程序。
variance  The report shows a large variance between the quoted and actual prices.  n. 差異；差額  報告顯示報價與實際價格之間有很大的差額。
recommendation  The committee made a recommendation to switch to a cheaper supplier.  n. 建議；推薦  委員會建議改用較便宜的供應商。
approved  Only approved vendors may be added to the company purchasing system.  adj. 核准的；認可的  只有經核准的廠商才能加入公司的採購系統。
weighting  Each criterion has a different weighting in the final vendor score.  n. 權重；加權  每項標準在最終廠商評分中的權重各不相同。
scoring  The scoring sheet ranks every bidder by price, quality, and reliability.  n. 評分；計分  評分表依價格、品質與可靠度為每位投標人排名。
awardee  The awardee must submit a performance bond within ten days.  n. 得標者  得標者必須在十天內提交履約保證金。
sourced  Sourced materials must meet the same quality standards as locally produced goods.  adj. 採購而來的  採購而來的材料必須符合與本地生產商品相同的品質標準。
# ===== 15 製造與生產 =====
manufacture  The company will manufacture its new product line in Vietnam.  v. 製造  這家公司將在越南生產新產品線。
factory  The factory employs more than two thousand workers in three shifts.  n. 工廠  這間工廠分三班，僱用超過兩千名員工。
production  Production will increase once the new equipment is installed.  n. 生產；產量  新設備安裝完成後，產量將會增加。
assembly  The assembly of each unit takes about twenty minutes.  n. 組裝  每個單位的組裝大約需要二十分鐘。
assemble  Workers assemble the circuit boards by hand in this section.  v. 組裝  工人在這個區域以手工組裝電路板。
plant  The new plant will begin operations in the spring.  n. 工廠；廠房  新工廠將於春季開始營運。
plant  The company plans to plant a thousand trees around the new facility.  v. 種植  公司計畫在新廠區周圍種一千棵樹。
material  Raw material costs have gone up by ten percent this year.  n. 材料；原料  今年原料成本上漲了百分之十。
capacity  The factory is running at full capacity to meet holiday demand.  n. 產能；容量  工廠正以滿載產能運轉，以因應假期需求。
quality  Quality control staff check every product before it leaves the plant.  n. 品質  品管人員在每項產品出廠前都會檢查。
defect  Inspectors found a small defect in the casing of several units.  n. 瑕疵；缺陷  檢驗人員發現數個產品的外殼有小瑕疵。
machinery  Heavy machinery must be operated only by trained staff.  n. 機械設備  重型機械只能由受過訓練的人員操作。
labor  Labor costs make up forty percent of our production expenses.  n. 勞力；勞動  人力成本占我們生產費用的百分之四十。
automation  Automation has reduced the time needed to produce each part.  n. 自動化  自動化縮短了生產每個零件所需的時間。
robot  A robot welds the frame of each vehicle with great precision.  n. 機器人  機器人以極高的精準度焊接每輛車的車架。
prototype  The engineers tested the prototype before approving mass production.  n. 原型；樣機  工程師在核准量產前測試了原型機。
output  Daily output rose to five thousand units after the upgrade.  n. 產出；產量  升級後，每日產出增加到五千件。
efficiency  Better scheduling has improved the efficiency of our operation.  n. 效率  更好的排程提升了我們的作業效率。
productivity  Worker productivity increased after we reorganized the production floor.  n. 生產力  我們重整生產線後，工人的生產力提高了。
batch  Each batch of paint is tested before it is shipped.  n. 一批；批次  每批油漆在出貨前都會經過測試。
process  The manufacturing process involves cutting, shaping, and polishing the metal.  n. 流程；製程  製造流程包括切割、塑形與拋光金屬。
process  We will process your refund within five business days.  v. 處理；辦理  我們會在五個工作天內處理您的退款。
safety  Safety goggles are required in all areas of the workshop.  n. 安全  工作坊的所有區域都必須配戴護目鏡。
protective  All visitors must wear protective helmets inside the plant.  adj. 防護的  所有訪客進入廠區都必須戴防護安全帽。
helmet  Please put on a helmet before entering the construction area.  n. 安全帽  進入施工區域前，請先戴上安全帽。
forklift  Only certified staff may drive the forklift in the warehouse.  n. 堆高機  只有具認證資格的人員才能駕駛倉庫裡的堆高機。
conveyor  The conveyor carries finished products to the packing station.  n. 輸送帶  輸送帶把成品送往包裝站。
mold  The mold must be cleaned after every hundred cycles.  n. 模具  模具每運轉一百次就必須清潔。
tool  Workers must return every tool to the cabinet at the end of the shift.  n. 工具  工人必須在輪班結束時把每件工具放回櫃子。
lubricate  Please lubricate the moving parts of the machine every month.  v. 潤滑  請每月為機器的活動零件上潤滑油。
breakdown  A breakdown in the cooling system halted production on Monday.  n. 故障；停擺  冷卻系統故障使週一的生產停擺。
overhaul  The plant will close for a complete overhaul of its machinery.  n. 大修；v. 徹底檢修  工廠將停工，全面大修機械設備。
downtime  Regular checks help us keep unplanned downtime to a minimum.  n. 停機時間  定期檢查有助於我們把非計畫性停機時間降到最低。
shutdown  The shutdown of the factory lasted one week during the renovation.  n. 停工；關閉  翻修期間，工廠停工了一週。
fabricate  The shop can fabricate custom parts from steel within a week.  v. 製造；加工  這間工坊能在一週內以鋼材加工訂製零件。
weld  Skilled workers weld the metal sections together to form the frame.  v. 焊接  熟練的工人把金屬部件焊接在一起，組成框架。
precision  The machine cuts each part with remarkable precision.  n. 精確度  這台機器切割每個零件都極為精確。
consistent  We need consistent quality from every batch we produce.  adj. 一致的；穩定的  我們需要每一批產品都有穩定一致的品質。
waste  The new system has reduced material waste by fifteen percent.  n. 廢料；浪費  新系統使材料浪費減少了百分之十五。
waste  Please do not waste paper when you print drafts.  v. 浪費  列印草稿時請不要浪費紙張。
scrap  Metal scrap is collected and sent for recycling every Friday.  n. 廢料；邊角料  金屬廢料每週五都會收集並送去回收。
recycle  The factory will recycle all plastic waste produced on site.  v. 回收  工廠將回收現場產生的所有塑膠廢料。
rework  Defective units are sent back for rework before shipment.  n. 返工；v. 重做  瑕疵品會在出貨前退回返工。
blueprint  The blueprint shows the exact layout of the new assembly line.  n. 藍圖；設計圖  藍圖顯示新組裝線的確切配置。
appliance  The company makes small kitchen appliances for export.  n. 家電用品  這家公司生產外銷的小型廚房家電。
textile  The textile mill produces cotton fabric for clothing makers.  n. 紡織品  紡織廠為成衣業者生產棉布。
steel  The factory uses high-grade steel to build its machines.  n. 鋼  工廠使用高級鋼材製造機器。
chemical  Workers must store each chemical in a labeled container.  n. 化學品；adj. 化學的  工人必須把每種化學品存放在有標示的容器中。
plastic  The plastic casing is molded in a single step.  n. 塑膠  塑膠外殼一個步驟就能成型。
semiconductor  Demand for semiconductor chips keeps growing every year.  n. 半導體  對半導體晶片的需求逐年成長。
automotive  The automotive plant produces two hundred vehicles per day.  adj. 汽車的  這間汽車工廠每天生產兩百輛汽車。
pharmaceutical  The pharmaceutical firm follows strict hygiene rules in its factory.  adj. 製藥的；n. 藥品  這家製藥公司在工廠內遵守嚴格的衛生規定。
ingredient  Every ingredient is tested before it goes into the final product.  n. 原料；成分  每種原料在進入最終產品前都會經過測試。
throughput  Faster packing raised the overall throughput of the line.  n. 產量；處理量  更快的包裝提高了生產線的整體產量。
bottleneck  The painting stage is the main bottleneck in our workflow.  n. 瓶頸  噴漆階段是我們工作流程中的主要瓶頸。
workflow  A clear workflow helps each department avoid delays.  n. 工作流程  清楚的工作流程能幫助各部門避免延誤。
emission  The plant cut its carbon emission by twenty percent last year.  n. 排放  這間工廠去年把碳排放量降低了百分之二十。
pollution  Pollution from the old factory has been reduced significantly.  n. 汙染  舊工廠造成的汙染已大幅減少。
energy  The new machines use thirty percent less energy than the old ones.  n. 能源  新機器比舊機器少用百分之三十的能源。
pilot  The company ran a pilot project before expanding production.  adj. 試驗性的；n. 試辦  公司在擴大生產前先執行了試辦專案。
retool  The plant will retool its line to produce electric vehicles.  v. 更換設備  工廠將更換生產線設備，以生產電動車。
lean  The lean approach to manufacturing removes unnecessary steps.  adj. 精實的  精實的製造方式能去除不必要的步驟。
mass-produced  Mass-produced furniture is usually cheaper than handmade pieces.  adj. 大量生產的  大量生產的家具通常比手工家具便宜。
craftsmanship  The craftsmanship of each watch reflects years of training.  n. 工藝；手藝  每支手錶的工藝都反映出多年的訓練。
casting  The casting of engine parts takes place in the north wing of the plant.  n. 鑄造；鑄件  引擎零件的鑄造在工廠北棟進行。
forge  Workers forge steel bars into tools using heavy presses.  v. 鍛造  工人用重型壓機把鋼條鍛造成工具。
milling  Milling machines shape metal blocks with great precision.  n. 銑削；碾磨  銑床能極精確地把金屬塊加工成形。
drilling  Drilling must stop immediately if the operator hears an unusual noise.  n. 鑽孔  如果操作員聽到異常聲響，必須立即停止鑽孔。
grinding  Grinding removes rough edges from the finished metal parts.  n. 研磨  研磨能去除金屬成品上的粗糙邊緣。
polishing  After polishing, each panel is checked for scratches.  n. 拋光  拋光後，每片面板都會檢查有無刮痕。
coating  A protective coating prevents the surface from rusting.  n. 塗層；鍍膜  保護塗層可防止表面生鏽。
plating  Gold plating is applied to the electrical connectors.  n. 電鍍  電連接器表面會鍍上一層金。
painting  Automated painting lines have doubled our daily output.  n. 噴漆；塗裝  自動化噴漆線使我們的每日產出增加了一倍。
finishing  The finishing department inspects every item before packaging.  n. 加工收尾；表面處理  加工收尾部門在包裝前會檢查每件產品。
sanding  Sanding is done by hand to give the furniture a smooth surface.  n. 打磨；砂磨  砂磨以手工進行，讓家具表面平滑。
annealing  Annealing softens the metal so that it can be shaped more easily.  n. 退火  退火能軟化金屬，使其更容易成形。
tempering  Tempering makes the glass stronger and safer for use in cars.  n. 回火；強化處理  回火使玻璃更堅固，也更適合用於汽車。
alloy  The company produces a light alloy used in aircraft frames.  n. 合金  這家公司生產用於飛機機身的輕合金。
aluminum  The new plant will produce aluminum cans for beverage makers.  n. 鋁  新工廠將為飲料業者生產鋁罐。
cement  The factory ships cement to construction sites across the country.  n. 水泥  這間工廠把水泥運往全國各地的工地。
fabric  The mill weaves cotton fabric for export to Europe.  n. 布料；織物  這間工廠織造外銷歐洲的棉布。
boiler  The boiler must be inspected every year to meet safety standards.  n. 鍋爐  鍋爐每年都必須檢查，以符合安全標準。
furnace  The furnace operates at very high temperatures around the clock.  n. 熔爐；爐  熔爐日夜不停地在極高溫下運作。
kiln  Ceramic tiles are baked in a large kiln for twelve hours.  n. 窯  陶瓷磚要在大型窯裡烘烤十二小時。
motor  A faulty motor caused the conveyor to stop during the night shift.  n. 馬達；電動機  有故障的馬達導致輸送帶在夜班期間停止運轉。
pump  A new pump was installed to move cooling water through the system.  n. 幫浦；v. 抽送  安裝了新的幫浦，以便在系統中輸送冷卻水。
turbine  The wind turbine supplies power to the entire factory.  n. 渦輪機  風力渦輪機為整間工廠供電。
valve  Close the valve before you begin any maintenance work.  n. 閥門  開始任何維修工作前，請先關閉閥門。
gasket  A worn gasket allowed oil to leak from the machine.  n. 墊圈  磨損的墊圈讓機器漏油。
hopper  Raw pellets are fed into the hopper at the start of the process.  n. 進料斗  原料粒子在製程開始時被送入進料斗。
silo  Grain is stored in a silo until it is ready for processing.  n. 筒倉；穀倉  穀物儲存在筒倉中，直到可以加工為止。
circuit  The technician tested each circuit board before final assembly.  n. 電路  技術人員在最後組裝前測試了每塊電路板。
actuator  The actuator moves the robotic arm to the correct position.  n. 致動器  致動器會把機械手臂移動到正確位置。
coolant  Check the coolant level before starting the cutting machine.  n. 冷卻劑  啟動切削機器前，請先檢查冷卻劑液位。
lubricant  Apply a thin layer of lubricant to all moving parts weekly.  n. 潤滑劑  請每週在所有活動零件上塗一薄層潤滑劑。
lubrication  Regular lubrication extends the life of the equipment.  n. 潤滑；上油  定期潤滑能延長設備的使用壽命。
torque  Tighten each bolt to the torque specified in the manual.  n. 扭力  請依手冊指定的扭力鎖緊每顆螺栓。
tooling  New tooling for the product line will be installed next month.  n. 工具設備；模具  產品線的新工具設備將於下個月安裝。
die  The steel die shapes the hot metal into the desired form.  n. 模具；沖模  鋼製模具把高溫金屬塑造成所需的形狀。
jig  A simple jig holds the part in place during drilling.  n. 夾具；治具  簡單的夾具能在鑽孔時固定零件。
stamping  Metal stamping is used to produce thousands of identical brackets per hour.  n. 沖壓  金屬沖壓可用來每小時生產數千個相同的支架。
pressing  Pressing the sheets into shape requires a force of several tons.  n. 壓製；沖壓  將板材壓製成形需要數噸的力量。
molding  Plastic molding is the main process at our second facility.  n. 模塑；成型  塑膠成型是我們第二廠區的主要製程。
# ===== 16 物流與運輸 =====
ship  Our team will ship the replacement parts to your office by Friday.  v. 運送；出貨  我們的團隊會在週五前把更換零件寄到您的辦公室。
cargo  The cargo was inspected at the port before it was loaded onto trucks.  n. 貨物；載運物  貨物在裝上卡車前已在港口完成檢驗。
track  You can track your order online using the number in this email.  v. 追蹤；查詢進度  您可以用這封電子郵件中的編號線上追蹤訂單。
port  Several ships are waiting outside the port because of the storm.  n. 港口  由於暴風雨，數艘船隻在港口外等候。
vessel  The vessel is scheduled to leave the harbor at dawn.  n. 船隻；船舶  這艘船預定黎明時離開港口。
truck  A refrigerated truck will bring the fresh produce to the market.  n. 卡車  冷藏卡車會把新鮮農產品運到市場。
load  Workers began to load the boxes onto the truck at six o'clock.  v. 裝載；n. 貨載  工人在六點開始把箱子裝上卡車。
unload  It took two hours to unload the shipment at the loading dock.  v. 卸貨  在裝卸碼頭卸下這批貨花了兩個小時。
haul  The company will haul the equipment to the new site on Monday.  v. 拖運；運送  公司將於週一把設備拖運到新場址。
destination  The package reached its final destination on Wednesday morning.  n. 目的地  包裹於週三早上抵達最終目的地。
transport  The firm specializes in the safe transport of medical supplies.  n. 運輸；v. 運送  這家公司專營醫療用品的安全運輸。
pickup  Customers may choose home delivery or pickup at the nearest depot.  n. 取貨；接送  顧客可以選擇宅配，或到最近的集貨站取貨。
manifest  The ship's manifest lists every item loaded at the port.  n. 貨物清單；艙單  這艘船的貨物清單列出了在港口裝載的每件貨品。
reroute  The driver had to reroute because the main bridge was closed.  v. 改道；改變路線  由於主橋封閉，司機必須改道。
fleet  The company plans to add ten electric vans to its delivery fleet.  n. 車隊  公司打算為其配送車隊增加十輛電動貨車。
depot  Packages are sorted at the regional depot before final delivery.  n. 集散站；倉庫  包裹在最後配送前會先在區域集散站進行分揀。
crate  Each crate is lined with foam to protect the glassware.  n. 板條箱；木箱  每個板條箱內都襯有泡棉，以保護玻璃器皿。
pack  Please pack the samples carefully to prevent any damage in transit.  v. 打包；包裝  請小心包裝樣品，以免運送途中受損。
declare  You must declare all goods worth more than five hundred dollars.  v. 申報  價值超過五百美元的貨品都必須申報。
quarantine  Imported plants must be placed in quarantine for ten days.  n. 檢疫；隔離  進口植物必須檢疫十天。
embargo  The embargo prevented the company from selling its products abroad.  n. 禁運；貿易禁令  禁運令使該公司無法把產品銷往國外。
overnight  Documents sent overnight will arrive before nine tomorrow morning.  adv. 隔夜地；adj. 隔夜的  隔夜寄送的文件將於明天早上九點前送達。
consignee  The consignee is responsible for paying all import duties.  n. 收貨人；受託人  收貨人須負責支付所有進口關稅。
shipper  The shipper must complete the form before the cargo is accepted.  n. 託運人；貨主  託運人必須先填妥表格，貨物才會被受理。
waybill  Enter the waybill number to find the location of your freight.  n. 運單；貨運單  輸入運單號碼即可查詢您的貨物位置。
weight  The weight of the package determines the shipping fee.  n. 重量  包裹的重量決定運費。
dimension  Please enter the dimensions of the box when you book the pickup.  n. 尺寸；規格  預約收件時，請輸入箱子的尺寸。
volume  The volume of online orders doubled during the holiday season.  n. 量；數量；容積  假期期間網路訂單量增加了一倍。
volume  Please turn down the volume during the meeting.  n. 音量  開會時請把音量調小。
pier  Containers are stacked along the pier awaiting customs inspection.  n. 碼頭；突堤  貨櫃沿著碼頭堆放，等待海關查驗。
terminal  The cargo terminal operates around the clock during the holiday season.  n. 貨運站；航廈  假期期間，貨運站全天候運作。
carton  Each carton contains twelve bottles and weighs about eight kilograms.  n. 紙箱  每個紙箱裝有十二瓶，重約八公斤。
misplace  Someone must have misplaced the invoice during the move.  v. 放錯地方；遺失  搬遷期間一定有人把發票放錯地方了。
spoil  Fresh fish will spoil quickly if it is not kept cold.  v. 腐壞；變質  鮮魚若不冷藏，很快就會腐壞。
refrigerate  Please refrigerate the goods immediately upon delivery.  v. 冷藏  貨物送達後請立即冷藏。
domestic  Domestic shipping usually takes between two and four days.  adj. 國內的  國內運送通常需要二到四天。
overseas  Overseas orders may take up to two weeks to arrive.  adj. 海外的；adv. 在海外  海外訂單最多可能需要兩週才會送達。
maritime  Maritime transport remains the cheapest way to move heavy goods.  adj. 海運的；海事的  海運仍是運送重型貨物最便宜的方式。
railway  The railway line connects the factory directly to the harbor.  n. 鐵路  這條鐵路線將工廠直接連接到港口。
trailer  The truck pulled a trailer loaded with building materials.  n. 拖車；掛車  這輛卡車拖著一輛裝滿建材的拖車。
detour  Drivers should take a detour while the bridge is closed.  n. 繞道  橋梁封閉期間，駕駛人應該繞道行駛。
congestion  Traffic congestion near the port caused several late deliveries.  n. 壅塞；擁擠  港口附近的交通壅塞造成數筆貨物延遲送達。
arrival  The estimated arrival time is listed on your tracking page.  n. 抵達；到貨  預計到貨時間列在您的追蹤頁面上。
depart  The train carrying our goods will depart at midnight.  v. 出發；離開  載運我們貨物的列車將於午夜出發。
proof  The driver took a photo as proof of delivery.  n. 證明；證據  司機拍了一張照片作為送達證明。
tamper  Any sign that someone tampered with the box should be reported.  v. 擅自更改；破壞  只要發現箱子有遭人動過手腳的跡象，都應該通報。
stack  Do not stack more than three boxes on top of each other.  v. 堆疊；n. 一疊  請勿將超過三個箱子疊在一起。
tanker  The oil tanker is expected to reach the port on Thursday morning.  n. 油輪；油罐車  這艘油輪預計週四早上抵達港口。
barge  Heavy equipment is moved up the river by barge.  n. 駁船  重型設備是靠駁船沿河向上游運送。
freighter  The freighter carries more than two thousand containers across the Pacific.  n. 貨輪  這艘貨輪載運兩千多個貨櫃橫越太平洋。
liner  The cruise liner will dock at the harbor terminal at dawn.  n. 郵輪；班輪  這艘郵輪將於黎明時分停靠港口碼頭。
wharf  Trucks wait at the wharf to collect the imported goods.  n. 碼頭  卡車在碼頭等候提領進口貨物。
quay  The cargo was unloaded onto the quay and checked by customs.  n. 碼頭；岸壁  貨物卸到碼頭上，並由海關查驗。
jetty  Small boats deliver supplies to the island from the jetty.  n. 防波堤；小碼頭  小船從小碼頭把物資運送到島上。
anchorage  The vessel waited at anchorage until a berth became available.  n. 錨地；停泊處  這艘船在錨地等候，直到有泊位空出來。
ballast  Empty ships carry water as ballast to stay stable at sea.  n. 壓艙物  空船會載水作為壓艙物，以便在海上保持穩定。
hull  Inspectors found a crack in the hull during the routine check.  n. 船體  檢查人員在例行檢查中發現船體有一道裂縫。
stern  Cargo was loaded at the stern of the ship.  n. 船尾  貨物是裝載在船尾。
bow  The pilot boat stayed close to the bow as the ship entered the channel.  n. 船首  船駛入航道時，引水船緊靠著船首。
deck  Containers are stacked six high on the upper deck.  n. 甲板  貨櫃在上層甲板上堆疊了六層高。
stevedore  A team of stevedores loaded the crates onto the vessel overnight.  n. 碼頭裝卸工  一組碼頭裝卸工連夜把板條箱裝上船。
