longshore  Longshore workers will vote on the new contract next week.  adj. 碼頭工人的  碼頭工人將於下週就新合約進行投票。
reefer  A reefer container keeps the fruit cold throughout the voyage.  n. 冷藏貨櫃  冷藏貨櫃讓水果在整趟航程中保持低溫。
refrigerated  Refrigerated trucks deliver fresh fish to restaurants every morning.  adj. 冷藏的  冷藏車每天早上把鮮魚送到餐廳。
chilled  Chilled products must be stored below five degrees at all times.  adj. 冷藏的；冷卻的  冷藏產品必須隨時保存在五度以下。
frozen  Frozen goods should be moved into the cold room immediately.  adj. 冷凍的  冷凍貨品應立即移入冷藏室。
hazmat  Only certified drivers may transport hazmat cargo on this route.  n. 危險物品  只有具備認證的駕駛才能在這條路線上運送危險物品貨物。
corrosive  Corrosive liquids must be packed in specially sealed containers.  adj. 腐蝕性的  腐蝕性液體必須裝在特殊密封的容器中。
explosive  Explosive materials are prohibited from all passenger flights.  adj. 易爆的；n. 炸藥  所有客運航班都禁止攜帶易爆物品。
livestock  Livestock transport is regulated to protect the welfare of animals.  n. 牲畜  牲畜運輸受到管制，以保護動物福祉。
perishables  Perishables should be delivered within twenty-four hours of dispatch.  n. 易腐壞物品  易腐壞物品應在發貨後二十四小時內送達。
oversized  Oversized loads require a special permit and an escort vehicle.  adj. 超大尺寸的  超大尺寸的貨物需要特別許可證和一輛護送車。
overweight  The truck was turned back at the checkpoint because it was overweight.  adj. 超重的  這輛卡車因超重而在檢查哨被攔回。
outsized  Outsized cargo is shipped on flatbed trucks designed for heavy loads.  adj. 超大的  超大型貨物是以專為重物設計的平板卡車運送。
crane  A mobile crane lifted the container onto the waiting trailer.  n. 起重機  一台移動式起重機把貨櫃吊到等候中的拖車上。
gantry  The gantry moves along the dock to lift containers from the ship.  n. 門式起重機  門式起重機沿著碼頭移動，把貨櫃從船上吊起。
hoist  Use the hoist to raise the heavy crates onto the platform.  n. 吊車；v. 吊起  請用吊車把沉重的板條箱吊上平台。
dolly  Workers moved the boxes on a dolly to the loading bay.  n. 手推車；平板車  工人用手推車把箱子運到裝卸區。
tractor  The tractor pulled the long trailer through the busy port gate.  n. 牽引車；曳引機  牽引車拖著長長的拖車穿過繁忙的港口大門。
lorry  A lorry carrying building materials blocked the narrow street.  n. 卡車；貨車  一輛載運建材的卡車堵住了狹窄的街道。
van  Our delivery van leaves the depot at eight every morning.  n. 廂型車；貨車  我們的配送廂型車每天早上八點離開集散站。
bus  The shuttle bus runs between the airport and the main train station.  n. 公車  接駁公車往返於機場與火車總站之間。
tram  A new tram line will connect the business district to the harbor.  n. 電車；路面電車  一條新的電車路線將連接商業區與港口。
metro  The metro is the fastest way to reach the convention center.  n. 地下鐵  搭地鐵是前往會議中心最快的方式。
locomotive  A diesel locomotive pulls the freight train through the mountains.  n. 火車頭  一輛柴油火車頭拉著貨運列車穿越山區。
boxcar  Each boxcar can hold up to ninety tons of grain.  n. 有蓋貨車廂  每節有蓋貨車廂最多可裝載九十噸穀物。
railcar  The chemical firm leases fifty railcars for its monthly shipments.  n. 鐵路車廂  這家化學公司租用五十節鐵路車廂來進行每月的出貨。
freeway  The freeway was closed for two hours after a truck overturned.  n. 高速公路  一輛卡車翻覆後，高速公路封閉了兩個小時。
interstate  Interstate trucking requires drivers to follow federal safety rules.  adj. 州際的  州際貨運要求駕駛人遵守聯邦安全規定。
bypass  Trucks must use the bypass to avoid the city center.  n. 繞道；環城道路  卡車必須走環城道路以避開市中心。
junction  The warehouse is located near the junction of two major highways.  n. 交叉口；交會處  這座倉庫位於兩條主要公路的交會處附近。
roundabout  Turn left at the roundabout to reach the distribution center.  n. 圓環  在圓環處左轉即可抵達配送中心。
weighbridge  Every truck is weighed at the weighbridge before it leaves the site.  n. 地磅  每輛卡車離開場區前都要在地磅過磅。
fumigation  Wooden pallets must undergo fumigation before export.  n. 燻蒸消毒  木棧板在出口前必須經過燻蒸消毒。
phytosanitary  A phytosanitary certificate is required for all imported plants.  adj. 植物檢疫的  所有進口植物都必須附有植物檢疫證書。
origin  The certificate of origin must accompany every shipment.  n. 原產地；來源  每批貨物都必須附上原產地證明書。
provenance  Buyers want proof of provenance for all imported goods.  n. 來源；出處  買家要求所有進口商品都提供來源證明。
declaration  Please complete the customs declaration before the cargo arrives.  n. 申報；申報單  請在貨物抵達前填妥海關申報單。
entry  The goods were held up at the point of entry for two days.  n. 入境；進口  這批貨物在入境口岸被扣留了兩天。
entry  Each contest entry must be submitted by Friday.  n. 參賽作品；報名  每件參賽作品都必須在週五前繳交。
exit  The truck cleared the exit gate at six o'clock.  n. 出口；離開  卡車在六點整通過出口大門。
diversion  A road diversion caused a delay of several hours for the convoy.  n. 改道；繞行  道路改道使車隊延誤了數小時。
# ===== 17 零售與購物 =====
sale  Our annual sale begins this Friday at nine in the morning.  n. 特賣；銷售  我們的年度特賣會將於本週五早上九點開始。
boutique  She opened a small boutique selling handmade jewelry downtown.  n. 精品店  她在市中心開了一間販售手工珠寶的小型精品店。
mall  The new shopping mall will open its doors next spring.  n. 購物中心  這座新的購物中心將於明年春天開幕。
fashion  The store follows the latest fashion trends from Paris.  n. 流行；時尚  這間店追隨來自巴黎的最新流行趨勢。
gift  The store offers free gift wrapping during the holidays.  n. 禮物  假期期間，這間店提供免費禮品包裝。
wrap  Our staff will gladly wrap your purchase as a gift.  v. 包裝  我們的店員樂意將您購買的商品包裝成禮物。
fitting  Fitting rooms are located at the rear of the second floor.  n. 試衣；試穿  試衣間位於二樓後方。
counter  Please bring your items to the counter on the first floor.  n. 櫃檯  請將您的商品帶到一樓的櫃檯。
reusable  Shoppers are encouraged to bring reusable bags to every store.  adj. 可重複使用的  我們鼓勵購物者在每家店都自備可重複使用的購物袋。
luxury  The store specializes in luxury watches and leather goods.  n. 奢華；adj. 精品的  這間店專賣精品手錶與皮革製品。
authentic  All items sold here are guaranteed authentic.  adj. 正品的；真正的  這裡販售的所有商品都保證是正品。
genuine  Please check that the leather is genuine before you buy it.  adj. 真品的；真誠的  購買前請先確認皮革是真品。
counterfeit  Counterfeit handbags were seized at the border last week.  adj. 偽造的；n. 仿冒品  上週邊境查獲了一批仿冒手提包。
limited  This limited edition watch will be sold in only fifty stores.  adj. 限量的；有限的  這款限量版手錶只會在五十家門市販售。
bestseller  The new cookbook quickly became a bestseller.  n. 暢銷商品  這本新食譜很快就成為暢銷書。
feature  This model has several new features, including a larger screen.  n. 特色；功能  這款機型有多項新功能，包括更大的螢幕。
feature  The new model will feature a larger screen.  v. 以…為特色；主打  新機型將配備更大的螢幕。
in-store  In-store pickup is free for orders over thirty dollars.  adj. 店內的  訂單金額超過三十美元，可免費到店取貨。
pre-order  Customers can pre-order the new console starting on Monday.  v. 預購  顧客從週一起即可預購新款主機。
shelving  The store is installing taller shelving to display more stock.  n. 貨架  這間店正在安裝更高的貨架，以陳列更多存貨。
chain  The grocery chain announced plans to close five underperforming stores.  n. 連鎖店  這家連鎖雜貨店宣布計畫關閉五間業績不佳的門市。
supermarket  The supermarket stays open until midnight on weekends.  n. 超級市場  這家超市週末營業到午夜。
grocery  Grocery prices have risen steadily over the past year.  n. 食品雜貨  過去一年來，食品雜貨價格持續上漲。
apparel  The company sells athletic apparel in more than forty countries.  n. 服飾  這家公司在四十多個國家銷售運動服飾。
electronics  The electronics department is located on the third floor.  n. 電子產品  電子產品專區位於三樓。
cosmetics  The cosmetics counter offers free consultations every afternoon.  n. 化妝品  化妝品櫃每天下午提供免費諮詢。
franchise  The coffee franchise plans to open ten new locations next year.  n. 加盟店；連鎖經營權  這家連鎖咖啡加盟品牌計畫明年新開十個據點。
faulty  Please return the faulty headphones along with your receipt.  adj. 有缺陷的；故障的  請將有瑕疵的耳機連同收據一併退回。
crowd  A large crowd gathered outside the store before the opening.  n. 人群  開店前，店外聚集了一大群人。
queue  Shoppers formed a long queue outside the store before dawn.  n. 排隊人龍；v. 排隊  天亮前，購物者就在店外排起了長長的人龍。
cart  Items in your online cart will be saved for seven days.  n. 購物車  您網路購物車中的商品將保留七天。
basket  Please place your selected items in the basket.  n. 購物籃  請將您挑選的商品放入購物籃。
tag  Check the tag to find out how to wash the sweater.  n. 標籤；吊牌  請查看標籤，了解這件毛衣的洗滌方式。
assortment  The store offers a wide assortment of organic snacks.  n. 品項；各式商品  這間店提供種類繁多的有機零食。
outfit  She bought a complete outfit for the interview.  n. 全套服裝  她為面試買了一整套服裝。
stylish  The boutique sells stylish bags made from recycled materials.  adj. 時髦的  這間精品店販售以回收材料製成的時髦包包。
refurbished  Refurbished laptops are sold at half the original price.  adj. 翻新的  翻新筆電以原價的一半出售。
wardrobe  The store helps customers build a versatile work wardrobe.  n. 衣櫥；服裝  這間店協助顧客打造百搭的上班服裝。
mannequin  The mannequin in the window wears our latest spring jacket.  n. 人體模特兒  櫥窗裡的人體模特兒穿著我們最新的春季外套。
kiosk  Touch the kiosk screen to check prices and product information.  n. 服務亭；自助服務機  觸碰自助服務機的螢幕即可查詢價格與商品資訊。
stall  Her stall at the weekend market sells fresh flowers.  n. 攤位  她在週末市集的攤位販售鮮花。
haggle  Shoppers often haggle over prices at the open-air market.  v. 討價還價  購物者經常在露天市場討價還價。
sneaker  Hundreds of fans lined up for the new sneaker release.  n. 運動鞋  數百名粉絲排隊搶購新款運動鞋的發售。
arcade  The new shopping arcade features forty small boutiques and cafes.  n. 商店街；拱廊商場  這條新的商店街有四十間小型精品店和咖啡館。
emporium  The old emporium sells everything from tools to clothing.  n. 大型商場；百貨商店  這間老百貨商店什麼都賣，從工具到服飾應有盡有。
flagship  The brand will open its flagship store in the city center next month.  n. 旗艦店；adj. 旗艦的  該品牌將於下個月在市中心開設旗艦店。
debut  The new collection will make its debut at the spring trade show.  n. 首次亮相；v. 首度推出  新系列將在春季貿易展上首度亮相。
footfall  Footfall in the mall rose by ten percent after the renovation.  n. 客流量  整修後，購物中心的客流量增加了百分之十。
endcap  Place the promotional items on the endcap at the end of the aisle.  n. 貨架端頭陳列  請把促銷商品放在走道盡頭的端架上。
planogram  Staff must follow the planogram when restocking the shelves.  n. 商品陳列圖  員工補貨上架時必須依照商品陳列圖。
facing  Facing the shelves each evening keeps the store looking neat.  n. 整理陳列；排面  每天傍晚整理貨架排面，能讓店面保持整齊。
poster  A large poster in the window announces the weekend sale.  n. 海報  櫥窗裡的大海報宣告週末特賣。
advert  The store placed an advert in the local paper.  n. 廣告  這間店在地方報紙上刊登了廣告。
antique  The shop specializes in antique furniture from the nineteenth century.  adj. 古董的；n. 古董  這間店專營十九世紀的古董家具。
artisan  Each artisan product comes with a card describing its maker.  n. 工匠；手工藝人  每件工匠手作商品都附有一張介紹製作者的卡片。
barter  Some vendors at the market still barter goods instead of using cash.  v. 以物易物  市集上有些攤商仍以物易物，而不用現金。
bespoke  The tailor offers a bespoke service for business suits.  adj. 訂製的  這位裁縫師提供商務西裝的訂製服務。
cashless  The store is now fully cashless and accepts only cards.  adj. 無現金的  這間店現已全面無現金，只接受刷卡。
cheap  Cheap items sold in bulk often have lower quality.  adj. 便宜的；廉價的  大量販售的廉價商品往往品質較差。
inexpensive  The brand makes stylish yet inexpensive clothing for young workers.  adj. 價格低廉的  這個品牌為年輕上班族製作時髦又平價的服飾。
underpriced  The underpriced model sold out within an hour of going on sale.  adj. 定價過低的  這款定價過低的機型開賣不到一小時就售罄了。
steep  The steep price of the sofa discouraged many shoppers.  adj. 過高的；陡峭的  這張沙發的高價讓許多購物者卻步。
markdowns  Additional markdowns will be applied to winter items on Monday.  n. 降價  週一起冬季商品將再加碼降價。
preorder  Customers can preorder the new model online before its release.  v. 預購  顧客可在新機型上市前於線上預購。
presale  The presale starts at nine and is open only to loyalty members.  n. 預售  預售於九點開始，僅限會員參加。
wishlist  Add the item to your wishlist and we will notify you of any discount.  n. 願望清單  將商品加入您的願望清單，一有折扣我們就會通知您。
favorites  Save your favorites to find them quickly on your next visit.  n. 我的最愛；收藏  將商品存入我的最愛，下次造訪時就能快速找到。
imitation  Be careful not to buy imitation leather goods at that price.  n. 仿製品；adj. 仿造的  小心別用那個價錢買到仿皮製品。
fake  The company warned shoppers about fake products sold on unofficial sites.  adj. 假的；n. 仿冒品  該公司警告購物者，非官方網站上販售的商品有假貨。
knockoff  The store was fined for selling knockoff handbags.  n. 仿冒品  這間店因販售仿冒手提包而被罰款。
resale  The resale value of this brand remains high.  n. 轉售；二手販賣  這個品牌的轉售價值依然很高。
secondhand  The shop sells secondhand books at half the original price.  adj. 二手的  這間店以原價的一半販售二手書。
used  Used cars are inspected before they are placed on the lot.  adj. 二手的；用過的  二手車在上架展售前都會經過檢查。
vintage  The boutique is famous for its vintage clothing from the 1960s.  adj. 復古的；老式的  這間精品店以販售一九六○年代的復古服飾聞名。
handmade  All our candles are handmade in small batches.  adj. 手工製的  我們所有的蠟燭都是小批量手工製作。
handcrafted  Handcrafted leather bags are displayed near the entrance.  adj. 手工打造的  手工打造的皮包陳列在入口附近。
tailored  A tailored jacket makes a strong impression at interviews.  adj. 量身訂做的  量身訂做的外套能在面試中留下深刻印象。
personalized  Personalized recommendations appear on your account page after you log in.  adj. 個人化的  登入後，您的帳戶頁面會顯示個人化推薦。
customized  Customers can order customized shirts with their company logo.  adj. 客製化的  顧客可以訂購印有公司標誌的客製化襯衫。
custom  The store offers custom framing for all prints.  adj. 訂製的  這間店為所有版畫提供訂製裱框服務。
engraving  Free engraving is included with every watch purchase.  n. 雕刻；刻字  每購買一支手錶都附贈免費刻字。
monogram  We can add a monogram to your bag for a small fee.  n. 字母組合圖案  我們可以收取少許費用，在您的包包上加上字母組合圖案。
multipack  A multipack of batteries is cheaper than buying them separately.  n. 多件裝  多件裝電池比單買便宜。
combo  The lunch combo includes a sandwich, a drink, and a dessert.  n. 套餐；組合  午餐套餐包含一份三明治、一杯飲料和一份甜點。
pair  This pair of shoes is on sale for half the regular price.  n. 一雙；一對  這雙鞋正以半價特賣。
set  The kitchen set comes with six plates and matching bowls.  n. 一組；一套  這組廚房用具附有六個盤子和配套的碗。
set  Please set the alarm before leaving the office.  v. 設定；安置  離開辦公室前請先設定警報。
variety  The store carries a wide variety of organic products.  n. 多樣性；種類  這間店販售各式各樣的有機產品。
collection  The spring collection arrives in stores next Tuesday.  n. 系列商品；收藏  春季系列商品將於下週二到店。
range  The company offers a full range of home appliances.  n. 範圍；系列  該公司提供全系列的家電產品。
lineup  This year's lineup includes three new smartphones.  n. 產品陣容  今年的產品陣容包括三款新智慧型手機。
catalogue  Our new catalogue features over a thousand items.  n. 型錄；商品目錄  我們的新型錄收錄了一千多項商品。
circular  Check this week's circular for special deals on groceries.  n. 傳單；宣傳單  請查看本週的傳單，了解食品雜貨的特價優惠。
insert  An insert in the Sunday paper offers a coupon for ten percent off.  n. 夾頁廣告；v. 插入  週日報紙裡的夾頁廣告附有九折優惠券。
expires  The offer expires at midnight on Sunday.  v. 到期；失效  這項優惠將於週日午夜截止。
validity  Check the validity of your voucher before you shop.  n. 有效性；有效期限  購物前請先確認您的優惠券是否有效。
chargeback  The customer filed a chargeback after the payment was processed twice.  n. 退款爭議；拒付  款項被重複扣款後，這位顧客提出了拒付申請。
swipe  Please swipe your card through the reader to complete the payment.  v. 刷卡  請將您的卡片在讀卡機上刷過以完成付款。
tap  Customers can simply tap their phone on the terminal to pay.  v. 輕觸；感應付款  顧客只要將手機輕觸終端機即可付款。
chip  The chip on your card must be inserted into the reader before you enter your PIN.  n. 晶片  輸入密碼前，必須先把卡片上的晶片插入讀卡機。
pin  You will need your pin to approve the purchase.  n. 個人密碼  您需要輸入個人密碼才能核准這筆購買。
contactless  Contactless payment is available at every checkout counter.  adj. 非接觸式的  每個結帳櫃檯都可使用非接觸式付款。
# ===== 18 訂單發票與付款 =====
due  The final payment is due on the fifteenth of next month.  adj. 到期的；應付的  最後一筆款項應於下個月十五日繳清。
total  The total comes to four hundred and twenty dollars.  n. 總額；adj. 總計的  總額為四百二十美元。
total  The charges total two hundred dollars.  v. 總計達  這些費用總計兩百美元。
subtotal  The subtotal does not include tax or shipping fees.  n. 小計  小計不含稅金或運費。
prepay  Customers who prepay for a year receive two free months.  v. 預付  預付一年費用的顧客可多得兩個月免費使用。
check  Make the check payable to Hartman Supplies Ltd.  n. 支票；v. 檢查  請將支票抬頭開立為哈特曼供應有限公司。
term  Standard payment terms require full payment within thirty days.  n. 條款；期限  標準付款條件要求在三十天內付清全額。
adjust  The finance team will adjust the invoice to reflect the discount.  v. 調整  財務團隊會調整發票以反映折扣。
waive  The bank agreed to waive the late fee this month.  v. 免除；放棄  銀行同意本月免收滯納金。
bounce  The check bounced because there were insufficient funds in the account.  v. 退票；跳票  這張支票因帳戶餘額不足而遭退票。
receivable  Accounts receivable rose sharply in the last quarter.  n. 應收帳款  應收帳款在上一季大幅增加。
amount  The total amount due is shown at the top of the invoice.  n. 金額；數量  應付總金額列在發票最上方。
sum  The sum of the three payments was higher than expected.  n. 總和；金額  三筆款項的總和比預期還高。
recurring  Recurring payments are taken from your account on the first of each month.  adj. 定期的；經常性的  定期款項會在每月一日從您的帳戶扣款。
monthly  Please arrange monthly payments by direct debit.  adj. 每月的  請以直接扣款方式安排每月付款。
payee  The payee must sign the form before the funds are released.  n. 收款人  收款人必須在款項撥付前簽署表格。
collect  The agency will collect all overdue payments on our behalf.  v. 催收；收取  該機構將代表我們催收所有逾期款項。
grace  A grace period of five days applies to late payments.  n. 寬限  遲繳款項適用五天的寬限期。
remit  Please remit the balance to the address printed below.  v. 匯款；繳付  請將餘額匯至下列地址。
cardholder  The cardholder must sign the receipt at the time of purchase.  n. 持卡人  持卡人必須在購買時於收據上簽名。
bookkeeping  Our bookkeeping software tracks every payment automatically.  n. 記帳；簿記  我們的記帳軟體會自動追蹤每筆付款。
shortfall  There was a shortfall of two thousand dollars in the account.  n. 不足額  帳戶中短少了兩千美元。
overpay  Customers who overpay will receive a credit on their next bill.  v. 多付  多付款項的客戶將在下期帳單中獲得抵扣。
refundable  The deposit is fully refundable if you cancel before the deadline.  adj. 可退款的  若在期限前取消，訂金可全額退還。
owe  Our records show that you still owe two hundred dollars.  v. 欠款  我們的紀錄顯示您仍欠款兩百美元。
tally  Please tally the receipts and submit the total by Friday.  v. 計算；核算  請核算收據並於週五前提交總額。
payer  The payer is responsible for any transfer fees.  n. 付款人  付款人須負責任何轉帳手續費。
arrears  Tenants who fall into arrears may lose their lease.  n. 欠款；拖欠  拖欠租金的房客可能會失去租約。
mismatch  A mismatch between the two totals delayed the audit.  n. 不符；不一致  兩個總額不一致，使查帳作業延後。
prepaid  A prepaid shipping label is attached to the package for easy returns.  adj. 預付的  包裹上附有預付運費標籤，方便退貨。
nonrefundable  A nonrefundable deposit is required to secure your reservation.  adj. 不可退款的  需支付不可退還的訂金以確保您的預約。
billable  Consultants must record all billable hours in the timesheet by Friday.  adj. 可計費的  顧問必須在週五前將所有可計費工時登錄於工時表。
fulfillment  Order fulfillment usually takes two business days after payment is received.  n. 訂單履行；出貨作業  收到款項後，訂單出貨作業通常需要兩個工作天。
dues  Annual membership dues are payable by the end of January.  n. 會費  年度會費須於一月底前繳納。
consign  The manufacturer will consign the first batch of goods to the local retailer.  v. 託售；託運  製造商將把第一批貨品託售給當地零售商。
underpay  Please notify us if we underpay any invoice by mistake.  v. 少付  若我們誤將任何發票少付款項，請通知我們。
overpayment  We will credit the overpayment to your next invoice.  n. 多付的款項  我們會將多付的款項抵扣到您的下一張發票。
overage  Any overage will be billed at the standard rate.  n. 超額；超出量  任何超出的部分將按標準費率計費。
verification  Payment will be released after verification of the shipping documents.  n. 驗證；核實  貨運文件經核實後，款項才會撥付。
lump-sum  Employees may choose a lump-sum payment instead of monthly installments.  adj. 一次付清的  員工可選擇一次付清，而不採用按月分期。
advance  Customers must pay a ten percent advance when placing a custom order.  n. 預付款  客戶訂製商品時須預付百分之十的款項。
prorate  The software fee will be prorated if you cancel in the middle of the month.  v. 按比例計算  若您在月中取消，軟體費用將按比例計算。
tiered  Our tiered pricing rewards customers who place larger orders.  adj. 分級的  我們的分級定價讓大量下單的客戶享有優惠。
denomination  The machine accepts bills of any denomination up to one hundred dollars.  n. 面額  這台機器接受面額最高一百美元的任何鈔票。
undercharge  Please let us know if we undercharge you for any item.  v. 少收費用  若我們有任何品項少收了您的費用，請告知我們。
correction  A correction to the invoice total will be sent by e-mail today.  n. 更正；修正  發票總額的更正資料今天將以電子郵件寄出。
rectify  We will rectify the billing error as soon as possible.  v. 糾正；矯正  我們會盡快更正這項帳單錯誤。
packing  The packing list shows every item included in the shipment.  n. 包裝；裝箱  裝箱單列出了這批貨物中的每一項物品。
bonded  The goods are stored in a bonded warehouse until customs fees are paid.  adj. 保稅的  貨物在關稅繳清前存放於保稅倉庫。
purchasing  The purchasing department reviews every order above five thousand dollars.  n. 採購  採購部門會審核每筆超過五千美元的訂單。
payday  Payday falls on the last Friday of every month.  n. 發薪日  發薪日是每月的最後一個週五。
proceeds  The proceeds from the sale will be deposited into your account.  n. 收益；所得  銷售所得將存入您的帳戶。
forgive  The lender agreed to forgive the late fee this time.  v. 免除；寬恕  貸款機構同意這次免除滯納金。
penalize  The contract may penalize vendors who deliver late.  v. 處罰；罰款  合約可能會對延遲交貨的廠商處以罰款。
collector  A collector contacted us about the unpaid balance.  n. 催收員；收款人  一位催收員就未付餘額與我們聯絡。
goods  All goods must be inspected before the invoice is approved.  n. 貨物；商品  所有貨物都必須在發票核准前完成檢驗。
defer  You may defer the payment until the end of next quarter.  v. 延後；延期  您可以將付款延後到下一季底。
prepayment  Prepayment is required for all international orders.  n. 預付款項  所有國際訂單都需預先付款。
repayment  The repayment schedule is attached to the loan agreement.  n. 還款；償還  還款時程表附在貸款合約中。
excise  Alcohol and tobacco are subject to excise tax set by the government.  n. 貨物稅；消費稅  酒類與菸品須課徵政府訂定的貨物稅。
overbill  Customers should report it if a vendor tries to overbill them.  v. 多收帳款  若廠商試圖多收費用，客戶應該通報。
postdate  Please postdate the check to the first of next month.  v. 填寫較晚的日期  請將支票日期填為下個月一日。
tab  Please put the lunch on the client's tab.  n. 賒帳；帳單  請把午餐記在客戶的帳上。
scam  Beware of the scam e-mail asking you to update your payment details.  n. 詐騙  請小心要求您更新付款資料的詐騙電子郵件。
auction  The surplus equipment will be sold at auction next week.  n. 拍賣  多餘的設備將於下週拍賣。
repossess  The bank may repossess the vehicle if payments stop.  v. 收回；取回  若停止還款，銀行可能會收回車輛。
spending  Monthly spending on office supplies has dropped by ten percent.  n. 支出；花費  辦公用品的每月支出已下降百分之十。
wallet  A digital wallet stores your card details securely.  n. 電子錢包；皮夾  電子錢包能安全地儲存您的卡片資料。
aging  An aging report lists unpaid invoices by the number of days overdue.  adj. 帳齡的；老化的  帳齡報表依逾期天數列出未付的發票。
backdate  Please do not backdate the invoice to last month.  v. 回溯日期  請不要將發票日期回溯到上個月。
stub  Keep the stub of your check for your records.  n. 存根  請保留支票存根作為紀錄。
refusal  Refusal of delivery will result in a return fee.  n. 拒絕；拒收  拒收貨物將產生退貨費用。
interest-free  The store offers an interest-free plan for purchases over two hundred dollars.  adj. 免息的  這家店針對兩百美元以上的消費提供免息方案。
tax-free  Gifts under fifty dollars are tax-free.  adj. 免稅的  五十美元以下的禮品免稅。
e-commerce  Our e-commerce platform processes thousands of orders every day.  n. 電子商務  我們的電子商務平台每天處理數千筆訂單。
shortchange  Please check your change so the cashier does not shortchange you.  v. 少找錢  請點清找零，以免收銀員少找您錢。
marketplace  Orders placed through the online marketplace are paid by credit card.  n. 交易平台；市集  透過線上交易平台下的訂單以信用卡付款。
remitter  The remitter must include the invoice number with the transfer.  n. 匯款人  匯款人必須在轉帳時附上發票號碼。
# ===== 19 專案管理與計畫 =====
resource  The department needs additional resources to finish the project on time.  n. 資源  該部門需要額外資源才能如期完成專案。
risk  A detailed risk assessment was completed before construction started.  n. 風險  施工開始前已完成詳細的風險評估。
phase  The first phase of the project will end in June.  n. 階段  專案的第一階段將於六月結束。
task  Each team member received a clear task list at the meeting.  n. 任務  每位團隊成員在會議上都拿到了清楚的任務清單。
scope  The client asked us to expand the scope of the project.  n. 範圍  客戶要求我們擴大專案範圍。
coordinate  Ms. Lee will coordinate the efforts of the three regional offices.  v. 協調  李女士將協調三個區域辦公室的工作。
implement  The company will implement the new system in September.  v. 實施  公司將於九月實施新系統。
prioritize  Please prioritize the urgent requests from our largest clients.  v. 優先處理  請優先處理我們最大客戶提出的緊急要求。
workload  The new hires will help reduce the workload of the sales staff.  n. 工作量  新進人員將協助減輕業務人員的工作量。
setback  The loss of a key supplier was a serious setback for the team.  n. 挫折；阻礙  失去一家主要供應商對團隊是一大挫折。
contingency  We prepared a contingency plan in case the vendor misses the date.  n. 應變措施  我們準備了應變計畫，以防廠商錯過交期。
extend  The client agreed to extend the deadline by two days.  v. 延長  客戶同意將期限延長兩天。
extend  We would like to extend an invitation to all of our clients.  v. 提出；給予  我們想向所有客戶發出邀請。
deliverable  The final deliverable includes a user manual and training slides.  n. 交付成果  最終交付成果包含使用手冊與訓練簡報。
initiative  The new initiative aims to shorten the product development cycle.  n. 計畫；倡議  這項新計畫旨在縮短產品開發週期。
plan  The project plan was shared with all department heads.  n. 計畫；v. 規劃  專案計畫已與所有部門主管分享。
feasibility  A feasibility study will determine whether the project should proceed.  n. 可行性  可行性研究將決定該專案是否應繼續進行。
overrun  The costs may overrun the original estimate if prices keep rising.  v. 超支  若價格持續上漲，成本可能會超出原先的估算。
slip  Any further changes could cause the launch date to slip.  v. 延誤；落後  任何進一步的變動都可能使上市日期延後。
slip  Please sign the delivery slip before the driver leaves.  n. 單據；便條  請在司機離開前簽署送貨單。
ongoing  The ongoing construction has caused traffic delays downtown.  adj. 進行中的  進行中的施工造成市中心交通延誤。
upcoming  Details about the upcoming project will be shared at the meeting.  adj. 即將到來的  即將展開的專案細節將在會議上說明。
execute  The crew will execute the plan as soon as the permits arrive.  v. 執行  許可證一到，施工人員就會執行計畫。
execution  The success of the project depends on careful execution.  n. 執行  專案的成功取決於謹慎的執行。
kickoff  A kickoff meeting will be held on Monday to introduce the team.  n. 啟動會議  週一將舉行啟動會議來介紹團隊。
handover  The handover of the finished building is scheduled for April 3.  n. 移交  完工大樓的移交預定於四月三日。
checkpoint  Each checkpoint lets us confirm that the work is on track.  n. 查核點  每個查核點都讓我們確認工作按進度進行。
dependency  The delay is caused by a dependency on another team's work.  n. 相依關係；依賴  這次延誤是因為依賴另一個團隊的工作成果。
constraint  Time is the biggest constraint on this project.  n. 限制  時間是這個專案最大的限制。
mitigate  Extra inspections will mitigate the risk of costly errors.  v. 減輕  額外的檢查將降低造成高額損失的錯誤風險。
anticipate  We anticipate that the work will be finished by mid-June.  v. 預期  我們預期工作將在六月中旬前完成。
streamline  The new software will streamline the approval process.  v. 精簡  新軟體將精簡核准流程。
accelerate  Hiring more engineers should accelerate the development schedule.  v. 加快  增聘工程師應能加快開發進度。
hinder  Poor communication can hinder progress on a complex project.  v. 阻礙  溝通不良可能會阻礙複雜專案的進展。
obstacle  The biggest obstacle was getting approval from the city council.  n. 障礙  最大的障礙是取得市議會的核准。
contractor  The contractor will finish the electrical work by Friday.  n. 承包商  承包商將在週五前完成電力工程。
subcontractor  A subcontractor was hired to handle the plumbing.  n. 分包商  公司僱用了一家分包商來處理水管工程。
staffing  Current staffing levels are too low to meet the deadline.  n. 人員配置  目前的人員配置過少，無法趕上期限。
headcount  The headcount for the project team will remain at twelve.  n. 編制人數  專案團隊的編制人數將維持在十二人。
manpower  The project is behind schedule because of a shortage of manpower.  n. 人力  由於人力短缺，專案進度落後。
buffer  We added a two-week buffer to the schedule to cover unexpected problems.  n. 緩衝  我們在時程中加入兩週的緩衝期，以應付突發問題。
commence  Construction will commence as soon as the permit is approved.  v. 開始  許可一經核准，工程就會開始。
sign-off  The design cannot move forward without final sign-off from the client.  n. 簽核  沒有客戶的最終簽核，設計就無法繼續進行。
iteration  Each iteration of the design improves on the last one.  n. 迭代；反覆  設計的每一次迭代都比上一版更好。
duration  The duration of the project is estimated at eight months.  n. 持續期間  專案的期間預計為八個月。
interval  Progress reports are due at regular intervals during the project.  n. 間隔  專案期間須定期繳交進度報告。
sequence  Tasks must be completed in the correct sequence.  n. 順序  任務必須依正確順序完成。
status  The status of each task is shown in the table below.  n. 狀態  每項任務的狀態列在下表中。
ahead  The construction is two weeks ahead of schedule.  adv. 提前；領先  工程進度比預定時程提前兩週。
undertake  The firm agreed to undertake the renovation of the old library.  v. 承擔；著手進行  該公司同意承接舊圖書館的整修工程。
initiate  The director will initiate the review process next month.  v. 發起  主任將於下個月啟動審查程序。
sprint  The development team will complete the next sprint by the end of this week.  n. 衝刺週期  開發團隊將在本週底前完成下一個衝刺週期。
scrum  Our daily scrum lasts only fifteen minutes.  n. 每日站會；敏捷開發會議  我們的每日站會只進行十五分鐘。
baseline  The baseline schedule was approved by all stakeholders last month.  n. 基準；基線  基準時程已在上個月獲得所有利害關係人核准。
charter  The project charter outlines the goals, budget, and team members.  n. 專案章程  專案章程概述了目標、預算與團隊成員。
roadmap  The product roadmap shows major releases over the next two years.  n. 發展藍圖；路線圖  產品路線圖列出未來兩年的主要發行項目。
stage  The project is now in the final stage of testing.  n. 階段  專案目前處於測試的最後階段。
wrap-up  A short wrap-up meeting will be held after the final review.  n. 總結；收尾  最終審查後將舉行簡短的總結會議。
effort  The team put significant effort into meeting the original deadline.  n. 努力；工作量  團隊投入大量心力以趕上原訂期限。
velocity  Team velocity increased after we simplified the approval process.  n. 開發速度；速率  我們簡化核准流程後，團隊的開發速度提高了。
retrospective  We held a retrospective to discuss what went well and what did not.  n. 回顧會議  我們召開了回顧會議，討論哪些做得好、哪些不好。
timeframe  The client agreed to a six-month timeframe for the project.  n. 時程；期限  客戶同意這個專案的時程為六個月。
slippage  Further slippage in the schedule could delay the product launch.  n. 進度落後  進度若再落後，可能會延後產品上市。
postponement  The postponement of the launch gave engineers more time to test.  n. 延期  上市延期讓工程師有更多時間測試。
implementation  The implementation of the new system will begin in January.  n. 實施；執行  新系統的實施將於一月開始。
closure  Project closure includes a final report and a review of lessons learned.  n. 結案；收尾  專案結案包括提交最終報告與檢討經驗教訓。
parallel  The two teams will work in parallel to save time.  adj. 平行的；同時進行的  兩個團隊將同時並行作業以節省時間。
concurrent  Several concurrent projects are competing for the same engineers.  adj. 同時發生的  好幾個同時進行的專案在爭搶同一批工程師。
sequential  Sequential tasks must be completed in a specific order.  adj. 循序的  循序任務必須依特定順序完成。
trigger  A missed milestone will trigger an automatic review by the committee.  v. 觸發；引發  錯過里程碑將自動觸發委員會的審查。
assumption  The budget is based on the assumption that costs will remain stable.  n. 假設  這份預算是以成本將維持穩定的假設為基礎。
acceptance  Final acceptance of the software depends on a successful user test.  n. 驗收；接受  軟體的最終驗收取決於使用者測試是否成功。
blocker  The missing permit is the main blocker for the construction phase.  n. 阻礙事項  缺少許可證是施工階段的主要阻礙事項。
impediment  Limited staff is the biggest impediment to completing the work on time.  n. 阻礙；障礙  人手有限是如期完成工作的最大阻礙。
hurdle  Securing funding was the first major hurdle for the project.  n. 難關；障礙  取得資金是這個專案的第一個重大難關。
pitfall  One common pitfall is failing to define the scope clearly.  n. 陷阱；隱藏的困難  一個常見的陷阱是沒有清楚界定範圍。
snag  We hit a snag when the supplier changed the delivery date.  n. 意外障礙  供應商更改交貨日期時，我們遇到了意外障礙。
complication  An unexpected complication delayed the installation by two days.  n. 複雜因素；併發問題  一個意外的複雜狀況使安裝延後了兩天。
workaround  The engineers found a workaround to keep the system running.  n. 權宜解決方案  工程師找到了讓系統繼續運作的權宜辦法。
fallback  We have a fallback plan in case the vendor misses the deadline.  n. 備用方案  我們有備用方案，以防廠商錯過期限。
overload  Adding more tasks could overload the team and reduce quality.  v. 使超載  增加更多任務可能使團隊負荷過重並降低品質。
overcommit  Managers should not overcommit the team during a busy season.  v. 承諾過多  旺季期間，主管不應對團隊承諾過多。
ramp-up  The ramp-up period for new staff is about four weeks.  n. 逐步提升期；上手期  新進人員的上手期約為四週。
incremental  The software will be released in incremental stages.  adj. 漸進的；逐步增加的  該軟體將分階段逐步發行。
iterative  An iterative approach lets us improve the design after each test.  adj. 反覆的；迭代的  迭代式做法讓我們能在每次測試後改進設計。
modular  A modular design makes the system easier to update.  adj. 模組化的  模組化設計使系統更容易更新。
scalable  We need a scalable solution that can handle future growth.  adj. 可擴充的  我們需要能因應未來成長的可擴充解決方案。
scalability  The report highlights concerns about the scalability of the platform.  n. 可擴充性  報告強調了對該平台可擴充性的疑慮。
synchronize  Please synchronize your calendars so everyone sees the new deadlines.  v. 使同步  請同步你們的行事曆，讓每個人都能看到新的期限。
underway  Construction of the new office is already underway.  adj. 進行中的  新辦公室的工程已經在進行中。
completion  The expected completion date is the end of March.  n. 完成  預計完成日期是三月底。
accomplish  The team managed to accomplish all of its goals ahead of schedule.  v. 完成；達成  團隊成功提前達成所有目標。
accomplishment  Finishing the project under budget was a major accomplishment.  n. 成就；完成的事  專案在預算內完成是一項重大成就。
interim  An interim report will be submitted at the halfway point.  adj. 中期的；暫時的  中期報告將在進度過半時提交。
revisit  We should revisit the timeline once the budget is confirmed.  v. 重新檢視  預算確認後，我們應該重新檢視時程。
reassign  The manager decided to reassign two engineers to the urgent project.  v. 重新指派  經理決定將兩名工程師改派到緊急專案。
redefine  The client asked us to redefine the scope of the work.  v. 重新定義  客戶要求我們重新界定工作範圍。
lifecycle  The lifecycle of the project includes planning, execution, and closure.  n. 生命週期  專案的生命週期包括規劃、執行與結案。
contingent  The launch date is contingent on the results of the final test.  adj. 視情況而定的  上市日期取決於最終測試的結果。
reallocate  We may need to reallocate funds to cover the extra testing.  v. 重新分配  我們可能需要重新分配資金來支應額外的測試。
compress  The team had to compress the testing phase to meet the launch date.  v. 壓縮  為趕上上市日期，團隊必須壓縮測試階段。
lag  A lag in approvals delayed the construction project.  n. 延遲；落差  核准作業的延遲拖慢了建設專案。
# ===== 20 管理策略與績效 =====
growth  The firm reported strong growth in the last quarter.  n. 成長  該公司回報上一季有強勁的成長。
improve  The training program should improve customer service skills.  v. 改善  這項訓練計畫應能提升客服技能。
optimize  Engineers worked to optimize the production line.  v. 最佳化  工程師致力於將生產線最佳化。
motivate  A clear vision can motivate employees to work harder.  v. 激勵  明確的願景能激勵員工更努力工作。
vision  The CEO shared her vision for the future of the company.  n. 願景  執行長分享了她對公司未來的願景。
mission  Our mission is to provide affordable healthcare to every family.  n. 使命  我們的使命是為每個家庭提供負擔得起的醫療照護。
innovation  Constant innovation is essential if we want to stay ahead of rivals.  n. 創新  想要領先對手，就必須不斷創新。
innovate  Companies must innovate constantly to survive in this market.  v. 創新  企業必須不斷創新，才能在這個市場存活。
transform  The new CEO hopes to transform the company culture.  v. 轉型；改變  新任執行長希望能轉變公司文化。
transition  The transition to remote work went smoothly for most teams.  n. 轉變；過渡  大多數團隊順利轉換為遠端工作。
merger  The merger created the largest logistics firm in the region.  n. 合併  這次合併造就了該地區最大的物流公司。
acquisition  The acquisition of the smaller rival doubled our customer base.  n. 收購  收購規模較小的對手使我們的客戶群倍增。
advantage  Our fast delivery gives us a clear advantage over competitors.  n. 優勢  我們的快速交貨讓我們明顯勝過競爭對手。
edge  Advanced technology gives the firm an edge in the industry.  n. 優勢  先進技術讓這家公司在業界取得優勢。
maximize  The goal is to maximize profits while keeping costs low.  v. 使最大化  目標是在壓低成本的同時使獲利最大化。
minimize  We must minimize waste to protect our margins.  v. 使最小化  我們必須減少浪費以保護利潤。
sustain  It is hard to sustain such rapid expansion without new funding.  v. 維持  沒有新資金，很難維持如此快速的擴張。
profitability  Higher prices improved the profitability of the product line.  n. 獲利能力  提高售價改善了這條產品線的獲利能力。
indicator  Rising sales are a good indicator of customer demand.  n. 指標  銷售成長是顯示客戶需求的良好指標。
achieve  The team worked hard to achieve its annual goals.  v. 達成  團隊努力工作以達成年度目標。
exceed  Results this quarter exceed our expectations.  v. 超過  本季的成果超出我們的預期。
outperform  Our new fund continues to outperform the market average.  v. 表現勝過  我們的新基金持續表現優於市場平均。
surpass  Revenue this year will surpass last year's total.  v. 超越  今年的營收將超越去年的總額。
underperform  Several branches continue to underperform compared with last year.  v. 表現不佳  幾家分公司的表現持續不如去年。
accountability  Clear goals create greater accountability among employees.  n. 問責  明確的目標能提高員工之間的問責意識。
collaborate  Teams from sales and design collaborate on every campaign.  v. 合作  業務與設計團隊在每次活動中都密切合作。
synergy  The merger created synergy between the two research teams.  n. 綜效  這次合併在兩個研究團隊之間創造了綜效。
align  Managers must align team goals with company strategy.  v. 使一致  主管必須使團隊目標與公司策略一致。
alignment  Better alignment between departments is critical for success.  n. 一致；協調  各部門之間更好的協調對成功至關重要。
agile  An agile team can respond quickly to market changes.  adj. 敏捷的  敏捷的團隊能對市場變化迅速做出反應。
adapt  Companies must adapt quickly to changing customer needs.  v. 適應  企業必須迅速適應不斷變化的客戶需求。
resilient  A resilient business can recover quickly from economic shocks.  adj. 有韌性的  有韌性的企業能從經濟衝擊中迅速恢復。
centralize  The firm will centralize purchasing to cut costs.  v. 集中  公司將集中採購以降低成本。
standardize  We aim to standardize procedures across all branches.  v. 標準化  我們的目標是讓所有分公司的作業流程標準化。
reform  The board proposed a major reform of the pay structure.  n. 改革；v. 改良  董事會提出了薪資結構的重大改革。
revamp  The retailer plans to revamp its online store.  v. 改造  這家零售商計畫改造其網路商店。
pioneer  The company was a pioneer in mobile banking.  n. 先驅；v. 開創  這家公司是行動銀行的先驅。
emerge  New competitors continue to emerge in the market.  v. 出現；崛起  市場上持續有新的競爭者出現。
thrive  Small businesses can thrive with the right support.  v. 蓬勃發展  小型企業在獲得適當支持下可以蓬勃發展。
flourish  The startup began to flourish after it entered the Asian market.  v. 繁榮  這家新創公司進入亞洲市場後開始蓬勃成長。
stagnant  Sales have remained stagnant for the past three quarters.  adj. 停滯的  過去三季的銷售額一直停滯不前。
downturn  The economic downturn reduced demand for luxury goods.  n. 衰退  經濟衰退降低了對奢侈品的需求。
boost  The new ad campaign should boost sales this holiday season.  v. 提振；n. 提升  新的廣告活動應能在這個假期旺季提振銷售。
surge  Online orders saw a surge during the holiday period.  n. 激增  假期期間網路訂單出現激增。
plateau  Sales have reached a plateau after months of steady growth.  n. 高原期；停滯期  銷售額在數個月穩定成長後進入了停滯期。
momentum  The company is gaining momentum in the European market.  n. 動能  這家公司在歐洲市場正逐漸累積動能。
projection  Sales projections for next year look very promising.  n. 預測  明年的銷售預測看起來非常樂觀。
outlook  The outlook for the retail industry remains positive.  n. 前景  零售業的前景依然樂觀。
emphasis  The new strategy places greater emphasis on customer service.  n. 重點；強調  新策略更加重視客戶服務。
focus  The firm will focus on cutting costs this year.  v. 專注；n. 焦點  公司今年將專注於降低成本。
core  Customer service is a core part of our business model.  adj. 核心的  客戶服務是我們商業模式的核心部分。
strengthen  We must strengthen our presence in the Asian market.  v. 加強  我們必須加強在亞洲市場的布局。
excel  Employees who excel in their roles will receive a bonus.  v. 表現優異  在職務上表現優異的員工將獲得獎金。
governance  Good governance requires clear rules and regular reporting to the board.  n. 公司治理；治理  良好的治理需要明確的規則以及定期向董事會報告。
oversight  The committee provides oversight of all major spending decisions.  n. 監督；督導  委員會負責監督所有重大支出決策。
directive  The directive requires every department to cut travel costs by ten percent.  n. 指令；指示  這項指令要求每個部門將差旅費用減少百分之十。
mandate  The board gave the new CEO a mandate to reduce costs.  n. 授權；任務  董事會授權新任執行長削減成本。
empowerment  Employee empowerment often leads to faster decisions and higher morale.  n. 賦權；授權  員工賦權往往能帶來更快的決策與更高的士氣。
culture  A strong company culture helps attract and retain talented workers.  n. 企業文化  強大的企業文化有助於吸引並留住人才。
cohesion  Team cohesion improved after the new manager clarified everyone's roles.  n. 凝聚力  新主管釐清每個人的角色後，團隊凝聚力提高了。
autonomy  Staff are given more autonomy to manage their own schedules.  n. 自主權  員工獲得更多自主權來安排自己的時程。
micromanage  Managers who micromanage their staff often reduce creativity.  v. 事無鉅細地管控  事事插手管控員工的主管往往會削弱創造力。
delegation  Effective delegation frees managers to focus on strategic issues.  n. 授權；委派  有效的授權讓主管得以專心處理策略性議題。
buy-in  Senior buy-in is essential before we launch the new policy.  n. 認同；支持  在推行新政策之前，高層的認同是不可或缺的。
decentralize  The firm plans to decentralize decision making across its regional offices.  v. 分權；下放權力  公司計畫將決策權下放到各地區辦事處。
transformation  The digital transformation has changed how every department works.  n. 轉型；轉變  數位轉型改變了每個部門的工作方式。
pivot  The startup had to pivot to a new business model.  v. 轉向；轉型  這家新創公司不得不轉向新的商業模式。
disrupt  New technology can disrupt traditional business models.  v. 顛覆；擾亂  新技術可能顛覆傳統的商業模式。
expansion  The expansion into Asian markets began last year.  n. 擴張；擴展  向亞洲市場的擴張從去年開始。
dominance  The company's dominance in the market is being challenged by new rivals.  n. 主導地位  這家公司的市場主導地位正受到新對手的挑戰。
dominate  Two large firms dominate the online retail industry.  v. 主導；佔據優勢  兩家大型公司主導著網路零售業。
capability  The new system improves our reporting capability.  n. 能力；功能  新系統提升了我們的報表能力。
insight  The survey provided valuable insight into customer needs.  n. 洞察；見解  這項調查提供了對客戶需求的寶貴見解。
scorecard  The balanced scorecard tracks financial and customer results each quarter.  n. 績效評分表  平衡計分卡每季追蹤財務與客戶方面的成果。
yardstick  Profit alone is not the only yardstick of success.  n. 衡量標準  利潤並非衡量成功的唯一標準。
rationalize  The firm will rationalize its product line to cut costs.  v. 合理化；精簡  公司將精簡產品線以降低成本。
divest  The company plans to divest its unprofitable retail division.  v. 出售；撤資  公司計畫出售其不賺錢的零售部門。
spin-off  The software unit became a separate spin-off last year.  n. 分拆出來的公司  軟體部門去年分拆成為一家獨立的公司。
stewardship  The CEO was praised for her careful stewardship of company resources.  n. 管理；託管  執行長因謹慎管理公司資源而受到讚揚。
corporate  The corporate office announced a new hiring policy.  adj. 公司的；企業的  公司總部宣布了新的僱用政策。
enterprise  The enterprise has grown rapidly over the past five years.  n. 企業；事業  這家企業在過去五年快速成長。
organizational  Organizational change is difficult without support from senior leaders.  adj. 組織的  沒有高階主管的支持，組織變革很困難。
tenure  Her tenure as director saw sales double.  n. 任期  在她擔任主任的任期內，銷售額成長了一倍。
managerial  Applicants must have at least three years of managerial experience.  adj. 管理的  應徵者必須具備至少三年的管理經驗。
prerogative  Setting the budget is the prerogative of senior management.  n. 特權；專屬權限  編列預算是高階管理層的專屬權限。
discretion  Managers have the discretion to approve small expenses.  n. 裁量權  主管有權裁量核准小額開支。
diligence  The team showed great diligence in preparing the annual report.  n. 勤勉；盡職調查  團隊在準備年度報告時展現了極大的勤奮。
proactive  We need a proactive approach to managing customer complaints.  adj. 積極主動的  我們需要以積極主動的方式處理客戶投訴。
visionary  The visionary founder expanded the firm into three new countries.  adj. 有遠見的  這位有遠見的創辦人將公司拓展到三個新國家。
decisive  Decisive action by management saved the company from losses.  adj. 果斷的  管理階層的果斷行動使公司免於虧損。
pragmatic  The new director takes a pragmatic approach to problem solving.  adj. 務實的  新任主管以務實的方式解決問題。
strategic  The merger is a strategic move to enter the European market.  adj. 策略性的  這次合併是進軍歐洲市場的策略性舉措。
tactic  Lowering prices is a short-term tactic rather than a long-term plan.  n. 戰術；策略手段  降價是短期戰術，而非長期計畫。
holistic  We need a holistic view of how each department affects overall results.  adj. 整體的  我們需要從整體角度了解各部門如何影響整體成果。
overarching  The overarching goal is to improve customer satisfaction.  adj. 首要的；涵蓋全面的  首要目標是提高客戶滿意度。
succession  Succession planning prepares future leaders for senior roles.  n. 接班；繼任  接班規劃讓未來的領導者為高階職位做好準備。
ethos  The company's ethos is built on honesty and teamwork.  n. 企業精神；風氣  公司的企業精神建立在誠信與團隊合作之上。
analysis  The analysis shows that profits rose after the restructuring.  n. 分析  分析顯示，重組之後利潤上升了。
resourceful  A resourceful manager can deliver good results with limited funds.  adj. 足智多謀的  足智多謀的經理能用有限的資金創造良好成果。
cost-cutting  The cost-cutting plan will save the company two million dollars.  adj. 削減成本的  這項削減成本計畫將為公司節省兩百萬美元。
# ===== 21 談判合約與協議 =====
party  Either party may cancel the agreement with thirty days' notice.  n. 當事人  任何一方當事人均可提前三十天通知取消協議。
party  The company will hold a retirement party for Mr. Cho on Friday.  n. 派對  公司將於週五為趙先生舉辦退休派對。
termination  The termination of the contract was announced yesterday.  n. 終止  合約的終止已於昨天宣布。
breach  Late delivery would be a breach of the contract.  n. 違反；v. 違約  延遲交貨將構成違反合約。
obligation  Each party has an obligation to protect confidential information.  n. 義務  各方都有義務保護機密資訊。
provision  A provision in the lease allows early termination.  n. 條款；規定  租約中的一項條款允許提前終止。
stipulation  One stipulation of the deal is that the staff keep their jobs.  n. 約定條件  這筆交易的一項約定條件是員工保住工作。
concession  The union made a concession on working hours.  n. 讓步  工會在工作時數上做出了讓步。
counteroffer  We received a counteroffer from the supplier this morning.  n. 還價  我們今天早上收到了供應商的還價。
enforceable  The clause is not enforceable under local law.  adj. 可強制執行的  依當地法律，這項條款不具強制執行力。
expiration  Please check the expiration date on your insurance policy.  n. 到期  請確認您的保單到期日。
addendum  The addendum extends the contract by another six months.  n. 附錄  這份附錄將合約再延長六個月。
supersede  This agreement will supersede all earlier contracts.  v. 取代  本協議將取代所有先前的合約。
exclusivity  The deal gives the retailer exclusivity for one year.  n. 獨家權  這項交易給予該零售商一年的獨家權。
mediate  A neutral expert was asked to mediate between the two sides.  v. 調解  雙方請了一位中立的專家居中調解。
signatory  Each signatory must provide a valid photo ID.  n. 簽署人  每位簽署人都必須出示有效的附照片身分證件。
undersigned  The undersigned agrees to pay the full amount by June.  n. 簽署人  簽署人同意在六月前支付全額款項。
ratify  Members will vote to ratify the new labor agreement.  v. 批准  會員將投票批准新的勞動協議。
consent  Written consent from the owner is required.  n. 同意  需要取得所有人的書面同意。
mutual  The two firms signed a mutual agreement to share research.  adj. 雙方的；相互的  兩家公司簽署了共享研究成果的雙方協議。
reciprocal  The two countries signed a reciprocal trade agreement.  adj. 互惠的  兩國簽署了互惠貿易協定。
insist  The buyer insisted on a full refund.  v. 堅持  買方堅持要求全額退款。
deadlock  Talks reached a deadlock over the pricing structure.  n. 僵局  談判在價格結構上陷入僵局。
impasse  Negotiators reached an impasse over the final price.  n. 僵局  談判代表在最終價格上陷入僵局。
favorable  The supplier offered more favorable payment terms.  adj. 有利的  供應商提供了更有利的付款條件。
unfavorable  The buyer rejected the unfavorable conditions in the draft.  adj. 不利的  買方拒絕了草案中的不利條件。
retainer  The law firm requires a monthly retainer of two thousand dollars.  n. 聘用費  這家律師事務所每月要收取兩千美元的聘用費。
forfeit  The buyer will forfeit the deposit if the sale is canceled.  v. 沒收；喪失  如果交易取消，買方將喪失訂金。
remedy  The contract describes the remedy for late delivery.  n. 補救辦法  合約載明了延遲交貨的補救辦法。
recourse  The buyer has no legal recourse if the goods are damaged.  n. 求償權  如果貨物受損，買方沒有法律上的求償權。
tenant  The tenant must give sixty days' notice before moving out.  n. 承租人  承租人搬離前必須提前六十天通知。
landlord  The landlord agreed to repair the heating system.  n. 房東  房東同意修理暖氣系統。
affiliate  The firm is an affiliate of a major international bank.  n. 關係企業  這家公司是一家大型國際銀行的關係企業。
partnership  The two firms formed a partnership to develop the software.  n. 合夥；合作  兩家公司結成夥伴關係來開發這套軟體。
alliance  The airlines formed an alliance to share routes.  n. 聯盟  這些航空公司組成聯盟以共享航線。
joint  The two firms announced a joint venture in Brazil.  adj. 聯合的  兩家公司宣布在巴西成立合資企業。
exempt  Registered nonprofits are exempt from this annual fee.  adj. 免除的  登記有案的非營利組織免繳這項年費。
exception  The manager made an exception to the policy.  n. 例外  經理對這項政策破例處理。
specify  The contract must specify the delivery date.  v. 明確規定  合約必須明確規定交貨日期。
bind  The agreement will bind both companies for five years.  v. 約束  這項協議將約束兩家公司五年。
renegotiate  The firm wants to renegotiate the terms of its lease.  v. 重新協商  公司想重新協商租約條款。
concede  The supplier agreed to concede on price if we extend the contract by two years.  v. 讓步；承認  如果我們把合約延長兩年，供應商同意在價格上讓步。
commit  Both firms must commit to the delivery schedule before the agreement is signed.  v. 承諾；致力於  在協議簽署之前，兩家公司都必須承諾遵守交貨時程。
condition  One condition of the sale is that the buyer completes payment within thirty days.  n. 條件；條款  這筆買賣的一項條件是買方須在三十天內付清款項。
consideration  The contract states that the payment is the consideration for the services provided.  n. 對價；考量  合約載明這筆款項是所提供服務的對價。
confidentiality  All staff must sign a confidentiality agreement before viewing the merger documents.  n. 保密；機密性  所有員工在查閱合併文件前都必須簽署保密協議。
nondisclosure  The nondisclosure clause prevents either side from sharing the pricing details.  n. 保密義務  保密條款禁止任何一方透露價格細節。
rescind  The board voted to rescind the offer after reviewing the revised financial report.  v. 撤銷；廢止  董事會在審閱修訂後的財務報告後投票撤銷了這項報價。
counterparty  Our legal team is reviewing the draft sent by the counterparty this morning.  n. 交易對手  我們的法務團隊正在審閱交易對手今天早上寄來的草案。
proviso  The deal is valid with one proviso, which is that all permits must be renewed.  n. 但書；附帶條件  這筆交易有效，但附帶一項條件，就是所有許可證都必須更新。
covenant  The loan includes a covenant that limits how much new debt the company can take.  n. 契約條款；約定  這筆貸款包含一項約定條款，限制公司可新增的債務金額。
novation  A novation will transfer the obligations of the old supplier to the new one.  n. 契約更替  契約更替會將舊供應商的義務轉移給新供應商。
redline  Please redline any changes to the draft so that everyone can see them easily.  v. 以紅筆標示修改  請在草案上以紅線標示所有修改，讓大家都能輕鬆看到。
licensor  The licensor retains ownership of the software while granting usage rights to the client.  n. 授權人  授權人在授予客戶使用權的同時保有軟體的所有權。
licensee  As the licensee, our firm may use the trademark only within the Asian market.  n. 被授權人  身為被授權人，我們公司只能在亞洲市場內使用這個商標。
lessor  The lessor must repair the roof before the new lease period begins.  n. 出租人  出租人必須在新租期開始前修好屋頂。
lessee  The lessee is responsible for paying the utility bills each month.  n. 承租人  承租人負責每月支付水電費帳單。
consortium  A consortium of three local firms won the contract to build the bridge.  n. 聯合企業；財團  由三家當地公司組成的聯合企業贏得了建造這座橋的合約。
bilateral  The two nations signed a bilateral trade agreement to reduce import taxes.  adj. 雙邊的  兩國簽署了雙邊貿易協定以降低進口稅。
unilateral  A unilateral change to the terms is not allowed under this agreement.  adj. 單方面的  依本協議，不得單方面變更條款。
standoff  The talks ended in a standoff because neither side would lower its demands.  n. 僵局；對峙  由於雙方都不肯降低要求，談判以僵局收場。
mediator  An independent mediator helped the union and management reach a compromise.  n. 調解人  一位獨立的調解人協助工會與資方達成妥協。
arbitrator  The arbitrator will issue a final decision within sixty days of the hearing.  n. 仲裁人  仲裁人將在聽證會後六十天內做出最終裁決。
hereunder  All payments due hereunder shall be made in U.S. dollars.  adv. 在本契約下  本契約下應付的所有款項均須以美元支付。
thereby  The company signed the lease, thereby gaining access to the entire second floor.  adv. 藉此；因此  公司簽下了租約，藉此取得整個二樓的使用權。
aforementioned  The aforementioned terms will remain in effect until the end of the year.  adj. 前述的  前述條款將持續有效到年底。
pertain  These rules pertain only to contracts signed after January first.  v. 與...有關；適用於  這些規定僅適用於一月一日以後簽訂的合約。
entail  The new agreement will entail higher costs for the shipping department.  v. 需要；牽涉  新協議將使運輸部門的成本增加。
accord  The two firms reached an accord on the schedule after a long meeting.  n. 協議；一致  兩家公司在開了很久的會之後就時程達成協議。
pact  The companies signed a pact to share research data for the next five years.  n. 協定；盟約  這些公司簽署協定，在未來五年共享研究資料。
treaty  The trade treaty removed several tariffs on imported electronics.  n. 條約  這項貿易條約取消了多項進口電子產品的關稅。
truce  Both sides agreed to a truce while the lawyers reviewed the revised terms.  n. 休戰；暫時停火  在律師審閱修訂條款期間，雙方同意暫時休戰。
assurance  We need written assurance that the supplier will meet the quality standards.  n. 保證；確保  我們需要供應商會達到品質標準的書面保證。
disclaimer  A disclaimer at the bottom of the page limits the company's responsibility.  n. 免責聲明  頁面底部的免責聲明限制了公司的責任。
caveat  There is one caveat: the discount applies only to bulk orders.  n. 附帶條件；警告  有一項附帶條件：折扣僅適用於大量訂購。
invalid  The contract is invalid without the signature of a company director.  adj. 無效的  這份合約若沒有公司董事的簽名就是無效的。
null  If either party fails to sign, the agreement will be null and void.  adj. 無效的  如果任何一方未簽署，協議將無效。
cure  The vendor has ten days to cure the breach or the contract will end.  v. 補救；糾正  供應商有十天時間補救違約情事，否則合約將終止。
remediate  The contractor agreed to remediate the defects at no additional cost.  v. 補救；修復  承包商同意免費修復這些瑕疵。
liquidated  The contract includes liquidated damages of five hundred dollars per day of delay.  adj. 預定賠償的  合約包含每延遲一天賠償五百美元的預定違約金。
rider  A rider attached to the policy extends coverage to include overseas travel.  n. 附加條款  附加在保單上的附約將保障範圍擴大到海外旅遊。
negotiator  Our lead negotiator has twenty years of experience in international trade deals.  n. 談判者  我們的首席談判代表擁有二十年的國際貿易交易經驗。
proposition  The client found our proposition attractive and asked for a formal quotation.  n. 提案；建議  客戶覺得我們的提案很有吸引力，並要求提供正式報價。
counterproposal  The supplier rejected our offer and sent a counterproposal on Tuesday.  n. 反提案  供應商拒絕了我們的報價，並在週二送來一份反提案。
offeror  The offeror may withdraw the offer at any time before it is accepted.  n. 要約人  要約人可在要約被接受之前隨時撤回。
offeree  The offeree has until Friday to respond to the proposed terms.  n. 受要約人  受要約人必須在週五前回覆所提議的條款。
obligate  This clause does not obligate either party to renew the agreement.  v. 使負有義務  這項條款並不要求任何一方續約。
subcontract  The main builder chose to subcontract the electrical work to a local firm.  v. 轉包；分包  主要承造商選擇將電力工程轉包給當地一家公司。
leeway  The contract gives us some leeway in choosing the delivery date.  n. 彈性空間；餘地  合約在選擇交貨日期上給了我們一些彈性空間。
latitude  Our manager has considerable latitude to adjust the terms during negotiations.  n. 自由度；權限  我們的經理在談判期間有相當大的權限可調整條款。
concur  The legal department will concur with the changes if the wording is clear.  v. 同意；意見一致  如果措辭清楚，法務部門將同意這些修改。
dissent  One director voiced dissent over the proposed terms of the merger.  n. 異議；不同意見  一位董事對合併的擬議條款表示異議。
stance  The union softened its stance after the company improved its offer.  n. 立場；態度  公司提高報價後，工會軟化了立場。
reciprocate  We hope the supplier will reciprocate by offering better delivery terms.  v. 回報；互惠  我們希望供應商能以提供更好的交貨條件作為回報。
grantor  The grantor transfers the rights to the property once all payments are received.  n. 授予人；讓與人  讓與人在收到所有款項後便會轉讓該不動產的權利。
grantee  The grantee must use the land only for agricultural purposes.  n. 受讓人；被授予人  受讓人只能將這塊土地用於農業用途。
assignee  The assignee takes over all rights and duties under the original agreement.  n. 受讓人  受讓人承接原協議下的所有權利與義務。
litigate  Both companies would prefer to settle rather than litigate for years.  v. 提起訴訟；打官司  兩家公司都寧可和解，也不願打好幾年的官司。
undertaking  The firm gave a written undertaking to repair any damage within a week.  n. 承諾；保證  公司提出書面承諾，將在一週內修復任何損壞。
# ===== 22 差旅與交通 =====

festival  The city's food festival attracts thousands of visitors every autumn.  n. 節慶；慶典  這座城市的美食節每年秋天都吸引數千名遊客。

booth  Please visit our booth to see the latest product demonstration.  n. 攤位  請來我們的攤位觀看最新的產品展示。
convention  The annual convention draws thousands of engineers from around the world.  n. 大會；慣例  年度大會吸引了來自世界各地的數千名工程師。
attendee  Each attendee will receive a name badge and a program.  n. 與會者  每位與會者都會收到一個名牌和一份節目表。
admission  Admission to the exhibit is free for members.  n. 入場；入場費  會員可免費入場參觀展覽。
concert  Tickets for the outdoor concert sold out within an hour.  n. 音樂會  戶外音樂會的門票在一小時內就銷售一空。
theater  The new theater seats nearly twelve hundred people.  n. 劇院  這座新劇院可容納近一千二百人。
rehearsal  The final rehearsal for the awards show is on Friday.  n. 彩排  頒獎典禮的最後一次彩排在週五。
entertainment  Live entertainment will be provided throughout the evening.  n. 娛樂  晚上將全程有現場娛樂表演。
recreation  The hotel provides recreation facilities including tennis courts.  n. 休閒活動  飯店提供包括網球場在內的休閒設施。
spa  Guests can relax at the hotel spa after a long day.  n. 水療中心  賓客可在漫長的一天後到飯店水療中心放鬆。
tour  The guide will lead a walking tour of the old town.  n. 導覽；旅行  導遊將帶領大家進行老城區的徒步導覽。
raffle  Every ticket buyer will be entered in the raffle for a free trip.  n. 抽獎  每位購票者都將參加抽獎，有機會贏得免費旅行。
mingle  Guests can mingle over drinks before the dinner starts.  v. 交際；交談  賓客可以在晚宴開始前一邊喝飲料一邊交談。
toast  The manager raised a toast to the success of the project.  n. 祝酒  經理舉杯祝賀專案成功。
host  Our company will host the annual dinner this year.  v. 主辦；招待  今年將由我們公司主辦年度晚宴。
refreshment  Light refreshments will be served during the break.  n. 茶點  休息時間會供應輕食茶點。
snack  Healthy snacks are available in the break room.  n. 點心  休息室裡有健康的點心。
dessert  The chef's chocolate dessert is a customer favorite.  n. 甜點  主廚做的巧克力甜點深受顧客喜愛。
seating  Outdoor seating is available when the weather is nice.  n. 座位安排  天氣好的時候有戶外座位。
decor  The elegant decor makes the lobby feel warm and inviting.  n. 裝潢；擺設  典雅的裝潢讓大廳顯得溫暖又迷人。
ambiance  Diners praised the relaxed ambiance of the rooftop restaurant.  n. 氛圍  用餐的客人稱讚屋頂餐廳輕鬆的氛圍。
outdoor  The outdoor event will be postponed if it rains.  adj. 戶外的  如果下雨，戶外活動將延期。
decorate  Volunteers will decorate the hall before the party.  v. 裝飾  志工將在派對前布置會場。
lodging  The company will cover lodging for up to three nights during the training trip.  n. 住宿  公司將負擔培訓旅行期間最多三晚的住宿費。
hotel  Our hotel is located within walking distance of the exhibition center.  n. 旅館；飯店  我們的旅館步行即可到達展覽中心。
inn  The small inn near the lake offers a quiet place to work for a few days.  n. 小旅館  湖邊的小旅館是能安靜工作幾天的好地方。
hostel  Students traveling on a budget often choose a hostel instead of a hotel.  n. 青年旅館  預算有限的學生常選擇青年旅館而不是飯店。
minibar  Charges for items taken from the minibar will be added to your final bill.  n. 客房小冰箱  從客房小冰箱取用物品的費用將加入您的最終帳單。
linen  Fresh linen and towels are provided every morning by the housekeeping team.  n. 床單；亞麻布  飯店房務團隊每天早上都會提供乾淨的床單和毛巾。
towel  Extra towels are available at the front desk upon request.  n. 毛巾  如有需要，可向櫃檯索取額外的毛巾。
bellhop  The bellhop carried our bags to the room on the fifth floor.  n. 行李員  行李員把我們的行李搬到五樓的房間。
brunch  The hotel serves a buffet brunch every Sunday from ten until two.  n. 早午餐  飯店每週日十點到兩點供應自助式早午餐。
waitress  The waitress recommended the daily special and took our order promptly.  n. 女服務生  女服務生推薦了今日特餐，並迅速為我們點餐。
ballroom  The awards dinner will be held in the main ballroom of the Grand Hotel.  n. 宴會廳  頒獎晚宴將在大飯店的主宴會廳舉行。
marquee  A large marquee was set up in the garden for the outdoor celebration.  n. 大帳篷  花園裡為戶外慶祝活動搭設了一頂大帳篷。
fundraiser  The charity fundraiser raised over fifty thousand dollars for the children's hospital.  n. 募款活動  這場慈善募款活動為兒童醫院募得了五萬多美元。
anniversary  The firm will hold a dinner to mark its twentieth anniversary next month.  n. 週年紀念  公司下個月將舉辦晚宴，紀念成立二十週年。
wedding  The restaurant offers special packages for wedding receptions of up to two hundred guests.  n. 婚禮  這家餐廳為最多兩百位賓客的婚宴提供特別方案。
reunion  The alumni reunion will take place at the downtown campus in October.  n. 重聚；同學會  校友重聚將於十月在市區校園舉行。
symposium  A two-day symposium on renewable energy begins at the convention center on Monday.  n. 研討會  一場為期兩天的再生能源研討會週一在會議中心開幕。
exhibitor  Each exhibitor will receive a booth, two chairs, and a small table.  n. 參展商  每位參展商都會獲得一個攤位、兩張椅子和一張小桌子。
emcee  The company hired a professional emcee to host the annual awards ceremony.  n. 司儀  公司聘請了專業司儀主持年度頒獎典禮。
dine  Guests are welcome to dine on the terrace if the weather is pleasant.  v. 用餐  如果天氣宜人，歡迎賓客在露台用餐。
diner  The diner next to the station serves a quick lunch for busy commuters.  n. 用餐者；簡餐店  車站旁的簡餐店為忙碌的通勤族提供快速午餐。
restaurant  The new restaurant on Main Street specializes in fresh seafood.  n. 餐廳  主街上的新餐廳專營新鮮海鮮。
sommelier  The sommelier suggested a local red wine to go with the main course.  n. 侍酒師  侍酒師推薦了一款當地紅酒來搭配主菜。
cocktail  A cocktail hour will begin at six o'clock before the dinner presentation.  n. 雞尾酒  晚餐簡報前將於六點開始雞尾酒時間。
seasoning  The chef adjusted the seasoning before sending the dish to the table.  n. 調味料  主廚在將菜餚送上桌前調整了調味。
garnish  The salad comes with a lemon garnish and a small bowl of dressing.  n. 裝飾配菜  這份沙拉附有檸檬裝飾配菜和一小碗沙拉醬。
vegan  The caterer will prepare a vegan menu for guests who request it in advance.  adj. 純素的  如果賓客事先提出要求，外燴業者將準備純素菜單。
gluten-free  Please let us know if you need a gluten-free meal for the banquet.  adj. 無麩質的  如果您需要宴會的無麩質餐點，請告訴我們。
tasting  A wine tasting will follow the afternoon session in the hotel lounge.  n. 試飲；品嚐  午後場次結束後，將在飯店貴賓室舉辦品酒會。
entertainer  The magician was the most popular entertainer at the company party.  n. 表演者  魔術師是公司派對上最受歡迎的表演者。
performer  Each performer will receive a free ticket for a guest of their choice.  n. 表演者  每位表演者將獲得一張可供自選賓客使用的免費入場券。
usher  An usher will show you to your seat before the concert begins.  n. 引座員  音樂會開始前，引座員會帶您到座位。
lodge  The mountain lodge offers rooms with a view of the valley and free breakfast.  n. 山間旅舍  這間山間旅舍的客房可欣賞山谷景色，並附免費早餐。
villa  We rented a villa by the sea for the executive retreat in June.  n. 別墅  我們為六月的高階主管休閒活動租了一棟海邊別墅。
cottage  The resort offers a private cottage for guests who prefer more quiet.  n. 小屋；度假小木屋  想要更安靜的賓客，度假村提供私人小木屋。
lavish  The company threw a lavish party to celebrate its record sales this year.  adj. 奢華的  公司舉辦了一場奢華派對，慶祝今年創紀錄的銷售成績。
ambience  The restaurant is known for its warm ambience and attentive service.  n. 氣氛；格調  這家餐廳以溫馨的氣氛和周到的服務聞名。
cozy  The cozy cafe next to the office is perfect for informal meetings.  adj. 舒適溫馨的  辦公室隔壁這家溫馨舒適的咖啡館很適合非正式會議。
scenic  The hotel offers a scenic view of the river from every guest room.  adj. 風景優美的  這家飯店每間客房都能欣賞風景優美的河景。
tableware  The rental company supplies all the tableware, chairs, and linens for the event.  n. 餐具  租賃公司為活動提供所有餐具、椅子和桌巾。
utensil  Each place setting includes a plate, a glass, and a full set of utensils.  n. 餐具；器皿  每個餐位都包含一個盤子、一個杯子和一整套餐具。
cutlery  Plastic cutlery will be provided for the outdoor picnic on Friday.  n. 刀叉餐具  戶外野餐週五將提供塑膠刀叉餐具。
# ===== 24 科技與資訊系統 =====
data  All customer data is stored on a secure server.  n. 資料  所有客戶資料都儲存在安全的伺服器上。
system  The new system will reduce the time needed to process orders.  n. 系統  新系統將縮短處理訂單所需的時間。
username  Please choose a username that is easy to remember.  n. 使用者名稱  請選擇一個容易記住的使用者名稱。
backup  Always keep a backup of important files on an external drive.  n. 備份  請務必將重要檔案備份在外接硬碟上。
database  The customer database is updated automatically every night.  n. 資料庫  客戶資料庫每晚都會自動更新。
firewall  A firewall protects our network from unauthorized users.  n. 防火牆  防火牆可保護我們的網路免受未經授權的使用者入侵。
malware  Security software can detect malware before it harms your files.  n. 惡意軟體  資安軟體能在惡意軟體損害您的檔案之前偵測到它。
virus  A virus infected several computers in the finance department.  n. 病毒  一個病毒感染了財務部門的數台電腦。
encrypt  We encrypt all payment information to keep it safe.  v. 加密  我們將所有付款資訊加密以確保安全。
authenticate  Users must authenticate their identity before opening the account.  v. 驗證身分  使用者必須先驗證身分才能開啟帳戶。
permission  You need permission from the administrator to open this folder.  n. 權限；許可  您需要管理員的許可才能開啟這個資料夾。
privacy  Our privacy policy explains how we protect your personal information.  n. 隱私  我們的隱私權政策說明了我們如何保護您的個人資訊。
cybersecurity  The firm invests heavily in cybersecurity training for its employees.  n. 資訊安全  這家公司在員工的資訊安全訓練上投入大量資金。
phishing  Employees should report any phishing emails to the IT department.  n. 網路釣魚  員工應將任何網路釣魚郵件通報給資訊部門。
vulnerability  The patch fixes a serious vulnerability in the operating system.  n. 弱點；漏洞  這個修補程式修正了作業系統中的一個嚴重漏洞。
patch  A security patch will be installed during the maintenance window.  n. 修補程式  安全修補程式將在維護時段安裝。
bug  The programmers fixed a bug that caused the app to freeze.  n. 程式錯誤  程式設計師修正了導致應用程式當機的錯誤。
glitch  A technical glitch delayed the launch of the online store.  n. 小故障  一個技術性小故障延後了網路商店的上線。
crash  The program may crash if you open too many files.  v. 當機；墜毀；n. 撞車事故  如果您同時開啟太多檔案，程式可能會當機。
diagnose  The software can diagnose network problems automatically.  v. 診斷  這套軟體能自動診斷網路問題。
restart  You must restart the device to complete the installation.  v. 重新啟動  你必須重新啟動裝置才能完成安裝。
reset  Click the link to reset your password.  v. 重設；重新設定  請點選連結重設你的密碼。
configure  The administrator will configure each laptop before it is issued.  v. 設定；配置  管理員會在發放前設定好每一台筆記型電腦。
integrate  The team plans to integrate the payroll system with the HR database.  v. 整合  團隊計畫將薪資系統與人資資料庫整合。
migrate  We will migrate all data to the new cloud platform this weekend.  v. 遷移  我們本週末會把所有資料遷移到新的雲端平台。
deploy  The developers will deploy the new version tomorrow morning.  v. 部署；上線  開發人員明天早上會部署新版本。
log  The log shows every failed attempt to enter the system.  n. 日誌；紀錄  日誌顯示了每一次登入系統失敗的嘗試。
user  Each user receives a unique password for the portal.  n. 使用者  每位使用者都會拿到入口網站專用的獨一無二密碼。
dashboard  The dashboard displays sales figures in real time.  n. 儀表板  儀表板會即時顯示銷售數字。
spreadsheet  Please enter the sales figures into the spreadsheet by Friday.  n. 試算表  請在週五前把銷售數字輸入試算表。
format  The report must be submitted in the following format.  n. 格式；v. 格式化  報告必須以下列格式提交。
algorithm  The search algorithm ranks results by relevance.  n. 演算法  搜尋演算法會依相關性為結果排序。
automate  We plan to automate the invoicing process to save time.  v. 自動化  我們計畫將開立發票的流程自動化以節省時間。
automatic  Automatic updates keep the software secure without any effort.  adj. 自動的  自動更新讓軟體不費吹灰之力就能保持安全。
digital  The magazine is now available in a digital edition.  adj. 數位的  這本雜誌現在有數位版可供閱讀。
offline  The system will go offline for maintenance at midnight.  adj. 離線的  系統將於午夜離線進行維護。
connect  Please connect the laptop to the wireless network.  v. 連接  請將筆記型電腦連上無線網路。
sync  The app will sync your calendar across all devices.  v. 同步  這個應用程式會在所有裝置間同步你的行事曆。
tablet  Sales reps use a tablet to show clients the catalog.  n. 平板電腦  業務代表用平板電腦向客戶展示型錄。
router  Restart the router if the connection becomes slow.  n. 路由器  如果連線變慢，請重新啟動路由器。
browser  Clear your browser cache if the page does not load.  n. 瀏覽器  如果網頁無法載入，請清除瀏覽器快取。
interface  The new interface is simple and easy for beginners to use.  n. 介面  新介面簡單明瞭，初學者也容易上手。
cloud  Files stored in the cloud can be opened from any device.  n. 雲端  儲存在雲端的檔案可從任何裝置開啟。
bandwidth  Video calls require more bandwidth than ordinary email.  n. 頻寬  視訊通話需要的頻寬比一般電子郵件多。
firmware  The firmware update will improve the speed of the device.  n. 韌體  這次的韌體更新將提升裝置的速度。
program  The program runs faster after the latest update.  n. 程式  最新更新後，這個程式執行得更快。
code  The developers rewrote the code to fix the error.  n. 程式碼  開發人員重寫了程式碼來修正錯誤。
developer  The developer is testing the new payment feature.  n. 開發人員  開發人員正在測試新的付款功能。
recover  The technician managed to recover the lost files.  v. 復原；恢復  技術人員成功復原了遺失的檔案。
restore  Click here to restore the system to its previous settings.  v. 還原  點選此處即可將系統還原為先前的設定。
sensor  A sensor detects movement and turns on the lights.  n. 感測器  感測器偵測到動作時就會開燈。
encryption  Data encryption protects customer records from being read by unauthorized users.  n. 加密  資料加密可防止客戶紀錄被未經授權的使用者讀取。
decrypt  Only authorized staff have the key needed to decrypt the stored files.  v. 解密  只有獲授權的員工持有解密儲存檔案所需的金鑰。
authentication  Two-step authentication is now required for all employee email accounts.  n. 驗證；認證  所有員工電子郵件帳號現在都必須使用兩步驟驗證。
hacker  A hacker gained access to the old server before the security team noticed.  n. 駭客  在資安團隊察覺之前，駭客就已入侵舊伺服器。
hack  Experts warned that the weak password could make it easy to hack the account.  v. 入侵；駭入  專家警告，這個薄弱的密碼可能讓帳號很容易被入侵。
cyberattack  The bank reported a cyberattack that briefly shut down its online services.  n. 網路攻擊  該銀行通報遭到網路攻擊，線上服務一度中斷。
ransomware  The hospital's computers were locked by ransomware, and staff had to use paper records.  n. 勒索軟體  醫院的電腦被勒索軟體鎖住，員工只得改用紙本紀錄。
antivirus  Please keep the antivirus program on your laptop updated at all times.  n. 防毒軟體  請隨時保持筆記型電腦上的防毒程式為最新版本。
debug  Our engineers will debug the program before the final release next week.  v. 除錯  我們的工程師會在下週正式發行前為程式除錯。
coding  Coding standards help our team write software that is easy to maintain.  n. 程式編寫  程式編寫規範能幫助團隊寫出易於維護的軟體。
programmer  The company is looking for a programmer with experience in mobile applications.  n. 程式設計師  公司正在徵求具備行動應用程式經驗的程式設計師。
script  A short script runs every night to copy the files to the backup drive.  n. 腳本；指令碼  每晚都有一段簡短的腳本把檔案複製到備份硬碟。
legacy  The legacy system is too old to work with our new accounting software.  adj. 舊有的；遺留的  這套舊有系統太老舊，無法與我們的新會計軟體搭配使用。
interoperable  The new devices are interoperable with products from most other manufacturers.  adj. 可互通的  這些新裝置可與大多數其他廠商的產品互通。
latency  High latency caused the video call to freeze several times during the meeting.  n. 延遲  高延遲導致視訊通話在會議期間多次當機。
memory  The program requires at least eight gigabytes of memory to run smoothly.  n. 記憶體  這個程式至少需要八GB的記憶體才能順暢執行。
processor  The new laptop has a faster processor and a longer battery life.  n. 處理器  這台新筆記型電腦的處理器更快，電池續航力也更久。
broadband  Broadband service will be installed in every office by the end of the month.  n. 寬頻  寬頻服務將在月底前裝設到每間辦公室。
ethernet  Please connect your computer with an ethernet cable for a more stable connection.  n. 乙太網路  請用乙太網路線連接你的電腦，連線會更穩定。
modem  Restart the modem if you cannot connect to the wireless network.  n. 數據機  如果無法連上無線網路，請重新啟動數據機。
domain  The company registered a new domain for its online store.  n. 網域  公司為其網路商店註冊了新網域。
hosting  The web hosting service includes daily backups and round-the-clock support.  n. 主機代管  這項網站主機代管服務包含每日備份與全天候支援。
uptime  The provider guarantees ninety-nine percent uptime for all its customers.  n. 正常運作時間  業者向所有客戶保證百分之九十九的正常運作時間。
redundancy  Built-in redundancy ensures the system keeps running if one server fails.  n. 備援；冗餘  內建備援機制可確保一台伺服器故障時系統仍持續運作。
failover  An automatic failover switches traffic to a second server within seconds.  n. 故障轉移  自動故障轉移會在數秒內把流量切換到第二台伺服器。
cache  Clearing the browser cache may fix the problem with the loading page.  n. 快取  清除瀏覽器快取或許能解決網頁載入的問題。
plugin  Install the plugin to add a calendar feature to your website.  n. 外掛程式  安裝這個外掛程式即可為你的網站增添行事曆功能。
widget  A weather widget on the home screen shows the forecast for each city.  n. 小工具；小元件  主畫面上的天氣小工具會顯示各城市的天氣預報。
app  Download the app to check your flight status and receive instant alerts.  n. 應用程式  下載這個應用程式即可查詢航班動態並接收即時通知。
schema  The database schema must be updated before the new feature goes live.  n. 資料庫結構；綱要  新功能上線前必須先更新資料庫結構。
metadata  Metadata attached to each photo shows the date and location where it was taken.  n. 詮釋資料  附在每張照片上的詮釋資料會顯示拍攝的日期與地點。
dataset  The analysts cleaned the dataset before running the sales forecast.  n. 資料集  分析師在進行銷售預測前先清理了資料集。
virtualization  Virtualization allows several systems to run on a single physical server.  n. 虛擬化  虛擬化讓多套系統能在單一實體伺服器上執行。
virtual  The team held a virtual meeting because members work in different countries.  adj. 虛擬的；線上的  由於成員分處不同國家工作，團隊召開了線上會議。
robotics  The firm invested heavily in robotics to speed up its assembly line.  n. 機器人技術  該公司大舉投資機器人技術以加快組裝線的速度。
wearable  The company launched a wearable device that tracks heart rate and sleep.  n. 穿戴式裝置  公司推出了一款可追蹤心率與睡眠的穿戴式裝置。
touchscreen  The kiosk has a large touchscreen that guides customers through the menu.  n. 觸控螢幕  這台服務機有一個大型觸控螢幕，引導顧客操作選單。
pixel  The new monitor has more pixels per inch, so images look much sharper.  n. 像素  這台新螢幕每英吋的像素更多，所以影像看起來清晰許多。
peripheral  A printer is a peripheral that connects to the computer with a single cable.  n. 週邊設備  印表機是一種只要一條線就能連接電腦的週邊設備。
charger  Please return the laptop and the charger to the IT desk by Friday.  n. 充電器  請在週五前將筆記型電腦與充電器歸還資訊部服務台。
provisioning  Account provisioning for new hires is handled by the IT department.  n. 帳號與資源配置  新進員工的帳號配置由資訊部門負責處理。
infrastructure  The company will upgrade its network infrastructure over the holiday weekend.  n. 基礎建設  公司將利用假日週末升級其網路基礎建設。
architecture  The system architecture was designed to support thousands of users at once.  n. 系統架構  這套系統架構的設計可同時支援數千名使用者。
integration  Integration with the payment system took longer than the team expected.  n. 整合  與付款系統的整合花費的時間比團隊預期的還長。
middleware  Middleware connects the old inventory program to the new online store.  n. 中介軟體  中介軟體將舊的庫存程式與新的網路商店連接起來。
open-source  The team prefers open-source tools because they are free and well supported.  adj. 開放原始碼的  團隊偏好開放原始碼工具，因為它們免費且支援完善。
proprietary  The software uses a proprietary format that other programs cannot open.  adj. 專有的；私有的  這套軟體使用其他程式無法開啟的專有格式。
licensing  Licensing costs for the design software rose sharply this year.  n. 授權；授權費  設計軟體的授權費用今年大幅上漲。
freemium  The app follows a freemium model, with advanced tools available for a monthly fee.  adj. 基本免費的  這款應用程式採用基本免費的模式，進階工具則需按月付費。
# ===== 25 研發品質與安全 =====
test  The engineers will test the new software before it is released to customers.  v. 測試；n. 測試；檢驗  工程師會在新軟體發送給客戶前先進行測試。
research  The company spends millions on research to develop better batteries.  n. 研究；v. 研究  公司投入數百萬元進行研究，以開發更好的電池。
standard  The new device meets all international safety standards.  n. 標準；adj. 標準的  這款新裝置符合所有國際安全標準。
emergency  In an emergency, use the nearest exit and call the security desk.  n. 緊急情況  遇到緊急情況時，請使用最近的出口並致電警衛室。
inspection  The annual inspection of the building is scheduled for next Tuesday.  n. 檢查；檢驗  這棟大樓的年度檢查訂於下週二進行。
protocol  Staff must follow the safety protocol when handling chemicals.  n. 規範；協定  員工處理化學品時必須遵守安全規範。
evacuate  Employees were asked to evacuate the building when the alarm sounded.  v. 疏散；撤離  警報響起時，員工被要求撤離大樓。
analyze  The lab will analyze the samples and send a report on Monday.  v. 分析  實驗室會分析樣本，並於週一寄出報告。
experiment  The scientists conducted an experiment to test the new formula.  n. 實驗；v. 做實驗  科學家進行了一項實驗來測試新配方。
recall  The manufacturer issued a recall of faulty heaters last month.  n. 產品召回；v. 召回  製造商上個月宣布召回有瑕疵的暖氣機。
recall  I cannot recall the name of the engineer who approved the design.  v. 回想；記起  我想不起來核准這項設計的工程師叫什麼名字。
injury  No injury was reported at the plant during the entire year.  n. 傷害；受傷  這間工廠全年沒有任何人受傷的通報。
alarm  The smoke alarm went off during the staff meeting.  n. 警報器；警報  員工會議進行時，煙霧警報器響了。
extinguisher  A fire extinguisher is mounted on every floor of the office.  n. 滅火器  辦公室每一層樓都裝設有滅火器。
goggles  Please put on goggles before operating the cutting machine.  n. 護目鏡  操作切割機前，請先戴上護目鏡。
precaution  Take every precaution when handling hazardous materials.  n. 預防措施  處理危險物品時請採取一切預防措施。
contamination  Strict cleaning rules help prevent contamination in the food plant.  n. 汙染  嚴格的清潔規定有助於防止食品工廠發生汙染。
toxic  The label warns that the cleaning liquid is toxic.  adj. 有毒的  標籤警告這種清潔液有毒。
flammable  Store flammable liquids away from heat sources.  adj. 易燃的  易燃液體請遠離熱源存放。
hygiene  Good hygiene is essential for all restaurant employees.  n. 衛生  良好的衛生習慣對所有餐廳員工都至關重要。
accuracy  The accuracy of the measurements is vital to this experiment.  n. 準確度  測量的準確度對這項實驗至關重要。
reliability  Testing confirmed the reliability of the new server.  n. 可靠性  測試證實了新伺服器的可靠性。
laboratory  The laboratory is open to authorized staff only.  n. 實驗室  實驗室僅開放給獲授權的員工進入。
specimen  Each specimen must be labeled and stored at a low temperature.  n. 樣本；標本  每份樣本都必須貼上標籤並低溫保存。
findings  The findings of the study were presented at the annual conference.  n. 調查結果  這項研究的調查結果在年度研討會上發表。
hypothesis  The experiment supports our original hypothesis about energy use.  n. 假設  這項實驗支持了我們原先對能源使用的假設。
breakthrough  Researchers announced a breakthrough in solar panel efficiency.  n. 突破  研究人員宣布在太陽能板效率方面有所突破。
fault  The fault was traced to a damaged cable in the control panel.  n. 故障；缺失  經追查，故障源自控制面板內一條受損的纜線。
mockup  The designer showed a mockup of the new package to the client.  n. 模型；樣稿  設計師向客戶展示了新包裝的模型。
simulate  The program can simulate how the bridge reacts to strong winds.  v. 模擬  這個程式可以模擬橋梁在強風下的反應。
citation  The inspector issued a citation for blocked fire exits.  n. 傳票；違規告發  檢查員因防火出口遭堵塞而開出違規告發單。
shelter  Staff should move to the shelter in the basement during a storm.  n. 避難所  暴風雨期間，員工應移往地下室的避難處。
rescue  Rescue teams arrived at the scene within ten minutes.  n. 救援；v. 救援  救援隊在十分鐘內抵達現場。
firefighter  A firefighter gave a safety talk to our new employees.  n. 消防員  一位消防員為我們的新進員工做了安全講座。
supervision  Trainees must work under close supervision for the first month.  n. 監督  受訓人員在第一個月必須在嚴密監督下工作。
durability  The durability of the casing was tested under heavy pressure.  n. 耐用性  外殼的耐用性在重壓下接受了測試。
leakage  Gas leakage was detected in the storage area during the routine check.  n. 洩漏  例行檢查時，在儲存區偵測到瓦斯洩漏。
reinforce  Engineers will reinforce the walls to improve earthquake safety.  v. 加強；強化  工程師將補強牆面以提升耐震安全。
hazardous  Hazardous waste must be disposed of by licensed workers.  adj. 有害的；危險的  有害廢棄物必須由持有執照的人員處理。
overheat  The motor may overheat if the fan is blocked.  v. 過熱  如果風扇被擋住，馬達可能會過熱。
checklist  Use the checklist to confirm each safety step has been completed.  n. 檢查清單  請利用檢查清單確認每個安全步驟都已完成。
incident  Please report any safety incident to your supervisor right away.  n. 事件；意外事故  任何安全事件請立即向主管通報。
robust  The new design proved robust under extreme testing conditions.  adj. 堅固的；穩健的  新設計在極端測試條件下證明十分穩固。
traceable  Every component is traceable to its original supplier.  adj. 可追溯的  每個零件都能追溯到原始供應商。
failure  The failure of the cooling unit caused a brief production delay.  n. 故障；失敗  冷卻裝置故障，造成生產短暫延誤。
flaw  The inspector found a flaw in the design before production began.  n. 瑕疵；缺陷  檢查員在開始生產前發現設計上有瑕疵。
anomaly  The software flagged an anomaly in last night's test results.  n. 異常；反常現象  軟體標出了昨晚測試結果中的一項異常。
sterile  All surgical tools must be kept sterile until they are used.  adj. 無菌的；消毒過的  所有手術器械在使用前都必須保持無菌。
contaminate  Workers must wear gloves so they do not contaminate the samples.  v. 汙染  工作人員必須戴手套，以免汙染樣本。
residue  The lab tests showed no chemical residue on the finished product.  n. 殘留物  實驗室檢測顯示成品上沒有化學殘留物。
spill  Please clean up any chemical spill immediately and notify the safety officer.  n. 溢出；灑出的液體  發現化學物質溢出請立即清理，並通知安全人員。
fume  The ventilation system removes harmful fumes from the paint shop.  n. 有害氣體；煙霧  通風系統會排除噴漆車間內的有害氣體。
respirator  Employees in the dusty area are required to wear a respirator.  n. 呼吸防護面具  在多塵區域工作的員工必須配戴呼吸防護面具。
harness  Workers on the roof must attach a safety harness before climbing.  n. 安全帶；護具  屋頂上的工人在攀爬前必須繫上安全帶。
glove  Disposable gloves are provided at the entrance of the laboratory.  n. 手套  實驗室入口處備有拋棄式手套。
caution  Use caution when handling the equipment during the maintenance period.  n. 小心；謹慎  維修期間操作設備時請小心謹慎。
cautious  The engineers were cautious about releasing the new design too early.  adj. 謹慎的；小心的  工程師對過早發表新設計抱持謹慎態度。
mishap  A minor mishap during testing delayed the launch by two days.  n. 小意外；不幸事件  測試期間的一起小意外使產品上市延後了兩天。
negligent  The company was found negligent for failing to repair the broken railing.  adj. 疏忽的；怠忽職守的  該公司因未修復損壞的欄杆而被認定疏忽。
injure  Faulty equipment could seriously injure the operators on the line.  v. 使受傷；傷害  有缺陷的設備可能使產線操作員受重傷。
evacuation  The evacuation drill will take place on the third floor at noon.  n. 疏散；撤離  疏散演練將於中午在三樓舉行。
extinguish  Staff should know how to extinguish a small fire safely.  v. 撲滅；熄滅  員工應知道如何安全地撲滅小型火災。
ergonomics  The consultant will review the ergonomics of our assembly workstations.  n. 人體工學  顧問將檢視我們組裝工作站的人體工學設計。
radiation  Technicians wear badges to measure their exposure to radiation.  n. 輻射  技術人員佩戴徽章以測量輻射暴露量。
ventilate  Please ventilate the room well when using these cleaning products.  v. 使通風；使空氣流通  使用這些清潔用品時，請讓房間充分通風。
toxicity  The report describes the toxicity of the new coating material.  n. 毒性  報告說明了這種新塗料的毒性。
safeguard  The new password rule is a safeguard against unauthorized access.  n. 保護措施；防護  新的密碼規定是防範未經授權存取的保護措施。
diagnostic  Run the diagnostic test before sending the device back to the factory.  adj. 診斷的；n. 診斷程式  將裝置寄回工廠前，請先執行診斷測試。
intrusion  The security team detected an intrusion into the company network.  n. 入侵；闖入  資安團隊偵測到有人入侵公司網路。
threat  Outdated software poses a serious threat to our network security.  n. 威脅；威脅因素  過時的軟體對我們的網路安全構成嚴重威脅。
conformity  The product was tested for conformity with international safety standards.  n. 符合；一致性  該產品已接受是否符合國際安全標準的測試。
deficiency  The audit revealed a deficiency in the way records were kept.  n. 缺失；不足  稽核發現紀錄保存的方式有缺失。
consistency  Customers expect consistency in the quality of every product we ship.  n. 一致性；穩定性  顧客期望我們出貨的每項產品品質都穩定一致。
precise  The machine makes precise cuts within a tenth of a millimeter.  adj. 精確的；準確的  這台機器的切割十分精確，誤差在零點一毫米以內。
invention  The invention was patented shortly after the first successful trial.  n. 發明；發明物  這項發明在首次試驗成功後不久就取得了專利。
inventor  The inventor presented her latest design at the technology fair.  n. 發明者；發明人  這位發明人在科技展上發表了她的最新設計。
discovery  The discovery of the defect saved the company a costly recall.  n. 發現；發現物  這項瑕疵的發現讓公司免於一次代價高昂的召回。
methodology  The study explains the methodology used to collect the data.  n. 方法；方法論  這份研究說明了蒐集資料所採用的方法。
replicate  Other labs were unable to replicate the original results.  v. 複製；重現  其他實驗室無法重現最初的結果。
formulate  The team will formulate a new plan to improve product safety.  v. 制定；研擬  團隊將研擬一項新計畫來提升產品安全。
tweak  A small tweak to the settings improved the battery life.  n. 微調；v. 稍作調整  設定上的小幅微調延長了電池續航力。
optimization  Code optimization reduced the loading time of the application by half.  n. 最佳化  程式碼最佳化使應用程式的載入時間減少了一半。
robustness  Engineers tested the robustness of the bridge design under heavy loads.  n. 穩健性；耐用度  工程師在重載條件下測試了橋梁設計的穩健性。
traceability  Barcodes give us full traceability of every part used in assembly.  n. 可追溯性  條碼讓我們能完整追溯組裝所用的每個零件。
validation  The software passed validation by an independent testing firm.  n. 驗證；確認  這套軟體通過了獨立測試公司的驗證。
reagent  Store each reagent at the temperature shown on the label.  n. 試劑  每種試劑都請依標籤所示溫度保存。
sampling  Random sampling is used to check the quality of each shipment.  n. 抽樣；取樣  每批出貨都以隨機抽樣的方式檢查品質。
iterate  We will iterate on the design until it meets customer needs.  v. 反覆改進；迭代  我們會反覆改進這項設計，直到符合客戶需求為止。
hypothesize  Researchers hypothesize that the new material will last longer.  v. 假設；提出假說  研究人員假設這種新材料會更耐用。
fail-safe  The machine includes a fail-safe that stops it when a door opens.  n. 故障自動防護裝置  這台機器配有故障自動防護裝置，門一打開就會停機。
corrosion  Regular painting prevents corrosion on the outdoor metal structures.  n. 腐蝕；鏽蝕  定期上漆可防止戶外金屬結構生鏽腐蝕。
fatigue  Metal fatigue was identified as the cause of the crack.  n. 疲勞；材料疲乏  金屬疲勞被認定為造成裂痕的原因。
shatter  The safety glass is designed not to shatter on impact.  v. 粉碎；碎裂  這種安全玻璃的設計使其受到撞擊時不會碎裂。
vigilance  Constant vigilance is required to keep the production area safe.  n. 警覺；警戒  要確保生產區安全，必須時時保持警覺。
sanitize  Staff must sanitize their hands before entering the food processing area.  v. 消毒；清潔  員工進入食品加工區前必須先消毒雙手。
disinfect  Cleaners disinfect all shared surfaces twice a day.  v. 消毒；殺菌  清潔人員每天會替所有共用表面消毒兩次。
experimentation  Careful experimentation showed which design was the most efficient.  n. 實驗；試驗  經過仔細的實驗，才知道哪種設計最有效率。
