tamper-proof  The medicine comes in tamper-proof packaging for customer safety.  adj. 防拆封的  這款藥品採用防拆封包裝，以保障顧客安全。
stress-test  We will stress-test the website before the holiday sale begins.  v. 壓力測試  我們會在假日特賣開始前對網站進行壓力測試。
leakproof  The new bottle has a leakproof cap that is ideal for travel.  adj. 防漏的  這款新水瓶有防漏瓶蓋，很適合旅行使用。
breakage  Extra padding was added to the crate to prevent breakage.  n. 破損；破裂  木箱內加了額外的襯墊以防止破損。
# ===== 26 房地產建築與設施 =====
rent  The rent for the studio includes water and internet.  n. 租金  這間套房的租金包含水費與網路費。
rent  We plan to rent additional space for the new sales team.  v. 租用  我們計畫為新的業務團隊租用更多空間。
property  The property has a large garden and a two-car garage.  n. 房地產；財產  這處房產有一座大花園和可停兩輛車的車庫。
construction  Construction of the new bridge will begin next spring.  n. 施工；建設  新橋的施工將於明年春天開始。
utility  The monthly fee covers all utility costs such as water and electricity.  n. 公用事業；水電費  月費涵蓋水電等所有公用事業費用。
installation  The installation of the solar panels takes about three days.  n. 安裝  太陽能板的安裝大約需要三天。
building  The building was constructed in 1998 and has twelve floors.  n. 建築物；大樓  這棟大樓建於一九九八年，共有十二層樓。
site  The construction site is closed to visitors without a permit.  n. 工地；場址  施工工地禁止未持許可證的訪客進入。
site  Visit our site to download the application form.  n. 網站  請瀏覽我們的網站下載申請表。
zoning  Local zoning laws limit the height of buildings in this area.  n. 土地分區規劃  當地的分區規劃法規限制了這一帶建築物的高度。
foundation  Workers poured the concrete foundation for the new factory.  n. 地基；基礎  工人為新工廠澆灌了混凝土地基。
architect  The architect presented the revised plans at Tuesday's meeting.  n. 建築師  建築師在週二的會議上提出了修訂後的設計圖。
demolish  The city plans to demolish the old warehouse next year.  v. 拆除  市府計畫明年拆除這座老舊倉庫。
remodel  We decided to remodel the kitchen before listing the house.  v. 改建；重新裝潢  我們決定在將房子上市出售前先重新裝潢廚房。
furnish  The apartment is fully furnished with modern furniture.  v. 提供家具；布置  這間公寓配備了齊全的現代家具。
corridor  The corridor on the second floor is being repainted.  n. 走廊  二樓的走廊正在重新粉刷。
plumbing  The plumbing in the old building needs to be replaced.  n. 水管系統；管線工程  這棟老建築的水管系統需要更換。
plumber  A plumber is coming to fix the leaking pipe.  n. 水管工  水管工人要來修理漏水的管子。
electrician  An electrician will check the wiring before the new tenants arrive.  n. 電工  電工將在新房客入住前檢查線路。
wiring  The faulty wiring was the cause of the power failure.  n. 電線；配線  線路故障是這次停電的原因。
pipe  A broken pipe flooded the basement last night.  n. 管線；水管  昨晚一根破裂的水管淹了地下室。
leak  Please report any leak in the roof to the facilities manager.  n. 漏水；v. 漏  屋頂若有任何漏水，請通報設施經理。
heating  The heating system will be inspected before winter.  n. 暖氣；供暖  暖氣系統將在冬季前接受檢查。
insulation  Better insulation will reduce the building's energy costs.  n. 隔熱；隔音材料  更好的隔熱設施能降低大樓的能源成本。
roof  The roof was damaged during last week's storm.  n. 屋頂  屋頂在上週的暴風雨中受損。
wall  The workers painted the wall in a light gray color.  n. 牆壁  工人把牆壁漆成淺灰色。
estate  The agency specializes in selling real estate in the city center.  n. 不動產；地產  這家仲介公司專門銷售市中心的不動產。
residential  The company plans to build a residential complex near the station.  adj. 住宅的  該公司計畫在車站附近興建住宅大樓。
sublease  She decided to sublease her apartment while working overseas.  v. 轉租  她決定在海外工作期間把公寓轉租出去。
evict  The landlord may evict tenants who fail to pay rent for months.  v. 驅逐；逐出  房東可以驅逐連續數月未繳房租的房客。
relocation  The relocation of the office is scheduled for early June.  n. 搬遷  辦公室的搬遷預定在六月初進行。
footage  The total footage of the office is about two thousand square feet.  n. 平方英尺面積  這間辦公室的總面積約為兩千平方英尺。
tile  The workers laid new tile in the bathroom.  n. 磁磚；瓷磚  工人在浴室鋪了新的磁磚。
paint  The walls need a fresh coat of paint before the open house.  n. 油漆；v. 粉刷  開放參觀日前，牆壁需要重新刷上一層油漆。
concrete  The workers poured concrete for the parking area this morning.  n. 混凝土  工人今天早上為停車區澆灌了混凝土。
lumber  The supplier delivered the lumber to the building site on Monday.  n. 木材  供應商週一把木材送到了工地。
brick  The new library is built with red brick.  n. 磚塊  新的圖書館是用紅磚建造的。
scaffold  Workers set up a scaffold along the front of the building.  n. 鷹架  工人沿著大樓正面搭起了鷹架。
excavate  Crews will excavate the site before laying the foundation.  v. 挖掘  施工人員將在打地基前先挖掘整地。
fixture  The lease includes all lighting fixtures in the apartment.  n. 固定設備；裝置  租約包含公寓內所有的照明設備。
generator  A backup generator keeps the hospital running during blackouts.  n. 發電機  備用發電機讓醫院在停電時仍能運作。
custodian  The custodian locks the building at ten each night.  n. 管理員；看管人  管理員每晚十點鎖上大樓。
neighborhood  The neighborhood has several good schools and a small park.  n. 社區；鄰近地區  這個社區有幾所好學校和一座小公園。
suburb  Many employees live in a quiet suburb outside the city.  n. 郊區  許多員工住在市區外安靜的郊區。
downtown  Our downtown office is within walking distance of the station.  adj. 市區的；adv. 往市中心  我們的市區辦公室離車站步行即可到達。
square  The company bought a square lot near the highway.  adj. 方形的；n. 廣場  公司在高速公路附近買了一塊方形土地。
inspector  The building inspector approved the plans on Thursday.  n. 檢查員；督察  建築檢查員在週四核准了這些設計圖。
tenancy  Her tenancy ends on the last day of August.  n. 租期；租賃  她的租期在八月的最後一天結束。
landscaping  The landscaping around the office makes it attractive to visitors.  n. 景觀美化  辦公室周圍的景觀美化讓訪客覺得很吸引人。
renovation  The renovation of the lobby will take about six weeks.  n. 翻修；整修  大廳的整修工程約需六週。
refurbish  The hotel will refurbish its conference rooms over the summer.  v. 整修；翻新  飯店將在夏季期間整修會議室。
sewage  The city is upgrading its sewage system this year.  n. 汙水  市政府今年正在升級汙水處理系統。
stairwell  Please keep the stairwell clear at all times.  n. 樓梯間  請隨時保持樓梯間暢通。
balcony  Each unit has a small balcony facing the river.  n. 陽台  每個單位都有一個面向河流的小陽台。
basement  Old files are stored in the basement of the building.  n. 地下室  舊檔案存放在大樓的地下室。
facade  The facade of the building will be cleaned next week.  n. 建築外觀；正面  大樓的外牆將於下週清洗。
workspace  The new workspace has plenty of natural light.  n. 工作空間  新的工作空間有充足的自然光。
vicinity  There are several restaurants in the vicinity of the office.  n. 附近地區  辦公室附近有好幾家餐廳。
accessible  The new entrance is accessible to people in wheelchairs.  adj. 易到達的；無障礙的  新的入口方便輪椅使用者進出。
spacious  The spacious conference room can seat fifty people.  adj. 寬敞的  這間寬敞的會議室可容納五十人。
realtor  The realtor showed us three apartments near the central station.  n. 房地產經紀人  房地產經紀人帶我們看了中央車站附近的三間公寓。
sublet  Tenants may not sublet the unit without written permission.  v. 轉租  房客未經書面許可不得轉租該單位。
occupant  Each occupant must carry an access card to enter the building.  n. 住戶；佔用者  每位住戶都必須攜帶門禁卡才能進入大樓。
easement  The utility company has an easement across the northern edge of the property.  n. 地役權；通行權  公用事業公司在這塊土地北側擁有通行地役權。
deed  The deed was signed and recorded at the county office on Friday.  n. 契據；地契  這份地契已於週五在郡辦公室簽署並登記。
walkthrough  A final walkthrough of the house is scheduled for Thursday morning.  n. 現場巡檢；實地查看  房屋的最後現場巡檢預定在週四早上進行。
excavation  Excavation for the new parking garage will start next month.  n. 開挖；挖掘工程  新停車場的開挖工程將於下個月開始。
masonry  The old masonry on the front wall needs to be repaired.  n. 石造工程；砌磚  前牆上老舊的砌磚需要修復。
plaster  Workers will plaster the walls before they are painted.  v. 粉刷；塗灰泥  工人會在牆壁上漆之前先塗抹灰泥。
drywall  The contractor installed drywall in all of the new offices.  n. 石膏板  承包商在所有新辦公室都安裝了石膏板。
flooring  The company chose wood flooring for the main lobby.  n. 地板；地板材料  公司為主大廳選用了木地板。
escalator  The escalator on the second floor is out of service today.  n. 電扶梯  二樓的電扶梯今天停止使用。
atrium  The hotel has a bright atrium with a coffee shop and seating area.  n. 中庭；挑高大廳  這家飯店有明亮的中庭，內設咖啡店和休息區。
courtyard  Guests may enjoy lunch in the quiet courtyard behind the building.  n. 庭院；中庭  賓客可以在大樓後方安靜的庭院享用午餐。
rooftop  The rooftop terrace is available for private events on weekends.  n. 屋頂；adj. 屋頂的  屋頂露臺週末可供私人活動租用。
sprinkler  A sprinkler system was installed on every floor of the warehouse.  n. 灑水滅火裝置  倉庫的每一層樓都安裝了自動灑水系統。
soundproof  The conference rooms are soundproof so meetings stay private.  adj. 隔音的  會議室具備隔音設計，讓會議內容保持隱密。
furnished  The furnished apartment includes a desk, a bed, and a sofa.  adj. 附家具的  這間附家具的公寓包含書桌、床和沙發。
unfurnished  We are looking for an unfurnished office of about 200 square meters.  adj. 未附家具的  我們正在找一間約兩百平方公尺、不附家具的辦公室。
open-plan  The open-plan office encourages teams to work closely together.  adj. 開放式格局的  開放式格局的辦公室鼓勵團隊密切合作。
district  The new headquarters is located in the downtown business district.  n. 地區；行政區  新總部位於市中心的商業區。
municipality  The municipality approved plans for a public library on Main Street.  n. 市政當局；自治市  市政當局核准了在主街興建公共圖書館的計畫。
skyline  The new tower will change the city skyline within two years.  n. 天際線  這座新大樓將在兩年內改變城市的天際線。
high-rise  The firm leases three floors in a high-rise near the harbor.  n. 高樓大廈  這家公司租用了港口附近一棟高樓的三個樓層。
townhouse  They bought a townhouse close to the subway station.  n. 連棟住宅  他們買了一間靠近地鐵站的連棟住宅。
condominium  The condominium has a gym and a swimming pool for residents.  n. 公寓大廈；住戶自有的公寓  這棟公寓大廈為住戶設有健身房和游泳池。
duplex  The company rents a duplex to house visiting engineers.  n. 雙拼住宅；複式住宅  公司租了一間複式住宅來安置來訪的工程師。
mansion  The historic mansion was converted into a conference center.  n. 豪宅；大宅  這座歷史悠久的大宅被改建成會議中心。
dormitory  The university plans to build a new dormitory for international students.  n. 學生宿舍  大學計畫為國際學生興建一棟新宿舍。
demolition  Demolition of the old factory is expected to take six weeks.  n. 拆除；拆毀  舊工廠的拆除工程預計需要六週。
retrofit  The city will retrofit older buildings with energy-saving windows.  v. 改裝；翻修升級  市政府將為老舊建築加裝節能窗戶。
acre  The developer purchased ten acres of land outside the city.  n. 英畝  開發商買下了市郊十英畝的土地。
boundary  A fence marks the boundary between the two properties.  n. 邊界；界線  一道圍籬標示出兩塊土地之間的界線。
driveway  Please do not park in front of the driveway at any time.  n. 車道；私人車道  請勿隨時將車停在車道出入口前。
sidewalk  The city repaired the sidewalk outside our office entrance.  n. 人行道  市政府修繕了我們辦公室入口外的人行道。
pavement  The pavement near the entrance was damaged during the storm.  n. 鋪面；路面  入口附近的路面在暴風雨中受損。
electricity  Electricity will be shut off in the building from nine to noon.  n. 電力；電  大樓將在九點到中午之間停電。
landscape  The architect designed a garden to landscape the main entrance.  v. 景觀美化  建築師設計了一座花園來美化主要入口的景觀。
tower  The glass tower will house offices, shops, and a hotel.  n. 塔樓；高層建築  這棟玻璃大樓將設有辦公室、商店和一間飯店。
penthouse  The penthouse on the top floor offers views of the entire bay.  n. 頂樓豪華公寓  頂樓的豪華公寓可俯瞰整個海灣。
flat  The agency lists a bright flat near the university for rent.  n. 公寓；單層住宅  仲介公司刊登了一間靠近大學、採光明亮的出租公寓。
landlady  The landlady will return the security deposit within thirty days.  n. 女房東  女房東會在三十天內退還押金。
# ===== 27 醫療環境與社區媒體 =====
health  The company offers a wellness program to support employee health.  n. 健康  公司提供健康促進計畫來維護員工的健康。
patient  The clinic asks every new patient to fill out a registration form.  n. 病患  診所要求每位新病患填寫掛號表。
patient  Thank you for being patient while we fix the problem.  adj. 有耐心的  感謝您在我們處理問題期間耐心等候。
clinic  The clinic is open from eight in the morning until six.  n. 診所  診所營業時間為早上八點到六點。
community  The library hosts free workshops for the local community.  n. 社區；社群  圖書館為當地社區舉辦免費的工作坊。
environment  The new policy aims to protect the environment and reduce waste.  n. 環境  這項新政策旨在保護環境並減少浪費。
reporter  A reporter from the local paper interviewed the mayor.  n. 記者  地方報社的一名記者訪問了市長。
news  The news about the factory closing surprised the entire town.  n. 新聞；消息  工廠要關閉的消息讓全鎮的人都很震驚。
public  The public is invited to the free health fair on Saturday.  n. 大眾；adj. 公眾的  歡迎大眾參加週六的免費健康博覽會。
doctor  Please ask your doctor before taking any new medication.  n. 醫師  服用任何新藥物前，請先諮詢您的醫師。
nurse  The nurse will take your temperature before the appointment.  n. 護理師  護理師會在看診前先幫您量體溫。
hospital  The hospital plans to add a new emergency wing next year.  n. 醫院  醫院計畫明年增建新的急診大樓。
treatment  The treatment for the injury takes about six weeks.  n. 治療  這項傷勢的治療約需六週。
prescription  You need a prescription to buy this medicine at the pharmacy.  n. 處方；處方箋  您需要有處方箋才能在藥局購買這種藥。
pharmacy  The pharmacy on Oak Street stays open until midnight.  n. 藥局  橡樹街上的藥局營業到午夜。
symptom  Please stay home if you have any symptom of the flu.  n. 症狀  如果有任何流感症狀，請留在家中。
vaccine  The vaccine will be available at the clinic next week.  n. 疫苗  診所下週將開始提供這種疫苗。
vaccinate  The clinic will vaccinate all staff against the flu before winter.  v. 接種疫苗  診所將在冬季前為所有員工接種流感疫苗。
checkup  Employees are entitled to one free checkup every year.  n. 健康檢查  員工每年享有一次免費健康檢查。
surgery  He recovered quickly after the knee surgery.  n. 手術  他在膝蓋手術後很快就康復了。
physician  A physician will review your test results during the visit.  n. 內科醫師；醫生  醫師會在看診時檢視您的檢驗結果。
recovery  The recovery from the operation took about two weeks.  n. 康復  手術後的康復過程花了大約兩週。
illness  A serious illness kept him away from work for a month.  n. 疾病  一場重病讓他一個月無法上班。
disease  The agency works to prevent the spread of infectious disease.  n. 疾病  該機構致力於防止傳染病的擴散。
infection  Wash your hands often to avoid infection.  n. 感染  請常洗手以避免感染。
medication  Take the medication twice a day after meals.  n. 藥物  這種藥請在飯後每日服用兩次。
dose  The label recommends a daily dose of two tablets.  n. 劑量  標籤建議每日劑量為兩錠。
ambulance  An ambulance arrived at the office within minutes.  n. 救護車  救護車在幾分鐘內就抵達了辦公室。
nutrition  The seminar covers nutrition and healthy eating at work.  n. 營養  這場研討會涵蓋營養以及職場上的健康飲食。
therapy  Physical therapy helped him return to work sooner.  n. 治療；療法  物理治療幫助他更早回到工作崗位。
renewable  The city invests in renewable energy such as wind and solar power.  adj. 可再生的  市政府投資風力和太陽能等再生能源。
conservation  Water conservation is a priority during the dry season.  n. 保育；節約  乾季期間，節約用水是首要之務。
conserve  Please conserve energy by turning off lights when leaving.  v. 節約；保存  離開時請關燈以節約能源。
solar  The company installed solar panels on the roof of its warehouse.  adj. 太陽能的  公司在倉庫屋頂安裝了太陽能板。
climate  Climate change is a major topic at this year's conference.  n. 氣候  氣候變遷是今年研討會的重要議題。
ecosystem  Pollution can seriously damage the local ecosystem.  n. 生態系統  汙染可能嚴重破壞當地的生態系統。
biodegradable  The restaurant switched to biodegradable packaging for takeout orders.  adj. 可生物分解的  這家餐廳的外帶餐點改用可生物分解的包裝。
carbon  The firm aims to cut its carbon footprint by half.  n. 碳  這家公司的目標是將碳足跡減少一半。
landfill  Recycling reduces the amount of trash sent to the landfill.  n. 垃圾掩埋場  回收可以減少送往垃圾掩埋場的垃圾量。
environmental  The report discusses the environmental impact of the new road.  adj. 環境的  這份報告探討了新道路對環境的影響。
volunteer  Dozens of volunteers cleaned the beach on Sunday.  n. 志工；v. 自願  數十名志工在週日清理了海灘。
charity  The company raised money for a local charity.  n. 慈善機構；慈善  公司為一家當地的慈善機構募款。
donate  Employees can donate old clothes to the community center.  v. 捐贈  員工可以把舊衣服捐給社區中心。
donation  The museum received a large donation from a local business.  n. 捐款；捐贈  博物館收到一家當地企業的大額捐款。
resident  Residents are invited to attend the town meeting on Thursday.  n. 居民  歡迎居民參加週四的鎮民大會。
municipal  The municipal government approved funds for a new park.  adj. 市政的  市政府核准了興建新公園的經費。
council  The city council will vote on the proposal next week.  n. 議會；委員會  市議會將於下週就這項提案進行表決。
nonprofit  The nonprofit organization provides free meals to families in need.  n. 非營利組織  這個非營利組織為有需要的家庭提供免費餐點。
civic  The mayor praised the civic spirit of local volunteers.  adj. 公民的；市民的  市長稱讚當地志工的公民精神。
journalist  A journalist asked the CEO about the planned merger.  n. 新聞工作者；記者  一名記者向執行長詢問了預定的合併案。
article  The article about the new hospital appeared on the front page.  n. 文章；報導  那篇關於新醫院的報導刊登在頭版。
newspaper  The newspaper published a special report on local business.  n. 報紙  這份報紙刊出了一篇有關本地企業的專題報導。
editor  The editor asked the writer to shorten the article.  n. 編輯  編輯要求作者縮短這篇文章。
publish  The magazine will publish the survey results next month.  v. 出版；發表  這本雜誌將在下個月發表調查結果。
column  She writes a weekly column about small business.  n. 專欄  她每週撰寫一篇關於小型企業的專欄。
magazine  The magazine features an article about green architecture.  n. 雜誌  這本雜誌刊有一篇關於綠建築的文章。
circulation  The paper has a daily circulation of over fifty thousand copies.  n. 發行量  這份報紙的每日發行量超過五萬份。
documentary  The documentary explores how pollution affects local communities.  n. 紀錄片  這部紀錄片探討汙染如何影響當地社區。
episode  The latest episode of the podcast focuses on public health.  n. 一集；一段插曲  這個播客的最新一集聚焦於公共衛生。
anchor  The news anchor thanked viewers at the end of the program.  n. 新聞主播  新聞主播在節目結束時感謝觀眾。
correspondent  A foreign correspondent filed a report from the conference site.  n. 特派記者  一名駐外特派記者從會議現場發回了報導。
poll  The poll shows that most voters favor the new transit plan.  n. 民調  民調顯示大多數選民支持新的交通計畫。
citizen  Every citizen has the right to access public information.  n. 市民；公民  每位公民都有權取得公共資訊。
welfare  The city provides welfare services to families in need.  n. 福利；福祉  市政府為有需要的家庭提供福利服務。
petition  Residents signed a petition to protect the old park.  n. 請願書  居民連署請願書，要求保護這座老公園。
pharmacist  The pharmacist explained how to take the medicine safely.  n. 藥師；藥劑師  藥師說明了如何安全地服藥。
surgeon  The surgeon met with the patient to discuss the operation.  n. 外科醫師  外科醫師與病患會面，討論手術事宜。
vaccination  Free flu vaccination is available to all employees this month.  n. 疫苗接種  本月所有員工都可免費接種流感疫苗。
immunization  Proof of immunization is required for all new students.  n. 免疫接種；預防接種  所有新生都必須提供預防接種證明。
wellbeing  The program supports the physical and mental wellbeing of staff.  n. 健康；幸福  這項計畫支持員工的身心健康。
sanitation  The city invested in improving sanitation in the older districts.  n. 公共衛生；環境衛生  市政府投資改善老舊地區的環境衛生。
pollutant  The factory reduced the amount of pollutant released into the river.  n. 汙染物  工廠減少了排入河中的汙染物量。
wildlife  The park offers guided tours to observe local wildlife.  n. 野生動物  公園提供導覽行程，讓人觀察當地的野生動物。
habitat  The project will protect the natural habitat of several rare birds.  n. 棲息地；生活環境  這項計畫將保護數種珍稀鳥類的自然棲息地。
drought  The long drought caused water prices to rise across the region.  n. 乾旱  長期的乾旱使整個地區的水價上漲。
flood  Heavy rain caused a flood that closed several roads downtown.  n. 洪水；水災  大雨引發水災，市區好幾條道路因而封閉。
sustainability  The company published a report on its sustainability goals.  n. 永續性；永續發展  公司發表了一份關於其永續發展目標的報告。
compost  The cafeteria will compost food waste to help the environment.  v. 堆肥化；n. 堆肥  自助餐廳將把廚餘製成堆肥，以協助保護環境。
fundraising  Fundraising for the new children's hospital exceeded expectations.  n. 募款；籌款  新兒童醫院的募款成果超出預期。
donor  An anonymous donor gave one million dollars to the clinic.  n. 捐贈者；捐血者  一位匿名捐贈者捐了一百萬美元給診所。
grassroots  The campaign began as a grassroots effort led by local volunteers.  adj. 基層的；草根的  這場活動起初是由當地志工發起的草根行動。
broadcaster  The national broadcaster will air the interview tonight at nine.  n. 廣播公司；播報員  國家廣播公司將在今晚九點播出這場訪談。
editorial  The editorial argues that the city should build more bike lanes.  n. 社論；adj. 編輯的  這篇社論主張市政府應興建更多自行車道。
columnist  The columnist writes about small business trends every Sunday.  n. 專欄作家  這位專欄作家每週日撰寫小型企業趨勢的文章。
journalism  She studied journalism before joining the local newspaper.  n. 新聞業；新聞學  她在加入地方報社之前主修新聞學。
podcast  The mayor discussed the new policy on a popular local podcast.  n. 播客；網路廣播節目  市長在一個受歡迎的在地播客節目中討論了這項新政策。
tabloid  The story first appeared in a tabloid and was later denied.  n. 小報；八卦報  這則報導最初出現在一份小報上，後來遭到否認。
airtime  The station gives free airtime to local nonprofit organizations.  n. 播出時間  這家電視台提供免費播出時間給當地的非營利組織。
viewership  Viewership of the evening news has increased by ten percent.  n. 收視人數；觀眾群  晚間新聞的收視人數增加了百分之十。
newscast  The evening newscast will cover the mayor's announcement live.  n. 新聞廣播；新聞節目  晚間新聞節目將現場報導市長的宣布內容。
hashtag  Please use the official hashtag when posting photos of the event.  n. 主題標籤  發布活動照片時，請使用官方主題標籤。
blog  The clinic's blog offers tips on staying healthy during winter.  n. 部落格  診所的部落格提供冬季保健的小訣竅。
tweet  The city sent a tweet to warn drivers about road closures.  n. 推文  市政府發了一則推文，提醒駕駛人道路封閉的消息。
paramedic  A paramedic arrived within minutes and treated the injured worker.  n. 急救人員；救護技術員  一名救護技術員在幾分鐘內抵達，並救治了受傷的工人。
ward  The children's ward on the fourth floor will reopen next week.  n. 病房；病區  四樓的兒童病房將於下週重新開放。
stethoscope  The nurse placed a stethoscope on the patient's chest.  n. 聽診器  護理師把聽診器放在病患的胸口上。
outpatient  Outpatient services are available from eight to five on weekdays.  n. 門診病人  門診服務在平日八點到五點提供。
inpatient  Inpatient care is covered under the standard insurance plan.  n. 住院病人  住院照護屬於標準保險方案的給付範圍。
bedside  The doctor spent time at the bedside of each patient during rounds.  n. 病床邊；床邊  醫師查房時在每位病患的床邊都花了一些時間。
chronic  The clinic offers treatment programs for chronic back pain.  adj. 慢性的；長期的  診所提供慢性背痛的治療方案。
contagious  The illness is highly contagious, so please stay home if you feel sick.  adj. 具傳染性的  這種疾病傳染性很強，所以身體不適時請留在家中。
# ===== 28 通用高頻動詞 =====
require  The position will require at least three years of sales experience.  v. 需要；要求  這個職位需要至少三年的業務經驗。
provide  The hotel will provide a free shuttle service to the airport.  v. 提供  飯店將提供前往機場的免費接駁服務。
obtain  You must obtain written permission before copying any of these documents.  v. 取得；獲得  複製這些文件之前，您必須先取得書面許可。
arrange  Our assistant arranged a meeting with the new supplier for Tuesday.  v. 安排；籌備  我們的助理安排了週二與新供應商會面。
receive  Employees will receive their bonuses at the end of the month.  v. 收到；接受  員工將在月底收到獎金。
ensure  The inspector will ensure that every machine meets safety standards.  v. 確保  檢查員將確保每台機器都符合安全標準。
indicate  The survey results indicate that customers prefer shorter delivery times.  v. 顯示；表明  調查結果顯示顧客偏好較短的送貨時間。
involve  The new project involves several departments across the company.  v. 涉及；牽涉  這項新專案牽涉公司內的多個部門。
include  The price includes breakfast and free parking.  v. 包含  這個價格包含早餐和免費停車。
establish  The company established its headquarters in Singapore in 2010.  v. 建立；設立  該公司於二〇一〇年在新加坡設立總部。
maintain  Regular cleaning helps maintain the quality of the equipment.  v. 維護；維持  定期清潔有助於維持設備的品質。
facilitate  The software facilitates communication between remote team members.  v. 促進；使便利  這套軟體能促進遠端團隊成員之間的溝通。
generate  The new campaign generated a great deal of interest among customers.  v. 產生；創造  這項新活動在顧客之間引起了極大的興趣。
reduce  The new machines helped reduce production costs significantly.  v. 減少；降低  新機器大幅降低了生產成本。
refer  Please refer to the attached manual for installation instructions.  v. 參考；提及  安裝說明請參閱附上的手冊。
refer  The doctor will refer you to a specialist.  v. 轉介；介紹至  醫師會將你轉介給專科醫師。
remain  Several positions remain open in the accounting department.  v. 保持；仍然  會計部門仍有數個職缺開放。
represent  Our sales team represents more than thirty international brands.  v. 代表；代理  我們的業務團隊代理超過三十個國際品牌。
suspend  The airline suspended all flights because of the heavy snow.  v. 暫停；中止  由於大雪，航空公司暫停了所有航班。
undergo  The building will undergo major renovations this summer.  v. 經歷；接受  這棟大樓今年夏天將進行大規模整修。
utilize  The firm utilizes solar energy to power its factory.  v. 利用  這家公司利用太陽能為工廠供電。
conduct  The researchers will conduct a survey of local residents.  v. 進行；實施  研究人員將對當地居民進行問卷調查。
consult  You should consult a lawyer before signing the agreement.  v. 諮詢；查閱  簽署協議前，你應該先諮詢律師。
contribute  Every team member contributed ideas to the final proposal.  v. 貢獻；捐助  每位團隊成員都為最終提案貢獻了想法。
determine  We need more data to determine the cause of the delay.  v. 決定；查明  我們需要更多資料來查明延誤的原因。
eliminate  The new system will eliminate unnecessary paperwork.  v. 消除；淘汰  新系統將免除不必要的文書作業。
encounter  Users may encounter errors when they try to log in.  v. 遭遇；碰到  使用者嘗試登入時可能會遇到錯誤。
gather  Staff gathered in the lobby for the safety briefing.  v. 聚集；收集  員工聚集在大廳聽取安全說明。
compile  The assistant will compile the sales figures into a single report.  v. 彙編；整理  助理會將銷售數據彙整成一份報告。
perform  The new software will perform all calculations automatically.  v. 執行；運作  新軟體會自動執行所有運算。
perform  The sales team performed well during the third quarter.  v. 表現  業務團隊在第三季表現良好。
prevent  Regular maintenance can prevent costly equipment failures.  v. 防止；避免  定期保養可以避免代價高昂的設備故障。
enable  The upgrade will enable employees to work from home securely.  v. 使能夠；啟用  這次升級將讓員工能安全地在家工作。
observe  All visitors must observe the safety rules posted at the entrance.  v. 遵守；觀察  所有訪客都必須遵守入口處張貼的安全規定。
tackle  We need a clear plan to tackle the backlog of customer complaints.  v. 處理；解決  我們需要一個明確的計畫來處理積壓的客訴。
preserve  The museum works hard to preserve its collection of historic documents.  v. 保存；維護  博物館努力保存其歷史文獻收藏。
reveal  The survey results reveal that most customers prefer online ordering.  v. 揭露；顯示  調查結果顯示，多數顧客偏好線上訂購。
rely  Many small businesses rely on seasonal workers during the holidays.  v. 依賴；仰賴  許多小型企業在假期期間仰賴季節性員工。
simplify  The new form will simplify the application process for all candidates.  v. 簡化  新表格將為所有應徵者簡化申請流程。
stress  The manager stressed the importance of meeting every deadline.  v. 強調  經理強調了達成每個截止期限的重要性。
stress  Constant overtime can stress even the most experienced employees.  v. 使感到壓力  持續加班連最資深的員工也會感到壓力。
urge  The director urged all staff to submit their expense reports on time.  v. 力勸；敦促  主任敦促所有員工準時提交費用報告。
weigh  Please weigh the costs and benefits before approving the proposal.  v. 權衡；衡量  核准提案前，請先權衡成本與效益。
advise  We advise customers to back up their files before updating the software.  v. 建議；勸告  我們建議顧客在更新軟體前先備份檔案。
intend  We intend to open a second branch in Osaka next spring.  v. 打算；意圖  我們打算明年春天在大阪開設第二間分公司。
convert  The factory will convert its old warehouse into a training center.  v. 轉換；改建  工廠將把舊倉庫改建成訓練中心。
derive  Most of our income is derived from long-term service contracts.  v. 取得；源自  我們大部分收入來自長期服務合約。
detect  The security system can detect unusual activity on the network.  v. 偵測；察覺  這套資安系統能偵測網路上的異常活動。
distinguish  It is hard to distinguish the original product from the cheap copies.  v. 區分；辨別  很難將原廠產品與廉價仿冒品區分開來。
embrace  The company has embraced remote work as a permanent policy.  v. 欣然接受；採用  公司已將遠距工作採納為長期政策。
foster  The workshop aims to foster cooperation between different departments.  v. 促進；培養  這場工作坊旨在促進各部門之間的合作。
impose  The city plans to impose a new fee on commercial parking lots.  v. 徵收；強制實施  市政府計畫對商業停車場徵收新費用。
incur  Late payments may incur additional charges under the terms of the contract.  v. 招致；產生費用  依合約條款，逾期付款可能產生額外費用。
interpret  Analysts interpret the latest figures as a sign of steady growth.  v. 解讀；詮釋  分析師將最新數據解讀為穩定成長的跡象。
justify  Can you justify the need for such a large budget increase?  v. 證明…合理；為…辯護  你能說明為何需要大幅增加預算嗎？
overcome  The firm managed to overcome a serious shortage of parts last year.  v. 克服  公司去年成功克服了嚴重的零件短缺。
overlook  Please do not overlook the deadline printed at the top of the form.  v. 忽略；漏看  請勿忽略表格上方印出的截止日期。
perceive  Customers often perceive higher prices as a sign of better quality.  v. 認為；感知  顧客常把較高的價格視為品質較好的象徵。
proceed  If you agree with the terms, please proceed to the checkout page.  v. 繼續進行；前往  若同意這些條款，請繼續前往結帳頁面。
render  The consultant will render a full report on the findings by Friday.  v. 提出；使成為  顧問將於週五前提出一份完整的調查結果報告。
shape  Customer feedback will shape our next product design.  v. 塑造；影響  顧客意見將左右我們下一款產品的設計。
widen  The bank hopes to widen its customer base among young professionals.  v. 擴大；拓寬  這家銀行希望擴大在年輕上班族之間的客群。
withstand  The new packaging can withstand extreme temperatures during shipping.  v. 承受；經得起  新包裝能承受運送過程中的極端溫度。
advocate  Several board members advocate a more cautious investment strategy.  v. 提倡；主張  幾位董事主張採取較為謹慎的投資策略。
alleviate  Additional staff will help alleviate the pressure on the support team.  v. 減輕；緩和  增派人手將有助減輕客服團隊的壓力。
assert  The report asserts that overall productivity has risen this year.  v. 斷言；主張  報告斷言今年整體生產力已經提升。
attribute  Management attributed the sales decline to increased competition.  v. 把…歸因於  管理階層將銷售下滑歸因於競爭加劇。
cease  The factory will cease production of the older model in March.  v. 停止；終止  工廠將於三月停止生產舊款機型。
cite  The article cites several studies on workplace productivity.  v. 引用；舉出  這篇文章引用了數項有關職場生產力的研究。
commend  The CEO commended the staff for their outstanding efforts.  v. 稱讚；表揚  執行長表揚員工傑出的努力。
compel  New regulations compel companies to disclose their environmental impact.  v. 迫使；強迫  新法規迫使企業揭露其對環境的影響。
comprise  The committee comprises members from five different departments.  v. 由…組成；包含  委員會由來自五個不同部門的成員組成。
constitute  Online sales now constitute half of the retailer's total revenue.  v. 構成；佔  線上銷售目前佔這家零售商總營收的一半。
cope  Our small staff struggled to cope with the sudden rise in orders.  v. 應付；處理  我們人手不多，難以應付訂單的突然增加。
curb  The government introduced measures to curb rising housing costs.  v. 抑制；控制  政府推出措施來抑制不斷上漲的住房成本。
deem  The proposal was deemed too risky by the finance committee.  v. 認為；視為  財務委員會認為這項提案風險太高。
depict  The brochure depicts the resort as a quiet and relaxing destination.  v. 描繪；描述  手冊把這座度假村描繪成安靜放鬆的去處。
deter  High prices may deter customers from buying the premium model.  v. 阻止；使打消念頭  高價可能使顧客打消購買高階機型的念頭。
devise  The engineers devised a simple method to reduce waste.  v. 設計；想出  工程師想出了一個減少浪費的簡單方法。
devote  The company devotes ten percent of its profits to community programs.  v. 投入；奉獻  公司將百分之十的利潤投入社區計畫。
diminish  Frequent delays have diminished our reputation among clients.  v. 減少；削弱  頻繁的延誤削弱了我們在客戶之間的聲譽。
elevate  The new campaign aims to elevate the brand's image among younger buyers.  v. 提升；提高  這項新活動旨在提升品牌在年輕買家心中的形象。
endeavor  We endeavor to answer all inquiries within one business day.  v. 努力；盡力  我們盡力在一個工作日內回覆所有詢問。
enrich  The training program will enrich the skills of our junior staff.  v. 充實；使豐富  培訓計畫將充實資淺員工的技能。
exert  The union exerted pressure on management to improve safety conditions.  v. 施加；運用  工會向管理階層施壓，要求改善安全條件。
exploit  The startup hopes to exploit a gap in the mobile payment market.  v. 利用；開發  這家新創公司希望利用行動支付市場的空缺。
expose  The audit exposed several errors in the quarterly accounts.  v. 揭發；使暴露  這次查核揭發了季度帳目中的數項錯誤。
govern  Strict rules govern the handling of customer data.  v. 規範；管理  嚴格的規定規範著客戶資料的處理。
grasp  New employees quickly grasped the basics of the billing system.  v. 理解；掌握  新進員工很快就掌握了計費系統的基本操作。
hinge  The success of the launch hinges on timely delivery of the parts.  v. 取決於  產品上市能否成功，取決於零件是否準時送達。
hamper  Poor weather hampered efforts to deliver supplies to the site.  v. 妨礙；阻礙  惡劣天氣阻礙了將物資送往現場的努力。
impair  Outdated equipment can impair the quality of our final products.  v. 損害；削弱  過時的設備可能損害我們最終產品的品質。
inspire  The keynote speaker inspired the audience with her story of success.  v. 激勵；鼓舞  主講人以她的成功故事鼓舞了聽眾。
intervene  The supervisor had to intervene when the discussion became heated.  v. 介入；干預  討論變得激烈時，主管不得不介入。
invoke  The tenant invoked a clause in the lease to end the agreement early.  v. 援引；行使  承租人援引租約中的一項條款提前終止合約。
offset  Lower shipping fees will help offset the rise in material costs.  v. 抵銷；彌補  較低的運費將有助抵銷材料成本的上漲。
omit  Please do not omit any item from the final inventory list.  v. 省略；遺漏  請勿遺漏最終庫存清單上的任何項目。
prolong  Regular servicing will prolong the life of the machinery.  v. 延長  定期維修保養將延長機器的使用壽命。
quantify  It is difficult to quantify the benefits of employee training.  v. 量化  員工訓練的效益很難量化。
refrain  Passengers are asked to refrain from using electronic devices during takeoff.  v. 避免；克制  請乘客在起飛期間不要使用電子裝置。
regain  The company hopes to regain its position as the market leader.  v. 重新獲得；恢復  公司希望重新奪回市場領導者的地位。
relieve  The extra funding will relieve some of the financial pressure on the school.  v. 緩解；減輕  額外的經費將減輕學校的部分財務壓力。
stimulate  Lower interest rates may stimulate consumer spending.  v. 刺激；促進  較低的利率可能刺激消費支出。
adhere  All staff must adhere to the safety guidelines at all times.  v. 遵守；堅持  所有員工隨時都必須遵守安全準則。
ascertain  We need to ascertain the cause of the delay before we reply.  v. 查明；確定  回覆之前，我們需要先查明延誤的原因。
augment  The company hired temporary workers to augment its regular staff.  v. 增加；擴充  公司僱用臨時員工來擴充正職人力。
bolster  The new campaign should bolster sales in the coming quarter.  v. 加強；支撐  新活動應能在下一季提振銷售。
coincide  The product launch will coincide with the trade show in May.  v. 同時發生；相符  產品上市將與五月的貿易展同時舉行。
conform  All packaging must conform to international standards.  v. 符合；遵照  所有包裝都必須符合國際標準。
curtail  The firm had to curtail its spending after the sales drop.  v. 縮減；削減  銷售下滑後，公司不得不縮減開支。
erode  Rising costs continue to erode our profit margins.  v. 侵蝕；逐漸削弱  不斷上升的成本持續侵蝕我們的利潤率。
jeopardize  Missing this deadline could jeopardize the entire contract.  v. 危害；使陷入危險  錯過這個期限可能危及整份合約。
liaise  She will liaise with the vendor to confirm the delivery date.  v. 聯絡；做為聯繫窗口  她會與供應商聯絡以確認交貨日期。
preclude  The tight schedule may preclude any further changes to the design.  v. 排除；使不可能  緊湊的時程可能使設計無法再做任何更動。
prevail  We hope that common sense will prevail during the negotiations.  v. 占上風；盛行  我們希望談判期間理性能占上風。
reside  Applicants must reside within the city limits to qualify.  v. 居住；定居  申請人必須居住在市區範圍內才符合資格。
reprimand  The manager decided to reprimand the employee for repeated lateness.  v. 申誡；斥責  經理決定因該員工一再遲到而予以申誡。
uphold  The court ruled to uphold the original decision.  v. 維持；支持  法院裁定維持原判決。
undermine  Constant delays can undermine the trust of our customers.  v. 破壞；逐漸削弱  持續的延誤可能損害顧客對我們的信任。
# ===== 29 通用高頻形容詞 =====
subsequent  Subsequent payments will be charged to your credit card automatically.  adj. 隨後的；後續的  後續款項將自動從你的信用卡扣款。
tentative  The tentative schedule is subject to change without notice.  adj. 暫定的  暫定時程可能不另行通知而變更。
responsible  The manager is responsible for training new employees.  adj. 負責的  經理負責訓練新進員工。
efficient  The new software makes our billing process more efficient.  adj. 有效率的  新軟體讓我們的計費流程更有效率。
significant  The merger will have a significant impact on our workforce.  adj. 重大的；顯著的  這次合併將對我們的員工產生重大影響。
current  Please check that your current address is correct.  adj. 目前的；現行的  請確認你目前的地址是否正確。
previous  The previous manager left the company in June.  adj. 先前的  前任經理在六月離開了公司。
additional  Additional fees may apply for international shipping.  adj. 額外的  國際運送可能會收取額外費用。
necessary  It is necessary to submit receipts for all expenses.  adj. 必要的  所有支出都必須繳交收據。
sufficient  We do not have sufficient funds to cover the repairs.  adj. 足夠的  我們沒有足夠的資金支付維修費用。
appropriate  Casual clothing is not appropriate for the client meeting.  adj. 適當的  休閒服裝不適合穿去客戶會議。
optional  The extended warranty is optional and costs an extra fifty dollars.  adj. 可選擇的  延長保固是自由選購的，需另付五十美元。
beneficial  Regular exercise is beneficial to both employees and the company.  adj. 有益的  規律運動對員工和公司都有益。
convenient  The hotel is in a convenient location near the station.  adj. 方便的  這間飯店位置便利，離車站很近。
thorough  A thorough inspection of the equipment takes about two hours.  adj. 徹底的；詳盡的  徹底檢查這套設備大約需要兩小時。
preliminary  Preliminary results of the survey will be released on Monday.  adj. 初步的  調查的初步結果將於週一公布。
applicable  This discount is applicable only to orders over one hundred dollars.  adj. 適用的  此優惠僅適用於金額超過一百美元的訂單。
substantial  The company reported a substantial increase in annual profits.  adj. 大量的；可觀的  公司公布年度獲利大幅增加。
considerable  The project required considerable time and effort from the team.  adj. 相當大的  這個專案需要團隊投入相當多的時間和心力。
diverse  Our diverse workforce speaks more than ten languages.  adj. 多元的  我們多元的員工團隊會說十幾種語言。
innovative  The firm is known for its innovative product designs.  adj. 創新的  這家公司以創新的產品設計聞名。
punctual  Please be punctual, because the meeting will start at nine sharp.  adj. 準時的  請準時，因為會議將於九點整開始。
lucrative  She left her job for a more lucrative position abroad.  adj. 利潤豐厚的；高薪的  她離開原職，轉往國外一個薪酬更優厚的職位。
adequate  The office does not have adequate storage space.  adj. 足夠的；適當的  這間辦公室沒有足夠的儲物空間。
reputable  Please buy tickets only from a reputable dealer.  adj. 信譽良好的  請只向信譽良好的經銷商購買門票。
renowned  The conference will feature a renowned expert in robotics.  adj. 著名的  這場研討會將邀請一位知名的機器人專家。
compliant  All products must be compliant with international safety standards.  adj. 符合規定的  所有產品都必須符合國際安全標準。
cumbersome  The old approval process was slow and cumbersome.  adj. 繁瑣的；笨重的  舊的核准流程既緩慢又繁瑣。
gradual  The report shows a gradual improvement in sales.  adj. 逐漸的  報告顯示銷售量逐步改善。
immediate  The system requires immediate attention from the technical team.  adj. 立即的  這套系統需要技術團隊立即處理。
inevitable  Some delays are inevitable during major construction projects.  adj. 無可避免的  大型工程進行期間，部分延誤是無可避免的。
informative  The brochure is informative and easy to read.  adj. 資訊豐富的  這本手冊資訊豐富又易於閱讀。
insufficient  Insufficient funds caused the bank to reject the payment.  adj. 不足的  由於資金不足，銀行拒絕了這筆付款。
intensive  The program offers intensive training in project management.  adj. 密集的  該課程提供專案管理的密集訓練。
keen  The firm shows a keen interest in expanding overseas.  adj. 熱切的；敏銳的  這家公司對拓展海外市場展現出濃厚的興趣。
lenient  The new return policy is more lenient than the old one.  adj. 寬鬆的  新的退貨政策比舊的更寬鬆。
meticulous  The accountant is meticulous about checking every figure.  adj. 一絲不苟的  這位會計師對每個數字的核對都一絲不苟。
noticeable  There has been a noticeable rise in online orders.  adj. 顯著的  線上訂單明顯增加。
numerous  Numerous customers have complained about the long waiting times.  adj. 許多的  許多顧客抱怨等候時間太長。
promising  The young designer has a promising future with the firm.  adj. 有前途的  這位年輕設計師在公司裡前途看好。
rigorous  New products undergo rigorous testing before they reach stores.  adj. 嚴格的  新產品在上架前都要經過嚴格的測試。
scarce  Skilled workers have become scarce in this region.  adj. 稀少的；短缺的  這個地區的技術人員已變得稀少。
secure  Please store your passwords in a secure location.  adj. 安全的；v. 確保  請將你的密碼存放在安全的地方。
sensitive  Please do not share sensitive data over unsecured networks.  adj. 敏感的；機密的  請勿透過不安全的網路分享敏感資料。
stringent  The country has stringent rules for food imports.  adj. 嚴格的  該國對食品進口有嚴格的規定。
tangible  The training produced tangible results within three months.  adj. 實質的；明確的  這項訓練在三個月內就產生了實質的成果。
unanimous  The board reached a unanimous decision after a short discussion.  adj. 一致同意的  董事會在簡短討論後達成一致決議。
unprecedented  The company achieved unprecedented growth last year.  adj. 前所未有的  公司去年達成了前所未有的成長。
viable  We are looking for a viable alternative to the current supplier.  adj. 可行的  我們正在尋找目前供應商之外可行的替代選擇。
widespread  There was widespread confusion about the new schedule.  adj. 普遍的  大家對新時程普遍感到困惑。
vulnerable  Old software can make the network vulnerable to attacks.  adj. 易受攻擊的  老舊軟體可能使網路容易遭受攻擊。
notable  The report mentions several notable achievements of the sales team.  adj. 顯著的；值得注意的  報告提到了業務團隊的數項顯著成就。
adjacent  The meeting room is adjacent to the main lobby.  adj. 相鄰的  會議室緊鄰主大廳。
ample  The venue has ample parking for all of our guests.  adj. 充足的  會場有充足的停車位可供所有賓客使用。
contemporary  The gallery displays contemporary art from local designers.  adj. 當代的  這間畫廊展出本地設計師的當代藝術作品。
crucial  Clear communication is crucial to the success of any project.  adj. 至關重要的  清楚的溝通對任何專案的成功都至關重要。
vital  Customer feedback is vital for improving our service.  adj. 極重要的；必要的  顧客意見對改善我們的服務極為重要。
complex  The tax rules for overseas income are quite complex.  adj. 複雜的  海外所得的稅務規定相當複雜。
obvious  The reason for the sudden drop in sales was not obvious.  adj. 明顯的  銷售量突然下滑的原因並不明顯。
apparent  It soon became apparent that the schedule was unrealistic.  adj. 顯而易見的  很快就能看出這份時程不切實際。
extensive  The hotel recently completed extensive renovations to its lobby.  adj. 廣泛的；大規模的  這間飯店最近完成了大廳的大規模整修。
massive  The retailer reported a massive increase in online orders.  adj. 巨大的；大量的  這家零售商公布線上訂單大幅成長。
minimal  The new process requires minimal training for staff.  adj. 最小的；極少的  新流程只需要員工接受極少的訓練。
optimal  Store the medicine at room temperature for optimal results.  adj. 最佳的  藥品請存放於室溫下以達最佳效果。
rapid  The region has seen rapid growth in the tourism industry.  adj. 快速的  該地區的觀光產業快速成長。
steady  The company has enjoyed steady profits for ten years.  adj. 穩定的；持續的  這家公司十年來獲利穩定。
distinct  Each department has a distinct role within the organization.  adj. 獨特的；明顯不同的  每個部門在組織內都有各自獨特的角色。
fundamental  The two firms have fundamental differences over pricing.  adj. 基本的；根本的  兩家公司在定價上有根本的分歧。
dynamic  We are looking for a dynamic leader to guide our sales team.  adj. 有活力的；動態的  我們正在尋找一位有活力的領導者來帶領業務團隊。
comparable  The two models offer comparable performance at very different prices.  adj. 可比較的；相當的  這兩款機型效能相當，價格卻差很多。
equivalent  A bachelor's degree or equivalent experience is required for this position.  adj. 相當的；同等的  這個職位需要學士學位或同等經驗。
explicit  The contract gives explicit instructions on how to request a refund.  adj. 明確的  合約中明確說明了申請退款的方式。
implicit  There was an implicit agreement not to discuss the matter publicly.  adj. 隱含的；不言明的  大家心照不宣地同意不公開討論這件事。
inherent  Every investment carries some inherent risk.  adj. 固有的；內在的  每項投資都帶有一些固有的風險。
persistent  Persistent noise from the construction site upset the neighbors.  adj. 持續的；堅持不懈的  工地持續不斷的噪音讓鄰居很不滿。
reluctant  Some customers are reluctant to share personal information online.  adj. 不情願的；勉強的  有些顧客不願在網路上提供個人資料。
diligent  Mr. Park is a diligent employee who always meets his deadlines.  adj. 勤奮的；認真的  朴先生是個勤奮的員工，總是如期完成工作。
eager  We are eager to hear your ideas at tomorrow's meeting.  adj. 渴望的；熱切的  我們很期待在明天的會議上聽到你的想法。
remarkable  The team made remarkable progress in only three months.  adj. 顯著的；非凡的  團隊在短短三個月內就有了顯著的進步。
sophisticated  The factory uses sophisticated machines to inspect every part.  adj. 精密的；複雜的  工廠使用精密的機器檢查每一個零件。
prominent  A prominent economist will speak at the conference.  adj. 著名的；突出的  一位知名經濟學家將在研討會上發表演說。
prestigious  She received a prestigious award for her contribution to design.  adj. 有聲望的；著名的  她因對設計的貢獻而獲頒一項享有盛譽的獎項。
adverse  Adverse weather conditions forced the airline to cancel several flights.  adj. 不利的；有害的  惡劣的天候迫使航空公司取消了數個航班。
abundant  The region has abundant natural resources for energy production.  adj. 豐富的；大量的  該地區擁有豐富的天然資源可用於能源生產。
conventional  Solar panels cost less than conventional power sources in many areas.  adj. 傳統的；慣例的  在許多地區，太陽能板的成本低於傳統能源。
conducive  A quiet workspace is conducive to productivity.  adj. 有助於的  安靜的工作空間有助於提升生產力。
consecutive  The firm reported record profits for the fifth consecutive quarter.  adj. 連續的  這家公司連續第五季公布創紀錄的獲利。
coherent  The proposal lacks a coherent plan for reducing costs.  adj. 連貫的；有條理的  這份提案缺乏連貫的成本削減計畫。
considerate  Please be considerate of colleagues when using the shared kitchen.  adj. 體貼的；考慮周到的  使用共用廚房時，請體諒同事。
discreet  Please be discreet when discussing the merger with outsiders.  adj. 謹慎的；不張揚的  與外人討論合併案時請謹慎行事。
imminent  The company announced that a restructuring is imminent.  adj. 即將發生的；迫近的  公司宣布組織重整即將展開。
inclusive  The monthly fee is inclusive of all taxes and service charges.  adj. 包含在內的；包容的  月費包含所有稅金和服務費。
instrumental  She was instrumental in securing the contract with our largest client.  adj. 起重要作用的  她在為我們最大的客戶爭取到合約一事上發揮了關鍵作用。
integral  Customer service is an integral part of our business model.  adj. 不可或缺的；整體的  客戶服務是我們商業模式中不可或缺的一環。
intricate  The intricate design of the watch requires skilled craftsmen.  adj. 錯綜複雜的；精細的  這支手錶設計精細複雜，需要熟練的工匠製作。
invaluable  Her experience proved invaluable during the negotiations.  adj. 極寶貴的  她的經驗在談判期間證明極為寶貴。
overwhelming  The overwhelming majority of staff voted in favor of the plan.  adj. 壓倒性的；極大的  絕大多數員工投票贊成這項計畫。
paramount  Customer safety is paramount in everything we do.  adj. 至關重要的；首要的  顧客安全是我們一切作為中的首要考量。
pivotal  The new director played a pivotal role in the company's recovery.  adj. 關鍵的；樞紐的  新任總監在公司的復甦中扮演了關鍵角色。
vague  The instructions in the email were too vague to follow.  adj. 模糊的；含糊的  電子郵件裡的說明太含糊，無法照著做。
prudent  It would be prudent to review the contract before signing it.  adj. 審慎的；明智的  簽約前先審閱合約會是明智之舉。
vigilant  Staff should remain vigilant when handling confidential data.  adj. 警覺的；警惕的  員工處理機密資料時應保持警覺。
credible  The report cites credible sources and recent survey data.  adj. 可信的；可靠的  這份報告引用了可靠的資料來源與最新的調查數據。
plausible  The supplier gave a plausible explanation for the late shipment.  adj. 說得通的；可信的  供應商對延遲出貨提出了說得通的解釋。
prevalent  Remote work has become prevalent in many industries.  adj. 普遍的；盛行的  遠距工作在許多產業已變得相當普遍。
hectic  The week before the audit is always hectic for the finance team.  adj. 忙亂的；繁忙的  稽核前一週，財務團隊總是忙亂不堪。
astute  She is an astute negotiator who rarely misses an opportunity.  adj. 精明的；敏銳的  她是精明的談判者，很少錯過任何機會。
tedious  Entering the data by hand is a tedious task.  adj. 乏味的；冗長的  手動輸入資料是件乏味的工作。
candid  He gave a candid assessment of the project's weaknesses.  adj. 坦率的；直言的  他對這個專案的缺點做出了坦率的評估。
# ===== 30 通用高頻副詞、介系詞、連接詞與公告用語 =====
approximately  The trip takes approximately three hours by train.  adv. 大約  火車行程大約需要三小時。
regarding  I am writing regarding your recent order.  prep. 關於  我來信是為了詢問您最近的訂單。
however  The offer is generous; however, we need more time to decide.  adv. 然而  這項優惠很優渥；然而，我們需要更多時間考慮。
therefore  The warehouse is full; therefore, we cannot accept more shipments.  adv. 因此  倉庫已滿；因此，我們無法再接收更多貨物。
although  Sales fell in May, although profits remained stable.  conj. 雖然  五月銷售下滑，不過利潤仍維持穩定。
despite  The event was a success despite the bad weather.  prep. 儘管  儘管天氣不佳，活動仍十分成功。
whereas  Sales rose in Asia, whereas they fell in Europe.  conj. 然而；反之  亞洲的銷售成長，而歐洲則下滑。
nevertheless  The task was difficult; nevertheless, the team finished on time.  adv. 儘管如此  這項任務很困難；儘管如此，團隊仍準時完成。
furthermore  The plan is affordable; furthermore, it is easy to implement.  adv. 此外  這項計畫價格實惠；此外，也很容易執行。
moreover  The software is fast; moreover, it is very easy to use.  adv. 而且；再者  這套軟體速度很快；而且非常容易使用。
meanwhile  Please start the setup; meanwhile, I will contact the vendor.  adv. 同時；在此期間  請先開始設定；在此期間，我會聯絡廠商。
accordingly  Prices have risen, and we have adjusted our fees accordingly.  adv. 相應地  價格已上漲，我們也已相應調整收費。
consequently  The supplier was late; consequently, production stopped for two days.  adv. 因此；結果  供應商延遲交貨；結果生產停擺了兩天。
otherwise  Please arrive early; otherwise, you may miss the opening remarks.  adv. 否則；不然  請提早抵達；否則您可能會錯過開場致詞。
unless  The meeting will begin on time unless the speaker is delayed.  conj. 除非  除非講者遲到，否則會議將準時開始。
throughout  The store offers discounts throughout the month of December.  prep. 遍及；整個期間  商店在整個十二月都提供折扣。
within  Please send your reply within five business days of receiving this letter.  prep. 在...之內  請在收到本信後五個工作天內回覆。
effective  The new policy is effective as of January first.  adj. 生效的；有效的  這項新政策自一月一日起生效。
pursuant  The notice was issued pursuant to Section Four of the agreement.  prep. 依照（pursuant to）  本通知依據協議第四條發出。
hereby  I hereby confirm that the information above is accurate.  adv. 特此  我特此確認以上資訊正確無誤。
thereafter  The fee is waived for one year and applies thereafter.  adv. 此後  費用第一年免收，之後開始收取。
whereby  The firm launched a system whereby customers can track orders online.  conj. 藉此  該公司推出了一套讓客戶可在線上追蹤訂單的系統。
herein  The terms described herein apply to all customers.  adv. 於此文件中  本文所述條款適用於所有客戶。
therein  The contract and the terms contained therein are legally binding.  adv. 在其中  合約及其中所載條款均具法律效力。
thereof  The tenant must pay the rent or any portion thereof by Friday.  adv. 其；它的（指前述事物）  承租人須於週五前繳付租金或其任何部分。
hereafter  The company, hereafter called the Supplier, will deliver all goods.  adv. 以下（文件中）  本公司以下簡稱供應商，將負責交付所有貨品。
notwithstanding  The event will proceed notwithstanding the heavy rain forecast for Saturday.  prep. 儘管  儘管週六預報有大雨，活動仍將照常舉行。
beforehand  Please read the instructions beforehand so the session runs smoothly.  adv. 事先  請事先閱讀說明，讓課程順利進行。
afterward  Lunch will be served, and the tour begins afterward.  adv. 之後  午餐將會供應，導覽隨後開始。
currently  We are currently hiring two sales assistants.  adv. 目前  我們目前正在招聘兩名業務助理。
previously  The product was previously sold only in Japan.  adv. 先前  這項產品先前只在日本販售。
recently  The firm recently opened an office in Toronto.  adv. 最近  該公司最近在多倫多開設了辦事處。
promptly  Please pay the invoice promptly to avoid late fees.  adv. 迅速地；準時地  請及時支付發票款項以避免滯納金。
immediately  Please call the help desk immediately if the system fails.  adv. 立即  如果系統故障，請立即致電服務台。
shortly  The presentation will begin shortly in the main hall.  adv. 不久  簡報即將在大廳開始。
formerly  Our director was formerly an engineer at a major airline.  adv. 以前  我們的總監以前是一家大型航空公司的工程師。
subsequently  The file was subsequently deleted by mistake.  adv. 隨後  該檔案隨後被誤刪了。
respectively  Sales reached two and three million dollars in May and June, respectively.  adv. 分別地  五月和六月的銷售額分別達到兩百萬和三百萬美元。
particularly  Demand was particularly strong in the northern region.  adv. 特別  北部地區的需求特別強勁。
especially  Delivery is especially important for small online businesses.  adv. 尤其  對小型網路商家來說，配送尤其重要。
significantly  Fuel costs have increased significantly since last year.  adv. 顯著地  自去年以來，燃料成本大幅上升。
substantially  The new design substantially reduces assembly time.  adv. 大幅地  新設計大幅縮短了組裝時間。
roughly  The project will cost roughly two million dollars.  adv. 大約  這個專案的成本大約是兩百萬美元。
nearly  The warehouse is nearly empty after the holiday sale.  adv. 幾乎  假期特賣後，倉庫幾乎空了。
merely  The fee is merely a deposit and will be refunded.  adv. 僅僅  這筆費用只是押金，將會退還。
solely  The decision was based solely on customer feedback.  adv. 僅；完全  這項決定完全是根據客戶意見做出的。
exclusively  This offer is available exclusively to registered members.  adv. 專門地；僅限  這項優惠僅限註冊會員。
primarily  The company serves clients primarily in the healthcare sector.  adv. 主要地  該公司服務的客戶主要集中在醫療保健產業。
mainly  Our customers are mainly small businesses in the area.  adv. 主要  我們的客戶大多是當地的小型企業。
largely  The delay was largely caused by a shortage of parts.  adv. 大部分；主要  延誤主要是因為零件短缺。
typically  Orders typically arrive within three to five business days.  adv. 通常  訂單通常在三到五個工作天內送達。
occasionally  The server occasionally shuts down without warning.  adv. 偶爾  伺服器偶爾會在沒有預警的情況下當機。
frequently  Customers frequently ask about our return policy.  adv. 經常  顧客經常詢問我們的退貨政策。
regularly  Employees should back up their files regularly to avoid losing data.  adv. 定期地  員工應定期備份檔案，以免資料遺失。
annually  The audit is conducted annually by an outside firm.  adv. 每年  外部事務所每年進行一次稽核。
quarterly  Financial results are published quarterly on the company website.  adv. 每季；adj. 每季的  財務業績每季會公布在公司網站上。
temporarily  The pool will be temporarily closed for repairs.  adv. 暫時地  游泳池將因維修而暫時關閉。
permanently  The store has closed permanently after thirty years in business.  adv. 永久地  這家店經營三十年後已永久歇業。
initially  The project was initially expected to cost less.  adv. 起初  這個專案起初預期成本會較低。
eventually  The firm eventually won the contract after months of talks.  adv. 最終  歷經數月洽談，該公司最終贏得了合約。
ultimately  The decision will ultimately rest with the board of directors.  adv. 最終；根本上  這項決定最終將由董事會裁定。
gradually  The company gradually shifted its operations online.  adv. 逐漸地  公司逐步將業務轉移到線上。
steadily  Our online sales have grown steadily over the past year.  adv. 穩定地  我們的線上銷售在過去一年穩定成長。
sharply  Share prices fell sharply after the announcement.  adv. 急劇地  公布消息後，股價急劇下跌。
slightly  The new model is slightly larger than the old one.  adv. 稍微  新款比舊款稍微大一些。
considerably  Costs vary considerably from one supplier to another.  adv. 相當大地  各供應商的成本差異相當大。
fairly  The process is fairly simple and takes ten minutes.  adv. 相當；公平地  這個流程相當簡單，只需十分鐘。
extremely  The training session was extremely helpful for new employees.  adv. 極度地  這場培訓對新進員工極有幫助。
highly  She is a highly skilled negotiator with ten years of experience.  adv. 非常；高度地  她是技巧高超的談判者，擁有十年經驗。
thoroughly  All equipment is thoroughly tested before it leaves the factory.  adv. 徹底地  所有設備在出廠前都經過徹底測試。
alongside  The new software will run alongside the existing system.  prep. 與...並行  新軟體將與現有系統並行運作。
amid  The firm hired more staff amid growing demand for its products.  prep. 在...之中  在產品需求不斷增長的情況下，該公司增聘了人手。
beyond  The repairs went beyond what the warranty covers.  prep. 超出  這些維修超出了保固涵蓋的範圍。
concerning  Please contact us with any questions concerning your account.  prep. 關於  如對您的帳戶有任何疑問，請與我們聯絡。
considering  The results are good, considering the limited budget.  prep. 考慮到  考慮到有限的預算，這些成果相當不錯。
per  The agency charges fifty dollars per hour.  prep. 每  該機構每小時收費五十美元。
via  You can submit the form via email or fax.  prep. 經由  您可以透過電子郵件或傳真提交表格。
versus  The report compares online versus in-store sales for last year.  prep. 對比  這份報告比較了去年的線上與實體店銷售。
upon  Payment is due upon receipt of the invoice.  prep. 一經；在...時  發票一經收到即須付款。
toward  The company is moving toward a paperless office.  prep. 朝向  公司正朝著無紙化辦公室邁進。
whether  Please tell us whether you can attend the dinner.  conj. 是否  請告訴我們您是否能出席晚宴。
albeit  The results were positive, albeit slightly lower than expected.  conj. 儘管  結果是正面的，儘管略低於預期。
hence  Demand is rising, hence the need for more staff.  adv. 因此  需求正在上升，因此需要更多人手。
thus  The shipment was delayed, and thus we missed the deadline.  adv. 因此  貨物延遲了，因此我們錯過了期限。
besides  The firm has three branches overseas besides the main office.  prep. 除...之外  除了總公司之外，這家公司在海外還有三間分公司。
instead  The supplier sent a blue model instead of the red one.  adv. 反而；代替  供應商寄來的是藍色款，而不是紅色款。
likewise  Please send the form, and your colleague should do likewise.  adv. 同樣地  請寄出表格，你的同事也應照做。
whenever  Contact us whenever you need technical support.  conj. 每當  每當您需要技術支援時，請隨時聯絡我們。
wherever  You can work wherever you have an internet connection.  conj. 無論何處  只要有網路連線，你在哪裡都能工作。
whichever  Choose whichever option best fits your travel plans.  pron. 無論哪一個  請選擇最符合您旅遊計畫的任一選項。
whoever  A gift card will go to whoever completes the survey first.  pron. 無論誰  禮品卡將頒發給最先完成問卷的人。
whatever  Please send us whatever documents you have available.  pron. 無論什麼  請寄給我們您手邊有的任何文件。
namely  Two things matter most, namely cost and delivery time.  adv. 即；也就是  最重要的有兩件事，即成本和交貨時間。
notably  Several departments, notably marketing, exceeded their targets.  adv. 尤其；顯著地  有幾個部門，尤其是行銷部，超越了目標。
additionally  The package includes a laptop; additionally, it comes with a free bag.  adv. 此外  這個套組包含一台筆電；此外還附贈一個免費提袋。
alternatively  You may pay online; alternatively, you can pay in person.  adv. 或者  您可以線上付款；或者，也可以親自付款。
conversely  Costs fell in Asia; conversely, they rose in Europe.  adv. 相反地  亞洲的成本下降；相反地，歐洲則上升。
similarly  The two models are similarly priced and equally reliable.  adv. 同樣地  這兩款機型價格相近，可靠度也不相上下。
overall  Customer satisfaction was high overall this quarter.  adv. 整體而言  本季整體而言，顧客滿意度很高。
altogether  The three branches employ four hundred people altogether.  adv. 總共  三間分公司總共雇用了四百人。
# ===== 31 一字多義補充（常考的多義字） =====
issue  Please report any issue with the system to the help desk.  n. 問題；議題  請將系統的任何問題回報給服務台。
issue  The bank will issue new credit cards next month.  v. 發行；核發  銀行下個月將發行新的信用卡。
issue  The latest issue of the magazine features a story on startups.  n. 期；刊號  最新一期雜誌刊登了一篇關於新創公司的報導。
address  Please enter your home address in the box below.  n. 地址  請在下方欄位填寫您的住家地址。
address  We must address the customers' complaints as soon as possible.  v. 處理；解決  我們必須盡快處理顧客的客訴。
address  The CEO will address the staff at the annual meeting.  v. 向…發表演說  執行長將在年會上向員工發表演說。
run  She has run a small consulting firm for ten years.  v. 經營；管理  她經營一家小型顧問公司已有十年。
run  The software will run on any standard laptop.  v. 運行；執行  這套軟體可在任何標準筆電上運行。
run  The company will run an advertisement in the local paper.  v. 刊登；播出  公司將在地方報紙上刊登一則廣告。
figure  The sales figure for March exceeded our forecast.  n. 數字；數據  三月的銷售數字超出了我們的預測。
figure  I figure that we can finish the report by noon.  v. 認為；估計  我想我們中午前可以完成報告。
firm  She works at a law firm downtown.  n. 公司；事務所  她在市中心的一家律師事務所工作。
firm  We need a firm commitment before we sign the deal.  adj. 堅定的；確定的  我們在簽約前需要確定的承諾。
matter  This is a matter for the human resources department.  n. 事情；事務  這件事屬於人力資源部門的職責。
matter  Your feedback matters a great deal to our team.  v. 重要；有關係  您的意見對我們團隊非常重要。
cover  The warranty will cover repairs for two years.  v. 涵蓋；保障  保固將涵蓋兩年的維修。
cover  Our budget will not cover the cost of a new server.  v. 支付；足以支付  我們的預算不足以支付一台新伺服器的費用。
minute  Please take the minutes during today's meeting.  n. 會議記錄（常用複數）  請在今天的會議中負責做會議記錄。
case  The legal team will present its case to the court on Tuesday.  n. 案件；訴訟  法務團隊將於週二向法院陳述其案件。
case  Pack the equipment in a protective case before shipping.  n. 箱；盒；保護套  出貨前請將設備裝入保護箱中。
act  The new act requires companies to publish their emissions.  n. 法案；法令  這項新法案要求企業公布其排放量。
act  We must act quickly to fix the billing error.  v. 採取行動  我們必須迅速採取行動來修正帳單錯誤。
fair  The company hosts a job fair every spring.  n. 博覽會；展覽會  公司每年春天都會舉辦就業博覽會。
fair  Employees expect to be paid a fair wage.  adj. 公平的；合理的  員工期望獲得合理的薪資。
line  The company will launch a new line of eco-friendly cleaners.  n. 產品系列  公司將推出一系列新的環保清潔用品。
line  Please hold the line while I transfer your call.  n. 電話線路  我轉接電話時請別掛斷。
carry  The store will carry a wide range of office supplies.  v. 販售；供應  這家商店將販售各式各樣的辦公用品。
drop  Sales may drop sharply if prices rise.  v. 下降；下滑  如果價格上漲，銷售量可能會大幅下滑。
draw  The exhibition is expected to draw thousands of visitors.  v. 吸引  這場展覽預計將吸引數千名訪客。
cut  The firm plans to cut costs by ten percent.  v. 削減；降低  公司計畫將成本削減百分之十。
right  Employees have the right to request flexible hours.  n. 權利  員工有權申請彈性工時。
